var __defProp = Object.defineProperty;
var __getOwnPropNames = Object.getOwnPropertyNames;
var __esm = (fn, res, err) => function __init() {
  if (err) throw err[0];
  try {
    return fn && (res = (0, fn[__getOwnPropNames(fn)[0]])(fn = 0)), res;
  } catch (e) {
    throw err = [e], e;
  }
};
var __export = (target, all) => {
  for (var name in all)
    __defProp(target, name, { get: all[name], enumerable: true });
};

// src/lib/crypto.ts
function bytesToBase64(bytes) {
  let binary = "";
  const chunk = 32768;
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunk) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode.apply(
      null,
      Array.from(bytes.subarray(i, i + chunk))
    );
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}
function base64ToBytes(value) {
  const binary = atob(value);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i += 1) {
    bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
  }
  return bytes;
}
async function pbkdf2(password, salt, iterations, bits) {
  const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    new TextEncoder().encode(password),
    "PBKDF2",
    false,
    ["deriveBits"]
  );
  const derived = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(
    {
      name: "PBKDF2",
      salt,
      iterations,
      hash: "SHA-256"
    },
    keyMaterial,
    bits
  );
  return new Uint8Array(derived);
}
async function hashPassword(password) {
  const salt = new Uint8Array(16);
  crypto.getRandomValues(salt);
  const hash = await pbkdf2(password, salt, PBKDF2_ITERATIONS, KEY_LENGTH_BITS);
  return `pbkdf2$${PBKDF2_ITERATIONS}$${bytesToBase64(salt)}$${bytesToBase64(hash)}`;
}
async function verifyPassword(password, stored) {
  if (!stored) return false;
  const parts = stored.split("$");
  if (parts.length !== 4 || parts[0] !== "pbkdf2") return false;
  const iterations = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
  if (!Number.isFinite(iterations) || iterations < 1e4) return false;
  let salt;
  let expected;
  try {
    salt = base64ToBytes(parts[2]);
    expected = base64ToBytes(parts[3]);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
  const actual = await pbkdf2(password, salt, iterations, expected.length * 8);
  if (actual.length !== expected.length) return false;
  let diff = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < actual.length; i += 1) {
    diff |= actual[i] ^ expected[i];
  }
  return diff === 0;
}
function generateCouponCode(prefix = "CAFE") {
  const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789";
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(6);
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  let code = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 1) {
    code += alphabet[bytes[i] % alphabet.length];
  }
  return `${prefix}-${code}`;
}
async function sha256Hex(input) {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(input));
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}
var PBKDF2_ITERATIONS, KEY_LENGTH_BITS;
var init_crypto = __esm({
  "src/lib/crypto.ts"() {
    "use strict";
    PBKDF2_ITERATIONS = 25e3;
    KEY_LENGTH_BITS = 256;
  }
});

// src/lib/fallback-adapters.ts
function networkCode(error) {
  const e = error;
  const code = typeof e?.cause?.code === "string" ? e.cause.code : typeof e?.code === "string" ? e.code : "";
  if (["ENOTFOUND", "EAI_AGAIN"].includes(code)) return "DNS_FAILURE";
  if (["CERT_HAS_EXPIRED", "DEPTH_ZERO_SELF_SIGNED_CERT", "UNABLE_TO_VERIFY_LEAF_SIGNATURE", "ERR_TLS_CERT_ALTNAME_INVALID", "ERR_SSL_PROTOCOL_ERROR"].includes(code)) return "TLS_FAILURE";
  if (["ECONNRESET", "ECONNREFUSED", "UND_ERR_SOCKET"].includes(code)) return "NETWORK_RESET";
  if (["ETIMEDOUT", "UND_ERR_CONNECT_TIMEOUT", "UND_ERR_HEADERS_TIMEOUT"].includes(code) || e?.name === "TimeoutError") return "NETWORK_TIMEOUT";
  return "FETCH_FAILED";
}
function identifier(value, signed = false) {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(value) && (signed || value >= 0) || typeof value === "string" && (signed ? /^-?\d{1,64}$/ : /^\d{1,64}$/).test(value);
}
function validChatId(value) {
  return /^-?[1-9]\d{0,19}$/.test(value) || /^@[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]{4,31}$/.test(value);
}
function validBotToken(value) {
  return /^\d{1,20}:[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,200}$/.test(value);
}

      constructor(channel, origin) {
        this.channel = channel;
        this.origin = origin;
      }
      channel;

      async request(token, method, body, timeoutMs) {
        const start = Date.now();

        let controller;
        let timer;

          d.code = code;
          d.elapsedMs = Math.max(0, Date.now() - start);
          console.warn("MESSENGER_REQUEST_FAILED", { ...d });
          const unknown = method === "sendMessage" && !["INVALID_TOKEN", "INVALID_CHAT", "FORBIDDEN", "RATE_LIMITED", "RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE", "REQUEST_SETUP_FAILED"].includes(code);

            ok: false,
            error: code + ": " + messages[code] + (d.httpStatus ? " \u0646\u062A\u06CC\u062C\u0647\u0654 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0634\u062E\u0635 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u062E\u0648\u062F\u06A9\u0627\u0631 \u062A\u06A9\u0631\u0627\u0631 \u0646\u0634\u062F." : ""),
            diagnostic: { ...d },
            ...retryAfter === void 0 ? {} : { retryAfter }
          };
        };
        try {
          if (typeof fetch !== "function" || typeof AbortController !== "function") return fail2("RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE");
          controller = new AbortController();
          timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
          d.phase = "request";
          const response = await fetch(`${this.origin}/bot${token}/${method}`, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Accept: "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify(body),
            redirect: "manual",
            signal: controller.signal
          });
          d.httpStatus = response.status;
          d.phase = "response";
          if (response.status >= 300 && response.status < 400) return fail2("REDIRECT_BLOCKED");
          d.phase = "body";
          const text = await response.text();
          d.phase = "response";
          let data = null;
          if (text.length <= 65536) {
            try {
              const value = JSON.parse(text);
              if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) data = value;
            } catch {
            }
          }
          const code = data?.ok === false && Number.isInteger(data.error_code) ? data.error_code : response.status;
          if (code === 429) {
            const delay = Number(data?.parameters?.retry_after ?? response.headers.get("Retry-After"));
            return fail2("RATE_LIMITED", Number.isFinite(delay) && delay > 0 ? Math.min(86400, Math.ceil(delay)) : 60);
          }
          if (!data || typeof data.ok !== "boolean") return fail2("INVALID_RESPONSE");
          if (response.ok && data.ok) {
            if (!data.result || typeof data.result !== "object" || Array.isArray(data.result)) return fail2("INVALID_RESPONSE");
            const valid = method === "sendMessage" ? identifier(data.result.message_id) : identifier(data.result.id, method === "getChat");
            if (!valid) return fail2("INVALID_RESPONSE");
            d.code = "OK";
            d.elapsedMs = Math.max(0, Date.now() - start);
            return { ok: true, data, diagnostic: { ...d } };
          }
          return fail2(code === 401 || code === 404 ? "INVALID_TOKEN" : code === 403 ? "FORBIDDEN" : code === 400 && method !== "getMe" ? "INVALID_CHAT" : "PROVIDER_ERROR");
        } catch (error) {
          return fail2(controller?.signal.aborted ? "NETWORK_TIMEOUT" : d.phase === "setup" ? "REQUEST_SETUP_FAILED" : d.phase === "body" ? "BODY_READ_FAILED" : networkCode(error));
        } finally {
          if (timer !== void 0) clearTimeout(timer);
        }
      }
      async send(token, chatId, text) {
        const result = await this.request(token, "sendMessage", { chat_id: chatId, text }, 12e3);
        if (!result.ok) return result;
        return { ok: true, messageId: String(result.data.result.message_id) };
      }
      /** Read-only bot identity and chat lookup. Never getUpdates, setWebhook or sendMessage. */
      async diagnose(token, chatId) {
        const checks = [];
        const bot = await this.request(token, "getMe", {}, 6e3);
        checks.push({
          stage: "bot",
          ok: bot.ok,
          code: bot.ok ? "BOT_REACHABLE" : bot.diagnostic.code,
          message: bot.ok ? "\u0627\u0631\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0637 \u0633\u0631\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0627 API \u0648 \u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u062A\u0648\u06A9\u0646 \u062A\u0623\u06CC\u06CC\u062F \u0634\u062F." : messages[bot.diagnostic.code],
          diagnostic: bot.diagnostic
        });
        if (bot.ok) {
          const chat = await this.request(token, "getChat", { chat_id: chatId }, 6e3);
          checks.push({
            stage: "chat",
            ok: chat.ok,
            code: chat.ok ? "CHAT_REACHABLE" : chat.diagnostic.code,
            message: chat.ok ? "API \u0627\u0637\u0644\u0627\u0639\u0627\u062A \u0645\u0642\u0635\u062F \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0631\u06AF\u0631\u062F\u0627\u0646\u062F\u061B \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0628\u0647\u200C\u062A\u0646\u0647\u0627\u06CC\u06CC \u062A\u0636\u0645\u06CC\u0646 \u0645\u062C\u0648\u0632 \u06CC\u0627 \u062A\u062D\u0648\u06CC\u0644 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A." : messages[chat.diagnostic.code],
            diagnostic: chat.diagnostic
          });
        }
        return { channel: this.channel, ready: checks.length === 2 && checks.every((c) => c.ok), checks, messageSent: false };
      }
    };
    adapters = {
      telegram: new BotApiAdapter("telegram", "https://api.telegram.org"),
      bale: new BotApiAdapter("bale", "https://tapi.bale.ai")
    };
  }
});

// src/lib/utils.ts
function ok(c, data, status = 200) {
  return c.json({ ok: true, data }, status);
}
function fail(c, message, status = 400, code) {
  return c.json({ ok: false, error: message, code }, status);
}
function safeJsonParse(raw2, fallback) {
  if (raw2 === null || raw2 === void 0 || raw2 === "") return fallback;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw2);
    return parsed ?? fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}
function zonedParts(date, timeZone) {
  const fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
    timeZone,
    year: "numeric",
    month: "2-digit",
    day: "2-digit",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    second: "2-digit",
    hour12: false,
    weekday: "short"
  });
  const parts = fmt.formatToParts(date);
  const get = (type) => parts.find((p) => p.type === type)?.value ?? "";
  let hour = parseInt(get("hour"), 10);
  if (!Number.isFinite(hour)) hour = 0;
  if (hour === 24) hour = 0;
  const year = parseInt(get("year"), 10);
  const month = parseInt(get("month"), 10);
  const day = parseInt(get("day"), 10);
  const iso = `${String(year).padStart(4, "0")}-${String(month).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(
    day
  ).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  const time = `${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${get("minute")}`;
  return {
    iso,
    time,
    year,
    month,
    day,
    hour,
    minute: parseInt(get("minute"), 10) || 0,
    weekday: get("weekday"),
    stamp: `${iso} ${time}:${get("second")}`
  };
}
function nowInZone(timeZone) {
  return zonedParts(/* @__PURE__ */ new Date(), timeZone);
}
function todayISO(timeZone = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE) {
  return nowInZone(timeZone).iso;
}
function sqliteToMs(value) {
  if (!value) return null;
  const normalized = value.includes("T") ? value.endsWith("Z") || /[+-]\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(value) ? value : `${value}Z` : `${value.replace(" ", "T")}Z`;
  const ms = Date.parse(normalized);
  return Number.isFinite(ms) ? ms : null;
}
function daysSince(value) {
  const ms = sqliteToMs(value);
  if (ms === null) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - ms) / 864e5));
}
function jalCal(jy) {
  const bl = JALALI_BREAKS.length;
  const gy = jy + 621;
  let leapJ = -14;
  let jp = JALALI_BREAKS[0];
  let jm = 0;
  let jump = 0;
  if (jy < jp || jy >= JALALI_BREAKS[bl - 1]) {
    return { leap: 0, gy, march: 20 };
  }
  for (let i = 1; i < bl; i += 1) {
    jm = JALALI_BREAKS[i];
    jump = jm - jp;
    if (jy < jm) break;
    leapJ = leapJ + div(jump, 33) * 8 + div(mod(jump, 33), 4);
    jp = jm;
  }
  let n = jy - jp;
  leapJ = leapJ + div(n, 33) * 8 + div(mod(n, 33) + 3, 4);
  if (mod(jump, 33) === 4 && jump - n === 4) leapJ += 1;
  const leapG = div(gy, 4) - div((div(gy, 100) + 1) * 3, 4) - 150;
  const march = 20 + leapJ - leapG;
  if (jump - n < 6) n = n - jump + div(jump + 4, 33) * 33;
  let leap = mod(mod(n + 1, 33) - 1, 4);
  if (leap === -1) leap = 4;
  return { leap, gy, march };
}
function g2d(gy, gm, gd) {
  let d = div((gy + div(gm - 8, 6) + 100100) * 1461, 4) + div(153 * mod(gm + 9, 12) + 2, 5) + gd - 34840408;
  d = d - div(div(gy + 100100 + div(gm - 8, 6), 100) * 3, 4) + 752;
  return d;
}
function d2g(jdn) {
  let j = 4 * jdn + 139361631;
  j = j + div(div(4 * jdn + 183187720, 146097) * 3, 4) * 4 - 3908;
  const i = div(mod(j, 1461), 4) * 5 + 308;
  const gd = div(mod(i, 153), 5) + 1;
  const gm = mod(div(i, 153), 12) + 1;
  const gy = div(j, 1461) - 100100 + div(8 - gm, 6);
  return { gy, gm, gd };
}
function toJalali(gy, gm, gd) {
  const jdn = g2d(gy, gm, gd);
  const gregorianYear = d2g(jdn).gy;
  let jy = gregorianYear - 621;
  const r = jalCal(jy);
  const jdn1f = g2d(gregorianYear, 3, r.march);
  let k = jdn - jdn1f;
  if (k >= 0) {
    if (k <= 185) {
      return { jy, jm: 1 + div(k, 31), jd: mod(k, 31) + 1 };
    }
    k -= 186;
  } else {
    jy -= 1;
    k += 179;
    if (r.leap === 1) k += 1;
  }
  return { jy, jm: 7 + div(k, 30), jd: mod(k, 30) + 1 };
}
function resolveTimeZone(name) {
  const raw2 = String(name ?? "").trim();
  if (!raw2) return DEFAULT_TIMEZONE;
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: raw2 });
    return raw2;
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_TIMEZONE;
  }
}
function toGregorian(jy, jm, jd) {
  const r = jalCal(jy);
  const jdn = g2d(r.gy, 3, r.march) + (jm - 1) * 31 - div(jm, 7) * (jm - 7) + jd - 1;
  return d2g(jdn);
}
function todayJalali(timeZone = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE) {
  const p = nowInZone(timeZone);
  return toJalali(p.year, p.month, p.day);
}
function utcStamp(ms) {
  return new Date(ms).toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace("T", " ");
}
function zonedDayRange(timeZone = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE, offsetDays = 0) {
  const now = /* @__PURE__ */ new Date();
  const p = zonedParts(now, timeZone);
  const asUTC = Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day, p.hour, p.minute, 0);
  const offset = asUTC - Math.floor(now.getTime() / 6e4) * 6e4;
  const startMs = Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day) - offset - offsetDays * 864e5;
  const dateISO = new Date(
    Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day) - offsetDays * 864e5
  ).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  return {
    start: utcStamp(startMs),
    end: utcStamp(startMs + 864e5),
    startMs,
    endMs: startMs + 864e5,
    dateISO
  };
}
function zonedDaySeries(timeZone = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE, count = 7) {
  const out = [];
  for (let i = count - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
    const r = zonedDayRange(timeZone, i);
    out.push({ start: r.start, end: r.end, date: r.dateISO });
  }
  return out;
}
function zonedDateStartMs(dateISO, timeZone = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE) {
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(String(dateISO).trim());
  if (!m) return null;
  const year = Number(m[1]);
  const month = Number(m[2]);
  const day = Number(m[3]);
  if (month < 1 || month > 12 || day < 1 || day > 31) return null;
  const guess = Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day, 0, 0, 0);
  let ms = guess;
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i += 1) {
    const p = zonedParts(new Date(ms), timeZone);
    const asUTC = Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day, p.hour, p.minute, 0);
    const offset = asUTC - Math.floor(ms / 6e4) * 6e4;
    ms = guess - offset;
  }
  return ms;
}
function jalaliMonthRange(timeZone = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE) {
  return jalaliMonthRangeAt(0, timeZone);
}
function jalaliMonthRangeAt(monthOffset = 0, timeZone = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE) {
  const { jy, jm } = todayJalali(timeZone);
  const shift = Math.max(0, Math.floor(monthOffset));
  let index = jy * 12 + (jm - 1) - shift;
  const targetYear = Math.floor(index / 12);
  const targetMonth = index % 12 + 1;
  index = targetYear * 12 + targetMonth;
  const nextYear = Math.floor(index / 12);
  const nextMonth = index % 12 + 1;
  const startG = toGregorian(targetYear, targetMonth, 1);
  const endG = toGregorian(nextYear, nextMonth, 1);
  const startISO = `${startG.gy}-${String(startG.gm).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(startG.gd).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  const endISO = `${endG.gy}-${String(endG.gm).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(endG.gd).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  const startMs = zonedDateStartMs(startISO, timeZone) ?? Date.UTC(startG.gy, startG.gm - 1, startG.gd);
  const endMs = zonedDateStartMs(endISO, timeZone) ?? Date.UTC(endG.gy, endG.gm - 1, endG.gd);
  return {
    start: utcStamp(startMs),
    end: utcStamp(endMs),
    startMs,
    endMs,
    label: `${JALALI_MONTHS[targetMonth - 1]} ${toPersianDigits(targetYear)}`,
    jy: targetYear,
    jm: targetMonth
  };
}
function toPersianDigits(input) {
  return String(input).replace(/[0-9]/g, (d) => "\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9"[parseInt(d, 10)]);
}
function toLatinDigits(input) {
  return input.replace(/[۰-۹]/g, (d) => String("\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9".indexOf(d))).replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (d) => String("\u0660\u0661\u0662\u0663\u0664\u0665\u0666\u0667\u0668\u0669".indexOf(d)));
}
function formatJalali(value, withYear = true) {
  if (!value) return "\u2014";
  const datePart = value.slice(0, 10);
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(datePart);
  if (!m) return "\u2014";
  const gy = parseInt(m[1], 10);
  const gm = parseInt(m[2], 10);
  const gd = parseInt(m[3], 10);
  const { jy, jm, jd } = toJalali(gy, gm, gd);
  const monthName = JALALI_MONTHS[jm - 1] ?? "";
  const base = `${toPersianDigits(jd)} ${monthName}`;
  return withYear ? `${base} ${toPersianDigits(jy)}` : base;
}
function formatJalaliDateTime(value, timeZone = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE) {
  const ms = sqliteToMs(value);
  if (ms === null) return "\u2014";
  const p = zonedParts(new Date(ms), timeZone);
  const j = formatJalali(p.iso);
  return `${j} \u2014 ${toPersianDigits(p.time)}`;
}
function jalaliWeekday(value) {
  if (!value) return "\u2014";
  const ms = Date.parse(`${value.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00Z`);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return "\u2014";
  return WEEKDAYS_FA[new Date(ms).getUTCDay()] ?? "\u2014";
}
function formatMoney(amount) {
  const n = Math.round(Number(amount) || 0);
  return n.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ",");
}
function formatNumberFa(amount) {
  return toPersianDigits(formatMoney(amount));
}
function normalizePhone(raw2) {
  if (!raw2) return "";
  let value = toLatinDigits(String(raw2)).trim();
  value = value.replace(/[\s\-()]/g, "");
  if (value.startsWith("+98")) value = `0${value.slice(3)}`;
  else if (value.startsWith("0098")) value = `0${value.slice(4)}`;
  else if (value.startsWith("98") && value.length === 12) value = `0${value.slice(2)}`;
  else if (value.startsWith("9") && value.length === 10) value = `0${value}`;
  return value;
}
function isValidBirthDate(value) {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) return false;
  const ms = Date.parse(`${value}T00:00:00Z`);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return false;
  const year = parseInt(value.slice(0, 4), 10);
  return year >= 1300 && year <= (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).getUTCFullYear();
}
function toPositiveInt(value, fallback = 0) {
  const n = Math.floor(Number(value));
  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) return fallback;
  return n;
}
function toIntInRange(value, min, max, fallback) {
  const n = Math.floor(Number(value));
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return fallback;
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, n));
}
function escapeHtml(value) {
  return String(value ?? "").replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
}
function renderTemplate(template, vars) {
  return String(template ?? "").replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (match2, key) => {
    const value = vars[key];
    return value === void 0 || value === null ? match2 : String(value);
  });
}

function invalidateSettingsCache() {
  settingsCache = null;
}
function coerceSettings(raw2) {
  const templates = raw2.sms_templates ?? {};
  const tiers = raw2.tiers ?? {};
  const templatesMerged = {
    welcome: str(templates.welcome, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.sms_templates.welcome),
    invoice: str(templates.invoice, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.sms_templates.invoice),
    birthday: str(templates.birthday, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.sms_templates.birthday),
    churn: str(templates.churn, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.sms_templates.churn),
    weather: str(templates.weather, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.sms_templates.weather),
    otp: str(templates.otp, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.sms_templates.otp),
    redeem: str(templates.redeem, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.sms_templates.redeem)
  };
  const provider = coerceSmsProvider(raw2.sms_provider);
  return {
    sms_templates: templatesMerged,
    points_mode: raw2.points_mode === "visit" || raw2.points_mode === "invoice" ? raw2.points_mode : "amount",
    points_per_currency: toIntInRange(raw2.points_per_currency, 1, 1e3, 1),
    invoice_points: toIntInRange(raw2.invoice_points, 0, 1e5, 15),
    visit_points: toIntInRange(raw2.visit_points, 1, 1e5, 15),
    visit_min_amount: toIntInRange(raw2.visit_min_amount, 0, 1e8, 3e4),
    redeem_enabled: toIntInRange(raw2.redeem_enabled, 0, 1, 1),
    redeem_points: toIntInRange(raw2.redeem_points, 1, 1e7, 200),
    redeem_value: toIntInRange(raw2.redeem_value, 1e3, 1e8, 2e4),
    redeem_expiry_days: toIntInRange(raw2.redeem_expiry_days, 1, 365, 30),
    otp_login_enabled: toIntInRange(raw2.otp_login_enabled, 0, 1, 1),
    require_shift: toIntInRange(raw2.require_shift, 0, 1, 0),
    currency_unit: toIntInRange(raw2.currency_unit, 1, 1e7, 1e3),
    tiers: {
      silver: toIntInRange(tiers.silver, 1, 1e8, 500),
      gold: toIntInRange(tiers.gold, 2, 1e8, 2e3),
      vip: toIntInRange(tiers.vip, 3, 1e8, 5e3)
    },
    cafe_name: str(raw2.cafe_name, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.cafe_name).slice(0, 60),
    cafe_city: str(raw2.cafe_city, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.cafe_city).slice(0, 60),
    weather_api_key: str(raw2.weather_api_key, "").slice(0, 200),
    sms_enabled: toIntInRange(raw2.sms_enabled, 0, 1, 1),
    sms_provider: provider,
    sms_api_key: str(raw2.sms_api_key, "").slice(0, 300),
    sms_sender: str(raw2.sms_sender, "").slice(0, 30),
    referral_reward_points: toIntInRange(raw2.referral_reward_points, 0, 1e6, 50),
    churn_grace_days: toIntInRange(raw2.churn_grace_days, 0, 60, 2),
    churn_min_visits: toIntInRange(raw2.churn_min_visits, 1, 100, 3),
    birthday_coupon_percent: toIntInRange(raw2.birthday_coupon_percent, 1, 100, 15)
  };
}
function str(value, fallback) {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : fallback;
}
function coerceSmsProvider(value) {
  return value === "smsir" || value === "kavenegar" ? value : "console";
}
async function loadSettings(env, force = false) {
  if (!force && settingsCache && Date.now() - settingsCache.at < CACHE_TTL_MS) {
    return settingsCache.value;
  }
  try {
    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT key, value FROM settings").all();
    const raw2 = {};
    for (const row of results ?? []) {
      raw2[row.key] = safeJsonParse(row.value, row.value);
    }
    const merged = coerceSettings(raw2);
    settingsCache = { value: merged, at: Date.now() };
    return merged;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("loadSettings failed:", error);
    return DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
  }
}
async function saveSetting(env, key, value) {
  await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)
     ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value`
  ).bind(key, JSON.stringify(value ?? null)).run();
  invalidateSettingsCache();
}
async function saveSettings(env, values) {
  const entries = Object.entries(values);
  if (!entries.length) return;
  if (entries.length > 50) throw new Error("TOO_MANY_SETTINGS");
  await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO settings(key,value) VALUES ${entries.map(() => "(?,?)").join(",")}
    ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value`).bind(...entries.flatMap(([key, value]) => [key, JSON.stringify(value ?? null)])).run();
  invalidateSettingsCache();
}
async function deleteSetting(env, key) {
  await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM settings WHERE key = ?").bind(key).run();
  invalidateSettingsCache();
}
async function getRawSetting(env, key) {
  const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?").bind(key).first();
  return row?.value ?? null;
}
async function loadMenuItems(env) {
  const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?").bind("menu_items").first();
  const items = safeJsonParse(row?.value ?? null, DEFAULT_MENU_ITEMS);
  if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) return DEFAULT_MENU_ITEMS;
  return items.filter((item) => item && typeof item.name === "string").map((item) => ({
    name: String(item.name).slice(0, 60),
    price: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item.price) || 0)),
    category: typeof item.category === "string" ? item.category.slice(0, 30) : void 0
  })).slice(0, 1e3);
}

        churn: "{name} \u0639\u0632\u06CC\u0632\u060C \u062F\u0644\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC\u062A \u062A\u0646\u06AF \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u2615\n{last_visit} \u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0627\u0632 \u0622\u062E\u0631\u06CC\u0646 \u062F\u06CC\u062F\u0627\u0631\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u06AF\u0630\u0634\u062A\u0647\u061B \u0628\u0627 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u06F1\u06F0\u066A \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0631\u0648\u06CC \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0639\u062F\u06CC \u0645\u0647\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0645\u0627 \u0628\u0627\u0634.",
        weather: "\u0633\u0644\u0627\u0645 {name}\u060C \u0647\u0648\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 {city}: {weather} \u0648 {temp} \u062F\u0631\u062C\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A. {advice} \u2615",
        otp: "\u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0628\u0647 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 {cafe}: {code}\n\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u062F \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u06F2 \u062F\u0642\u06CC\u0642\u0647 \u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u062F\u0627\u0631\u062F\u061B \u0622\u0646 \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0627 \u06A9\u0633\u06CC \u0628\u0647 \u0627\u0634\u062A\u0631\u0627\u06A9 \u0646\u06AF\u0630\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062F.",
        redeem: "{name} \u0639\u0632\u06CC\u0632\u060C {points} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0628\u0647 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 {coupon} \u0628\u0647 \u0627\u0631\u0632\u0634 {value} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u062A\u0628\u062F\u06CC\u0644 \u0634\u062F.\n\u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u062A\u0627 {expiry}. \u2615"
      },
      points_mode: "amount",
      points_per_currency: 1,
      currency_unit: 1e3,
      visit_points: 15,
      invoice_points: 15,
      visit_min_amount: 3e4,

      otp_login_enabled: 1,
      require_shift: 0,
      tiers: { silver: 500, gold: 2e3, vip: 5e3 },
      cafe_name: "\u06A9\u0627\u0641\u0647 \u0645\u0644\u062A",
      cafe_city: "\u062A\u0647\u0631\u0627\u0646",
      weather_api_key: "",
      sms_enabled: 1,
      sms_provider: "console",
      sms_api_key: "",
      sms_sender: "",
      referral_reward_points: 50,
      churn_grace_days: 2,
      churn_min_visits: 3,
      birthday_coupon_percent: 15
    };
    EDITABLE_SETTING_KEYS = [
      "sms_templates",
      "points_mode",

      "invoice_points",
      "visit_min_amount",

    settingsCache = null;
    CACHE_TTL_MS = 3e4;
  }
});

// src/lib/fallback.ts
async function encryptionKey(env) {
  let bytes;
  try {
    bytes = base64ToBytes(env.FALLBACK_ENCRYPTION_KEY ?? "");
  } catch {
    throw new Error("FALLBACK_KEY_MISSING: \u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F \u0631\u0645\u0632\u0646\u06AF\u0627\u0631\u06CC Fallback \u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.");
  }
  if (bytes.length !== 32) throw new Error("FALLBACK_KEY_MISSING: \u0627\u0628\u062A\u062F\u0627 Secret \u0628\u0627 \u0646\u0627\u0645 FALLBACK_ENCRYPTION_KEY (\u06F3\u06F2 \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062A Base64) \u0631\u0627 \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.");
  return crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", bytes, "AES-GCM", false, ["encrypt", "decrypt"]);
}
async function seal(env, value, context) {
  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
  const ciphertext = await crypto.subtle.encrypt({
    name: "AES-GCM",
    iv,
    additionalData: new TextEncoder().encode(context)
  }, await encryptionKey(env), new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(value)));
  return `v1.${bytesToBase64(iv)}.${bytesToBase64(new Uint8Array(ciphertext))}`;
}
async function unseal(env, value, context) {
  const [version, iv, ciphertext] = value.split(".");
  if (version !== "v1") throw new Error("INVALID_CIPHERTEXT");
  const plain = await crypto.subtle.decrypt({
    name: "AES-GCM",
    iv: base64ToBytes(iv),
    additionalData: new TextEncoder().encode(context)
  }, await encryptionKey(env), base64ToBytes(ciphertext));
  return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(plain));
}
async function loadFallbackConfig(env) {
  const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?").bind(CONFIG_KEY).first();
  if (!row) return { enabled: false, channels: [] };
  return { ...JSON.parse(row.value), configured: true };
}
async function channelToken(env, config) {
  const fromEnv = config.channel === "telegram" ? env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN : env.BALE_BOT_TOKEN;
  if (fromEnv) return fromEnv;
  return config.token ? unseal(env, config.token, `token:${config.channel}`) : "";
}
function safeFallbackConfig(env, config) {
  let encryptionKeyConfigured = false;
  try {
    encryptionKeyConfigured = base64ToBytes(env.FALLBACK_ENCRYPTION_KEY ?? "").length === 32;
  } catch {
  }
  return {
    enabled: config.enabled,
    notifyEnabled: config.notifyEnabled === true,
    notifyInvoices: config.notifyInvoices !== false,
    notifyActions: config.notifyActions !== false,
    encryptionKeyConfigured,
    channels: config.channels.map((c) => ({
      channel: c.channel,
      chatId: c.chatId,
      hasToken: !!c.token || !!(c.channel === "telegram" ? env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN : env.BALE_BOT_TOKEN),
      tokenSource: (c.channel === "telegram" ? env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN : env.BALE_BOT_TOKEN) ? "env" : c.token ? "database" : "none"
    }))
  };
}
async function saveFallbackConfig(env, body) {
  if (!body || typeof body !== "object") throw new Error("\u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u062A \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.");
  const input = body;
  if (typeof input.enabled !== "boolean" || !Array.isArray(input.channels) || input.channels.length > 2) throw new Error("\u0633\u0648\u0626\u06CC\u0686 \u0648 \u0641\u0647\u0631\u0633\u062A \u0645\u0642\u0635\u062F\u0647\u0627 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.");
  const previous = await loadFallbackConfig(env);
  for (const key of ["notifyEnabled", "notifyInvoices", "notifyActions"]) {
    if (input[key] !== void 0 && typeof input[key] !== "boolean") throw new Error("\u0633\u0648\u0626\u06CC\u0686 \u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.");
  }
  const notifyEnabled = input.notifyEnabled === void 0 ? previous.notifyEnabled === true : input.notifyEnabled === true;
  const notifyInvoices = input.notifyInvoices === void 0 ? previous.notifyInvoices !== false : input.notifyInvoices === true;
  const notifyActions = input.notifyActions === void 0 ? previous.notifyActions !== false : input.notifyActions === true;
  if (notifyEnabled && !notifyInvoices && !notifyActions) throw new Error("\u062D\u062F\u0627\u0642\u0644 \u06CC\u06A9 \u0646\u0648\u0639 \u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A \u0631\u0627 \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.");
  const channels = [];
  for (const item of input.channels) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") throw new Error("\u0645\u0642\u0635\u062F \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.");
    const c = item;
    if (c.channel !== "telegram" && c.channel !== "bale") throw new Error("\u0641\u0642\u0637 \u062A\u0644\u06AF\u0631\u0627\u0645 \u0648 \u0628\u0644\u0647 \u067E\u0634\u062A\u06CC\u0628\u0627\u0646\u06CC \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u0646\u062F.");
    if (channels.some((x) => x.channel === c.channel)) throw new Error("\u06A9\u0627\u0646\u0627\u0644 \u062A\u06A9\u0631\u0627\u0631\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A.");
    if (typeof c.chatId !== "string" || !validChatId(c.chatId.trim())) throw new Error("Chat ID \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0639\u062F\u062F \u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u06CC\u0627 \u0646\u0627\u0645 \u06A9\u0627\u0646\u0627\u0644 \u0628\u0627 @ \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.");
    if (c.token !== void 0 && typeof c.token !== "string") throw new Error("\u062A\u0648\u06A9\u0646 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.");
    const token = String(c.token ?? "").trim();
    if (token && (c.channel === "telegram" ? env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN : env.BALE_BOT_TOKEN)) throw new Error("\u062A\u0648\u06A9\u0646 \u0627\u0632 Secret \u0648\u0631\u06A9\u0631 \u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F\u061B \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u0622\u0646 \u0631\u0627 \u0627\u0632 \u0647\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0628\u062E\u0634 \u0627\u0646\u062C\u0627\u0645 \u062F\u0647\u06CC\u062F.");
    if (token && !validBotToken(token)) throw new Error("\u0642\u0627\u0644\u0628 \u062A\u0648\u06A9\u0646 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.");
    const old = previous.channels.find((x) => x.channel === c.channel);
    const encrypted = c.clearToken === true ? "" : token ? await seal(env, token, `token:${c.channel}`) : old?.token ?? "";
    const candidate = { channel: c.channel, chatId: c.chatId.trim(), token: encrypted };
    if ((input.enabled || notifyEnabled) && !validBotToken(await channelToken(env, candidate))) throw new Error("\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0647\u0631 \u0645\u0642\u0635\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u06CC\u06A9 \u062A\u0648\u06A9\u0646 \u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0644\u0627\u0632\u0645 \u0627\u0633\u062A.");
    channels.push(candidate);
  }
  if (input.enabled || notifyEnabled) {
    await encryptionKey(env);
    if (!channels.length) throw new Error("\u062D\u062F\u0627\u0642\u0644 \u06CC\u06A9 \u0645\u0642\u0635\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.");
  }
  await env.DB.batch([
    env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO settings(key,value) VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value`).bind(CONFIG_KEY, JSON.stringify({ enabled: input.enabled, channels, notifyEnabled, notifyInvoices, notifyActions })),
    // Pending work for removed destinations must not be sent or kept indefinitely.
    env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE fallback_jobs SET status='skipped',error='CONFIG_CHANGED: \u0645\u0642\u0635\u062F \u062D\u0630\u0641 \u06CC\u0627 \u0645\u0633\u06CC\u0631 \u062E\u0627\u0645\u0648\u0634 \u0634\u062F.',
      payload=CASE WHEN type='otp' THEN '' ELSE payload END,completed_at=datetime('now')
      WHERE status='pending' AND (
        NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM json_each(?) WHERE json_extract(value,'$.channel')=fallback_jobs.channel AND json_extract(value,'$.chatId')=fallback_jobs.chat_id)
        OR (reason NOT IN ('test','event') AND ?=0)
        OR (reason='event' AND (?=0 OR (type='report_invoice' AND ?=0) OR (type='report_action' AND ?=0))))`).bind(JSON.stringify(channels), input.enabled ? 1 : 0, notifyEnabled ? 1 : 0, notifyInvoices ? 1 : 0, notifyActions ? 1 : 0)
  ]);
}
async function diagnoseFallback(env, channel) {
  const config = await loadFallbackConfig(env);
  const destination = config.channels.find((c) => c.channel === channel);
  if (!destination) throw new Error("CHANNEL_NOT_CONFIGURED");
  const token = await channelToken(env, destination);
  if (!validBotToken(token) || !validChatId(destination.chatId)) throw new Error("INVALID_CONFIG");
  return adapters[channel].diagnose(token, destination.chatId);
}
function smsPanelActive(env, settings) {
  if (settings.sms_enabled === 0 || settings.sms_provider === "console") return false;
  const key = settings.sms_provider === "smsir" ? env.SMSIR_API_KEY : env.KAVENEGAR_API_KEY;
  return !!(key || settings.sms_api_key).trim() && (settings.sms_provider !== "smsir" || !!(env.SMS_SENDER || settings.sms_sender).trim());
}
async function enqueueFallback(env, params, reason, testChannel) {
  const config = await loadFallbackConfig(env);
  if (reason === "event" ? !config.notifyEnabled || (params.type === "report_invoice" ? config.notifyInvoices === false : config.notifyActions === false) : !config.enabled && reason !== "test") return [];
  const phone = normalizePhone(params.phone);
  if (reason !== "event" && !/^09\d{9}$/.test(phone) || phone && !/^09\d{9}$/.test(phone) || !params.message.trim()) throw new Error("INVALID_FALLBACK_MESSAGE");
  if (params.message.length > 2e3) throw new Error("MESSAGE_TOO_LONG");
  const selected = config.channels.filter((c) => !testChannel || c.channel === testChannel);
  if (!selected.length) throw new Error("\u0647\u06CC\u0686 \u0645\u0642\u0635\u062F \u0630\u062E\u06CC\u0631\u0647\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647\u200C\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.");
  const now = Date.now();
  const ids = [];
  const bindings = [];
  const rows = [];
  for (const c of selected) {
    if (!validChatId(c.chatId) || !validBotToken(await channelToken(env, c))) throw new Error("\u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u062A \u0645\u0642\u0635\u062F \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.");
    const id = params.eventKey ? "event-" + await sha256Hex(params.eventKey + ":" + c.channel + ":" + c.chatId) : crypto.randomUUID();
    const payload = await seal(env, { phone, message: params.message, requestedAt: new Date(now).toISOString(), userId: params.userId, title: params.title }, `job:${id}`);
    rows.push("SELECT ? AS id,? AS channel,? AS chat_id,? AS user_id,? AS type,? AS payload,? AS reason,? AS next_at,? AS expires_at");
    bindings.push(id, c.channel, c.chatId, params.userId, params.type, payload, reason, now, Math.min(now + (params.type === "otp" ? 11e4 : 864e5), params.expiresAt ?? Infinity));
    ids.push(id);
  }
  const insert = env.DB.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fallback_jobs(id,channel,chat_id,user_id,type,payload,reason,next_at,expires_at)
    SELECT * FROM (${rows.join(" UNION ALL ")}) WHERE (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM fallback_jobs WHERE status IN ('pending','processing')) <= ?`).bind(...bindings, 1e3 - ids.length);
  const inserted = params.type === "otp" && params.userId !== null ? (await env.DB.batch([
    env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE fallback_jobs SET status='skipped',error='SUPERSEDED: \u06A9\u062F \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F \u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A \u0634\u062F.',payload='',completed_at=datetime('now')
        WHERE type='otp' AND user_id=? AND status='pending'`).bind(params.userId),
    insert
  ]))[1] : await insert.run();
  if (inserted.meta.changes !== ids.length) {
    if (!params.eventKey) throw new Error("QUEUE_FULL");
    const count = await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM fallback_jobs WHERE id IN (${ids.map(() => "?").join(",")})`).bind(...ids).first();
    if (count?.n !== ids.length) throw new Error("QUEUE_FULL");
  }
  return ids;
}
function formatFallbackMessage(p, type) {
  if (type.startsWith("report_")) return [p.title || "\u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A", `\u0632\u0645\u0627\u0646: ${formatJalaliDateTime(p.requestedAt)}`, p.phone ? `\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC:
${p.phone}` : "", p.message].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n");
  return [
    "\u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06CC \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u2014 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645 \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0628\u0647 \u0627\u062F\u0645\u06CC\u0646 \u0631\u0633\u06CC\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A",
    `\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06A9:
${p.phone}`,
    `\u0632\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A: ${formatJalaliDateTime(p.requestedAt)}`,
    `\u0646\u0648\u0639: ${{ welcome: "\u062E\u0648\u0634\u200C\u0622\u0645\u062F", invoice: "\u0631\u0633\u06CC\u062F \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F", birthday: "\u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F", churn: "\u06CC\u0627\u062F\u0622\u0648\u0631\u06CC \u062D\u0636\u0648\u0631", weather: "\u0622\u0628\u200C\u0648\u0647\u0648\u0627", referral: "\u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641\u06CC", otp: "\u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F", redeem: "\u062A\u0628\u062F\u06CC\u0644 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632", admin: "\u062A\u0633\u062A \u0627\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0644" }[type] || type}`,
    p.userId !== null ? `\u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u0647 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0628\u0631: ${p.userId}` : "",
    type === "otp" ? "\u26A0\uFE0F \u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0645\u062D\u0631\u0645\u0627\u0646\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u062D\u062F\u0627\u06A9\u062B\u0631 \u062A\u0627 \u06F2 \u062F\u0642\u06CC\u0642\u0647 \u067E\u0633 \u0627\u0632 \u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A \u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A. \u062F\u0631 \u06A9\u0627\u0646\u0627\u0644 \u0639\u0645\u0648\u0645\u06CC \u0646\u0641\u0631\u0633\u062A\u06CC\u062F." : "",
    `\u0645\u062A\u0646 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645:
${p.message}`
  ].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n");
}
async function processFallbackQueue(env, maintenance = false) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const owner = crypto.randomUUID();
  const config = await loadFallbackConfig(env);
  if (!config.configured) return false;
  if (!maintenance) {
    const due = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM fallback_jobs WHERE status='pending' AND next_at<=? LIMIT 1").bind(now).first();
    if (!due) return false;
  }
  const lease = await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE fallback_dispatch SET until_ms=?,owner=? WHERE id=1 AND until_ms<=? RETURNING id").bind(now + 45e3, owner, now).first();
  if (!lease) return false;
  try {
    await env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM fallback_jobs WHERE status NOT IN ('pending','processing') AND created_at<datetime('now','-30 days')`).run();
    await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE fallback_jobs SET payload='' WHERE type='otp' AND payload!='' AND expires_at<=?`).bind(now).run();
    await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE fallback_jobs SET status='failed',error='DELIVERY_UNKNOWN: \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u0632\u0634 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u06CC \u0645\u062A\u0648\u0642\u0641 \u0634\u062F\u061B \u0627\u062D\u062A\u0645\u0627\u0644 \u062A\u062D\u0648\u06CC\u0644 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0634\u062E\u0635 \u0627\u0633\u062A.',completed_at=datetime('now')
      WHERE status='processing' AND locked_at<?`).bind(now - 45e3).run();
    await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE fallback_jobs SET status='expired',error='EXPIRED: \u0645\u0647\u0644\u062A \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u062A\u0645\u0627\u0645 \u0634\u062F.',payload=CASE WHEN type='otp' THEN '' ELSE payload END,completed_at=datetime('now')
      WHERE status='pending' AND expires_at<=?`).bind(now).run();
    const job = await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE fallback_jobs SET status='processing',locked_at=?,attempts=attempts+1
      WHERE id=(SELECT id FROM fallback_jobs WHERE status='pending' AND next_at<=? AND channel NOT IN (SELECT channel FROM fallback_channel_limits WHERE until_ms>?) ORDER BY CASE WHEN type='otp' THEN 0 WHEN reason='test' THEN 1 WHEN reason='event' THEN 3 ELSE 2 END,next_at,rowid LIMIT 1)
      AND status='pending' RETURNING *`).bind(now, now, now).first();
    if (!job) return false;
    let status = "failed", error = null, providerId = null;
    let nextAt = now;
    try {
      const channel = config.channels.find((c) => c.channel === job.channel && c.chatId === job.chat_id);
      const { loadSettings: loadSettings2 } = await Promise.resolve().then(() => (init_settings(), settings_exports));
      const settings = job.reason === "inactive" ? await loadSettings2(env, true) : null;
      if (!channel || (job.reason === "event" ? !config.notifyEnabled || (job.type === "report_invoice" ? config.notifyInvoices === false : config.notifyActions === false) : !config.enabled && job.reason !== "test") || job.reason === "inactive" && settings && smsPanelActive(env, settings)) {
        status = "skipped";
        error = "CONFIG_CHANGED: \u0645\u0633\u06CC\u0631 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646 \u062E\u0627\u0645\u0648\u0634 \u06CC\u0627 \u0645\u0642\u0635\u062F/\u067E\u0646\u0644 \u0627\u0635\u0644\u06CC \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.";
      } else if (job.expires_at <= Date.now() + 12e3) {
        status = "expired";
        error = "EXPIRED";
      } else {
        const token = await channelToken(env, channel);
        if (!validBotToken(token)) throw new Error("INVALID_TOKEN");
        const payload = await unseal(env, job.payload, `job:${job.id}`);
        const result = await adapters[job.channel].send(token, job.chat_id, formatFallbackMessage(payload, job.type));
        if (result.ok) {
          status = "sent";
          providerId = result.messageId;
        } else {
          error = result.error;
          if (result.retryAfter) {
            nextAt = Date.now() + Math.max(3e3, result.retryAfter * 1e3);
            await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE fallback_channel_limits SET until_ms=? WHERE channel=?").bind(nextAt, job.channel).run();
            if (job.attempts < 3 && nextAt < job.expires_at - 12e3) status = "pending";
            else error = "RATE_LIMITED: \u0633\u0642\u0641 \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634 \u06CC\u0627 \u0645\u0647\u0644\u062A \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u062A\u0645\u0627\u0645 \u0634\u062F\u061B \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u0634\u062F.";
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {
      error = "CONFIG_OR_KEY_ERROR: \u062A\u0648\u06A9\u0646 \u06CC\u0627 \u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F \u0631\u0645\u0632\u0646\u06AF\u0627\u0631\u06CC \u062F\u0631 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u0631\u0633/\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.";
    }
    await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE fallback_jobs SET status=?,error=?,provider_id=?,next_at=?,
      payload=CASE WHEN type='otp' AND ?!='pending' THEN '' ELSE payload END,
      completed_at=CASE WHEN ?='pending' THEN NULL ELSE datetime('now') END WHERE id=? AND status='processing'`).bind(status, error, providerId, nextAt, status, status, job.id).run();
    return true;
  } finally {
    await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE fallback_dispatch SET until_ms=?,owner=NULL WHERE id=1 AND owner=?").bind(Date.now() + 3e3, owner).run();
  }
}
async function fallbackHistory(env, page = 1, status = "", channel = "") {
  page = Math.max(1, Math.min(1e4, Math.floor(page) || 1));
  const where = ["1=1"];
  const binds = [];
  if (["pending", "processing", "sent", "failed", "skipped", "expired"].includes(status)) {
    where.push("status=?");
    binds.push(status);
  }
  if (["telegram", "bale"].includes(channel)) {
    where.push("channel=?");
    binds.push(channel);
  }
  const filter = where.join(" AND ");
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT * FROM fallback_jobs WHERE ${filter} ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 30 OFFSET ?`).bind(...binds, (page - 1) * 30).all();
  const count = await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM fallback_jobs WHERE ${filter}`).bind(...binds).first();
  const items = await Promise.all((results ?? []).map(async (job) => {
    let content = null;
    if (job.type !== "otp" && job.payload) {
      try {
        content = await unseal(env, job.payload, `job:${job.id}`);
      } catch {
      }
    }
    const { payload, ...safe } = job;
    return { ...safe, content };
  }));
  const { results: counts } = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT status,COUNT(*) AS n FROM fallback_jobs GROUP BY status").all();
  const stats = Object.fromEntries((counts ?? []).map((c) => [c.status, c.n]));
  return { items, total: count?.n ?? 0, page, stats };
}
async function drainFallbackQueue(env) {
  if (await processFallbackQueue(env, true)) {
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 3100));
    await processFallbackQueue(env);
  }
}
async function retryFallbackJob(env, id, confirmDuplicateRisk = false) {
  const job = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM fallback_jobs WHERE id=?").bind(id).first();
  if (!job) throw new Error("NOT_FOUND");
  if (job.type === "otp") throw new Error("OTP_NO_REPLAY");
  if (job.status !== "failed" || job.expires_at <= Date.now() + 12e3) throw new Error("NOT_RETRYABLE");
  const certain = /^(INVALID_TOKEN|INVALID_CHAT|FORBIDDEN|CONFIG_OR_KEY_ERROR|RATE_LIMITED):/.test(job.error ?? "");
  if (!certain && !confirmDuplicateRisk) throw new Error("CONFIRM_DUPLICATE_RISK");
  const config = await loadFallbackConfig(env);
  const channel = config.channels.find((c) => c.channel === job.channel && c.chatId === job.chat_id);
  if (!channel || (job.reason === "event" ? !config.notifyEnabled || (job.type === "report_invoice" ? config.notifyInvoices === false : config.notifyActions === false) : !config.enabled && job.reason !== "test"))
    throw new Error("CONFIG_CHANGED");
  if (!validBotToken(await channelToken(env, channel))) throw new Error("INVALID_TOKEN");
  await unseal(env, job.payload, `job:${job.id}`);
  const changed = await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE fallback_jobs SET status='pending',attempts=0,next_at=?,locked_at=NULL,error=NULL,completed_at=NULL
    WHERE id=? AND status='failed' AND expires_at>? RETURNING id`).bind(Date.now(), id, Date.now() + 12e3).first();
  if (!changed) throw new Error("NOT_RETRYABLE");
  return { previousAttempts: job.attempts, previousError: job.error };
}
var CONFIG_KEY;
var init_fallback = __esm({
  "src/lib/fallback.ts"() {
    "use strict";
    init_crypto();
    init_fallback_adapters();
    init_utils();
    CONFIG_KEY = "sms_fallback_config";
  }
});

// src/lib/sms.ts
function sanitizeMessage(message, maxLength = SMS_MAX_LENGTH) {
  return String(message ?? "").replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/g, "").trim().slice(0, maxLength);
}
function queueErrorMessage(error) {
  return error instanceof Error && error.message === "MESSAGE_TOO_LONG" ? "\u0645\u062A\u0646 \u0628\u06CC\u0634 \u0627\u0632 \u06F2\u06F0\u06F0\u06F0 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u062C\u0644\u0648\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC \u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u0627\u0642\u0635\u060C \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u0634\u062F." : "\u062B\u0628\u062A \u062F\u0631 \u0635\u0641 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642 \u0628\u0648\u062F\u061B \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u062A\u060C \u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F \u0648 \u0638\u0631\u0641\u06CC\u062A \u0635\u0641 \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0631\u0631\u0633\u06CC \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.";
}
async function finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result) {
  if (result.status === "failed" && /^09\d{9}$/.test(normalizePhone(params.phone))) {
    try {
      const ids = await enqueueFallback(env, { ...params, message: sanitizeMessage(params.message, Infinity) }, "primary_failed");
      if (ids.length) {
        result.status = "pending";
        result.error = "\u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0635\u0644\u06CC \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642\u061B \u062F\u0631 \u0635\u0641 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646 \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u062F\u0645\u06CC\u0646 \u0642\u0631\u0627\u0631 \u06AF\u0631\u0641\u062A. " + (result.error ?? "");
      }
    } catch (error) {
      result.error = (result.error ?? "") + " | FALLBACK_QUEUE_FAILED: " + queueErrorMessage(error);
    }
  }
  try {
    await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO sms_logs (user_id, type, status, message, provider_id, error)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    ).bind(
      params.userId,
      params.type,
      result.status,
      params.type === "otp" ? "[\u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0645\u062D\u0631\u0645\u0627\u0646\u0647 \u2014 \u0630\u062E\u06CC\u0631\u0647 \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F]" : sanitizeMessage(params.message),
      result.messageId ?? null,
      result.error ?? null
    ).run();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("sms_logs insert failed:", error);
  }
}
function normalizeSmsMobile(raw2) {
  return normalizePhone(raw2);
}
function missingConfigMessage(provider) {
  if (provider === "smsir") {
    return "\u0633\u0631\u0648\u06CC\u0633 sms.ir \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0645\u0627 \u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F API \u06CC\u0627 \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062E\u0637 \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.";
  }
  if (provider === "kavenegar") {
    return "\u0633\u0631\u0648\u06CC\u0633 \u06A9\u0627\u0648\u0647\u200C\u0646\u06AF\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0645\u0627 \u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F API \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.";
  }
  return "\u0627\u0631\u0627\u0626\u0647\u200C\u062F\u0647\u0646\u062F\u0647 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.";
}
async function sendViaSmsIr(apiKey, sender, mobile, message) {
  const lineNumber = Number(String(sender).replace(/\D/g, ""));
  if (!Number.isFinite(lineNumber) || lineNumber <= 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062E\u0637 \u0641\u0631\u0633\u062A\u0646\u062F\u0647 (LineNumber) \u062F\u0631 sms.ir \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A."
    };
  }
  let response;
  try {
    response = await fetch("https://api.sms.ir/v1/send/bulk", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        Accept: "application/json",
        "X-API-KEY": apiKey
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        lineNumber,
        messageText: message,
        mobiles: [mobile],
        sendDateTime: null
      }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12e3)
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: `\u0627\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0644 \u0628\u0647 sms.ir \u0628\u0631\u0642\u0631\u0627\u0631 \u0646\u0634\u062F: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "\u062E\u0637\u0627\u06CC \u0634\u0628\u06A9\u0647"}`
    };
  }
  if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F API \u0633\u0627\u0645\u0627\u0646\u0647 sms.ir \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    return { ok: false, error: `sms.ir \u067E\u0627\u0633\u062E HTTP ${response.status} \u062F\u0627\u062F.` };
  }
  let payload;
  try {
    payload = await response.json();
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u067E\u0627\u0633\u062E sms.ir \u0642\u0627\u0628\u0644 \u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0646 \u0646\u0628\u0648\u062F." };
  }
  if (payload.status === 1) {
    const id = payload.data?.messageIds?.[0] ?? payload.data?.packId;
    return { ok: true, messageId: id !== void 0 && id !== null ? String(id) : void 0 };
  }
  return {
    ok: false,
    error: payload.message ? String(payload.message) : `sms.ir \u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A ${payload.status ?? "\u0646\u0627\u0645\u0634\u062E\u0635"} \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0631\u06AF\u0631\u062F\u0627\u0646\u062F.`
  };
}
async function sendViaKavenegar(apiKey, sender, mobile, message) {
  const url = new URL(`https://api.kavenegar.com/v1/${encodeURIComponent(apiKey)}/sms/send.json`);
  url.searchParams.set("receptor", mobile);
  if (sender) url.searchParams.set("sender", sender);
  url.searchParams.set("message", message);
  let response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(url.toString(), {
      method: "GET",
      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(1e4)
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: `\u0627\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0644 \u0628\u0647 \u06A9\u0627\u0648\u0647\u200C\u0646\u06AF\u0627\u0631 \u0628\u0631\u0642\u0631\u0627\u0631 \u0646\u0634\u062F: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "\u062E\u0637\u0627\u06CC \u0634\u0628\u06A9\u0647"}`
    };
  }
  if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F API \u06A9\u0627\u0648\u0647\u200C\u0646\u06AF\u0627\u0631 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    return { ok: false, error: `\u06A9\u0627\u0648\u0647\u200C\u0646\u06AF\u0627\u0631 \u067E\u0627\u0633\u062E HTTP ${response.status} \u062F\u0627\u062F.` };
  }
  let payload;
  try {
    payload = await response.json();
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u067E\u0627\u0633\u062E \u06A9\u0627\u0648\u0647\u200C\u0646\u06AF\u0627\u0631 \u0642\u0627\u0628\u0644 \u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0646 \u0646\u0628\u0648\u062F." };
  }
  const entry = payload.entries?.[0];
  const apiStatus = payload.return?.status ?? entry?.status ?? 0;
  if (apiStatus === 200) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      messageId: entry?.messageid ? String(entry.messageid) : void 0
    };
  }
  return {
    ok: false,
    error: payload.return?.message || entry?.statustext || `\u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A ${apiStatus}`
  };
}
async function sendSms(env, settings, params) {
  const message = sanitizeMessage(params.message);
  if (!message) {
    const result2 = { status: "skipped", error: "\u0645\u062A\u0646 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u062E\u0627\u0644\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A" };
    await finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result2);
    return result2;
  }
  const provider = settings.sms_provider;
  const mobile = normalizeSmsMobile(params.phone);
  if (!/^09\d{9}$/.test(mobile)) {
    const result2 = { status: "failed", error: "\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644 \u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u0646\u062F\u0647 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
    await finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result2);
    return result2;
  }
  if (!smsPanelActive(env, settings)) {
    try {
      const ids = await enqueueFallback(env, { ...params, phone: mobile, message: sanitizeMessage(params.message, Infinity) }, "inactive");
      if (ids.length) {
        const result2 = { status: "pending", error: "\u062F\u0631 \u0635\u0641 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646 \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u062F\u0645\u06CC\u0646\u061B \u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u0628\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06A9 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
        await finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result2);
        return result2;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      const result2 = { status: "skipped", error: "FALLBACK_QUEUE_FAILED: " + queueErrorMessage(error) };
      await finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result2);
      return result2;
    }
    if (settings.sms_enabled === 0) {
      const result2 = { status: "skipped", error: "\u067E\u0646\u0644 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9\u06CC \u0648 \u0645\u0633\u06CC\u0631 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646 \u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u0647\u0633\u062A\u0646\u062F." };
      await finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result2);
      return result2;
    }
  }
  if (provider === "console") {
    console.log(`[SMS:console] to=${mobile} type=${params.type} :: ${params.type === "otp" ? "[REDACTED OTP]" : message}`);
    const result2 = { status: "sent" };
    await finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result2);
    return result2;
  }
  const envKey = provider === "smsir" ? env.SMSIR_API_KEY : env.KAVENEGAR_API_KEY;
  const apiKey = (envKey || settings.sms_api_key).trim();
  const sender = (env.SMS_SENDER || settings.sms_sender).trim();
  if (!apiKey) {
    const result2 = { status: "failed", error: missingConfigMessage(provider) };
    await finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result2);
    return result2;
  }
  let outcome;
  if (provider === "smsir") {
    if (!sender) {
      const result2 = {
        status: "failed",
        error: "\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062E\u0637 \u0641\u0631\u0633\u062A\u0646\u062F\u0647 (LineNumber) \u062F\u0631 sms.ir \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A."
      };
      await finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result2);
      return result2;
    }
    outcome = await sendViaSmsIr(apiKey, sender, mobile, message);
  } else {
    outcome = await sendViaKavenegar(apiKey, sender, mobile, message);
  }
  const result = outcome.ok ? { status: "sent", messageId: outcome.messageId } : { status: "failed", error: outcome.error };
  await finalizeSmsResult(env, params, result);
  return result;
}
async function fetchSmsCredit(env, settings) {
  const apiKey = (env.SMSIR_API_KEY || settings.sms_api_key).trim();
  if (settings.sms_provider !== "smsir") {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u062F\u06CC\u062F\u0646 \u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631\u060C \u0633\u0631\u0648\u06CC\u0633 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0631\u0627 \u0631\u0648\u06CC sms.ir \u0628\u06AF\u0630\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062F." };
  }
  if (!apiKey) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F API \u0633\u0627\u0645\u0627\u0646\u0647 sms.ir \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
  }
  try {
    const response = await fetch("https://api.sms.ir/v1/credit", {
      method: "GET",
      headers: { Accept: "application/json", "X-API-KEY": apiKey },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(1e4)
    });
    if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
      return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F API \u0633\u0627\u0645\u0627\u0646\u0647 sms.ir \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
    }
    if (!response.ok) {
      return { ok: false, error: `sms.ir \u067E\u0627\u0633\u062E HTTP ${response.status} \u062F\u0627\u062F.` };
    }
    const payload = await response.json();
    if (payload.status === 1 && typeof payload.data === "number") {
      return { ok: true, credit: payload.data };
    }
    return { ok: false, error: payload.message || "\u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642 \u0628\u0648\u062F." };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: `\u0627\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0644 \u0628\u0647 sms.ir \u0628\u0631\u0642\u0631\u0627\u0631 \u0646\u0634\u062F: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "\u062E\u0637\u0627\u06CC \u0634\u0628\u06A9\u0647"}`
    };
  }
}
function renderSms(settings, type, vars) {
  const template = settings.sms_templates[type] ?? "";
  return renderTemplate(template, {
    cafe: settings.cafe_name,
    city: settings.cafe_city,
    ...vars
  });
}
function classifyWeather(id, temp) {
  if (id >= 200 && id < 300) {
    return {
      label: "\u0631\u0639\u062F \u0648 \u0628\u0631\u0642",
      emoji: "\u26C8\uFE0F",
      advice: "\u0647\u0648\u0627\u06CC \u0637\u0648\u0641\u0627\u0646\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0627\u06AF\u0631 \u0628\u06CC\u0631\u0648\u0646 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0622\u06CC\u06CC \u062D\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F\u060C \u06CC\u06A9 \u0642\u0647\u0648\u0647 \u06AF\u0631\u0645 \u062D\u0627\u0644\u062A \u0631\u0627 \u062E\u0648\u0628 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u062F"
    };
  }
  if (id >= 300 && id < 400) {
    return {
      label: "\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0627\u0646 \u0646\u0645\u200C\u0646\u0645",
      emoji: "\u{1F326}\uFE0F",
      advice: "\u0686\u062A\u0631 \u06CC\u0627\u062F\u062A \u0646\u0631\u0647 \u0648 \u06CC\u06A9 \u0646\u0648\u0634\u06CC\u062F\u0646\u06CC \u06AF\u0631\u0645 \u0645\u0647\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0645\u0627 \u0628\u0627\u0634"
    };
  }
  if (id >= 500 && id < 600) {
    return {
      label: "\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0627\u0646\u06CC",
      emoji: "\u{1F327}\uFE0F",
      advice: "\u0686\u062A\u0631 \u06CC\u0627\u062F\u062A \u0646\u0631\u0647 \u0648 \u06CC\u06A9 \u0646\u0648\u0634\u06CC\u062F\u0646\u06CC \u06AF\u0631\u0645 \u0645\u0647\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0645\u0627 \u0628\u0627\u0634"
    };
  }
  if (id >= 600 && id < 700) {
    return {
      label: "\u0628\u0631\u0641\u06CC",

    };
  }
  if (id >= 700 && id < 800) {
    return {
      label: "\u0645\u0647\u200C\u0622\u0644\u0648\u062F",
      emoji: "\u{1F32B}\uFE0F",
      advice: "\u0627\u062D\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0637 \u06A9\u0646\u060C \u062F\u06CC\u062F \u06A9\u0645 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u06CC\u06A9 \u0642\u0647\u0648\u0647 \u062F\u0627\u063A \u0647\u0645\u0631\u0627\u0647\u062A \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F"
    };

  if (id === 800) {

        label: "\u0622\u0641\u062A\u0627\u0628\u06CC \u0648 \u06AF\u0631\u0645",

      };
    }
    if (temp <= 8) {
      return {
        label: "\u0622\u0641\u062A\u0627\u0628\u06CC \u0648 \u0633\u0631\u062F",
        emoji: "\u{1F324}\uFE0F",
