    advice: "\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u062E\u0648\u0628\u06CC \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u06CC\u06A9 \u0641\u0646\u062C\u0627\u0646 \u0642\u0647\u0648\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A"
  };
}
async function fetchWeather(env, settings) {
  const apiKey = env.OPENWEATHER_API_KEY || settings.weather_api_key;
  const city = settings.cafe_city || "Tehran";
  if (!apiKey) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      city,
      temp: 0,
      conditionId: 0,
      description: "",
      label: "",
      advice: "",
      emoji: "",
      error: "\u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F API \u0647\u0648\u0627\u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u06CC \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A"
    };
  }
  try {
    const url = new URL("https://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather");
    url.searchParams.set("q", city);
    url.searchParams.set("appid", apiKey);
    url.searchParams.set("units", "metric");
    url.searchParams.set("lang", "fa");
    const response = await fetch(url.toString(), {
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(1e4)
    });
    if (!response.ok) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        city,
        temp: 0,
        conditionId: 0,
        description: "",
        label: "",
        advice: "",
        emoji: "",
        error: `OpenWeather HTTP ${response.status}`
      };
    }
    const payload = await response.json();
    const weather = payload.weather?.[0];
    const conditionId = weather?.id ?? 800;
    const temp = Math.round(Number(payload.main?.temp) || 0);
    const info = classifyWeather(conditionId, temp);
    return {
      ok: true,
      city: payload.name || city,
      temp,
      conditionId,
      description: weather?.description ?? "",
      label: info.label,
      advice: info.advice,
      emoji: info.emoji
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      city,
      temp: 0,
      conditionId: 0,
      description: "",
      label: "",
      advice: "",
      emoji: "",
      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "\u062E\u0637\u0627\u06CC \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0622\u0628\u200C\u0648\u0647\u0648\u0627"
    };
  }
}
function weatherSummary(info) {
  if (!info.ok) return info.error ?? "\u0646\u0627\u0645\u0634\u062E\u0635";
  return `${info.label}\u060C ${toPersianDigits(info.temp)}\xB0C`;
}
var SMS_MAX_LENGTH;
var init_sms = __esm({
  "src/lib/sms.ts"() {
    "use strict";
    init_fallback();
    init_utils();
    SMS_MAX_LENGTH = 480;
  }
});

// src/lib/db.ts
function calculatePoints(amount, settings) {
  const safeAmount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(amount) || 0));
  const unit = Math.max(1, settings.currency_unit);
  const ratio = Math.max(1, settings.points_per_currency);
  return Math.floor(safeAmount / unit) * ratio;
}
function calculateTier(points, settings) {
  const p = Math.max(0, Math.floor(points));
  if (p >= settings.tiers.vip) return "vip";
  if (p >= settings.tiers.gold) return "gold";
  if (p >= settings.tiers.silver) return "silver";
  return "bronze";
}
function tierLabel(tier) {
  return TIER_LABELS[tier] ?? "\u0628\u0631\u0646\u0632\u06CC";
}
async function findUserByPhone(db, phone) {
  return db.prepare("SELECT * FROM users WHERE phone = ? LIMIT 1").bind(normalizePhone(phone)).first();
}
async function findUserByPhoneLoose(db, rawPhone) {
  const normalized = normalizePhone(rawPhone);
  if (!normalized) return null;
  const digits = normalized.replace(/\D/g, "");
  const local = digits.startsWith("0") ? digits : `0${digits}`;
  const withoutZero = local.slice(1);
  const variants = Array.from(
    /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
      normalized,
      local,
      `+98${withoutZero}`,
      `98${withoutZero}`,
      `0098${withoutZero}`,
      withoutZero
    ])
  ).filter((value) => value.length > 0);
  if (variants.length === 0) return null;
  const placeholders = variants.map(() => "?").join(", ");
  const row = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT * FROM users
       WHERE phone IN (${placeholders})
          OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(phone, ' ', ''), '-', ''), '+98', '0') IN (?, ?)
       ORDER BY CASE WHEN phone = ? THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
       LIMIT 1`
  ).bind(...variants, local, withoutZero, normalized).first();
  return row ?? null;
}
async function findUserById(db, id) {
  return db.prepare("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1").bind(id).first();
}
async function generateUniqueReferralCode(db) {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 10; attempt += 1) {
    const code = generateCouponCode("REF");
    const exists = await db.prepare("SELECT id FROM users WHERE referral_code = ? LIMIT 1").bind(code).first();
    if (!exists) return code;
  }
  return `REF-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}`;
}
async function resolveReferrer(db, codeOrPhone) {
  const raw2 = String(codeOrPhone ?? "").trim();
  if (!raw2) return null;
  const byCode = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM users WHERE UPPER(referral_code) = UPPER(?) LIMIT 1").bind(raw2).first();
  if (byCode) return byCode;
  const normalized = normalizePhone(raw2);
  if (/^09\d{9}$/.test(normalized)) {
    const byPhone = await findUserByPhone(db, normalized);
    if (byPhone && byPhone.role === "customer") return byPhone;
  }
  return null;
}
async function registerCustomer(env, settings, input) {
  const phone = normalizePhone(input.phone);
  const existing = await findUserByPhone(env.DB, phone);
  if (existing) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "PHONE_EXISTS" };
  }
  let referrer = null;
  if (input.referral && input.referral.trim()) {
    referrer = await resolveReferrer(env.DB, input.referral);
    if (!referrer) {
      return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "INVALID_REFERRAL" };
    }
    if (referrer.phone === phone) {
      return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641 \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062E\u0648\u062F\u062A\u0627\u0646 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.", code: "INVALID_REFERRAL" };
    }
  }
  const referralCode = await generateUniqueReferralCode(env.DB);
  try {
    const smsOptIn = input.sms_opt_in === 0 ? 0 : 1;
    const result = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO users (name, phone, birth_date, points, tier, referred_by, role, referral_code, sms_opt_in)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0, 'bronze', ?, 'customer', ?, ?)`
    ).bind(
      input.name,
      phone,
      input.birth_date,
      referrer ? referrer.id : null,
      referralCode,
      smsOptIn
    ).run();
    const userId = Number(result.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0);
    const user = await findUserById(env.DB, userId);
    if (!user) {
      return { ok: false, error: "\u062E\u0637\u0627 \u062F\u0631 \u0627\u06CC\u062C\u0627\u062F \u062D\u0633\u0627\u0628 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0628\u0631\u06CC.", code: "DB_ERROR" };
    }
    if (smsOptIn === 1) {
      await sendSms(env, settings, {
        userId: user.id,
        phone: user.phone,
        type: "welcome",
        message: renderSms(settings, "welcome", {
          name: user.name,
          referral_code: user.referral_code ?? referralCode,
          points: 0
        })
      });
    }
    return {
      ok: true,
      user,
      referrerName: referrer?.name ?? null,
      referralCode: user.referral_code ?? referralCode
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("registerCustomer failed:", error);
    return { ok: false, error: "\u062E\u0637\u0627\u06CC \u067E\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u062F\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u062F\u0631 \u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0627\u0645.", code: "DB_ERROR" };
  }
}
function resolvePayment(amount, input) {
  const total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(amount) || 0));
  const rawCash = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(input?.cash ?? 0) || 0));
  const rawCard = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(input?.card ?? 0) || 0));
  const rawOnline = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(input?.online ?? 0) || 0));
  const tendered = rawCash + rawCard + rawOnline;
  if (!Number.isFinite(tendered) || tendered > 5e10) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A\u06CC \u0628\u06CC\u0634 \u0627\u0632 \u062D\u062F \u0645\u062C\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "INVALID_PAYMENT" };
  }
  if (tendered < total) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: `\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A\u06CC \u06A9\u0645\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B ${formatNumberFa(total - tendered)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u06A9\u0645 \u0627\u0633\u062A.`,
      code: "PAYMENT_SHORT"
    };
  }
  const change = tendered - total;
  if (change > rawCash) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "\u0628\u0627\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647 \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F \u0628\u06CC\u0634\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0646\u0642\u062F \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A\u06CC \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.",
      code: "INVALID_CHANGE"
    };
  }
  const cash = rawCash - change;
  const hasCash = cash > 0;
  const hasCard = rawCard > 0;
  const hasOnline = rawOnline > 0;
  let method;
  if (hasCash && (hasCard || hasOnline)) method = "mixed";
  else if (hasCash) method = "cash";
  else if (hasCard) method = "card";
  else if (hasOnline) method = "online";
  else method = "cash";
  return { ok: true, payment: { method, cash, card: rawCard, online: rawOnline, change, tendered } };
}
function itemsSubtotal(items) {
  return (items ?? []).reduce((sum, item) => {
    const qty = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(item.qty) || 1));
    const price = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item.price) || 0));
    return sum + qty * price;
  }, 0);
}
function normalizeItems(raw2) {
  if (!Array.isArray(raw2)) return [];
  return raw2.slice(0, 50).map((item) => {
    const obj = item ?? {};
    const name = String(obj.name ?? "").replace(/[<>]/g, "").trim().slice(0, 60);
    const qty = Math.min(99, Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(obj.qty) || 1)));
    const price = Math.min(1e9, Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(obj.price) || 0)));
    if (!name) return null;
    return { name, qty, price };
  }).filter((item) => item !== null);
}
async function evaluateCoupon(db, code, userId, subtotal) {
  const clean = String(code ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!clean) return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u062E\u0627\u0644\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
  const coupon = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM coupons WHERE UPPER(code) = ? LIMIT 1").bind(clean).first();
  if (!coupon) return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F." };
  if (coupon.is_active !== 1) return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
  if (coupon.user_id !== null && coupon.user_id !== userId) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0645\u062E\u0635\u0648\u0635 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062F\u06CC\u06AF\u0631\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
  }
  if (coupon.used_count >= coupon.max_uses) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
  }
  if (coupon.expires_at) {
    const expiresMs = sqliteToMs(coupon.expires_at);
    if (expiresMs !== null && expiresMs < Date.now()) {
      return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0628\u0647 \u067E\u0627\u06CC\u0627\u0646 \u0631\u0633\u06CC\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A." };
    }
  }
  const discount = coupon.kind === "percent" ? Math.floor(subtotal * Math.min(100, Math.max(0, coupon.value)) / 100) : Math.min(subtotal, Math.max(0, coupon.value));
  return { ok: true, coupon, discount };
}
async function hasVisitPointsToday(db, userId, timeZone) {
  const row = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT id FROM invoices
       WHERE user_id = ? AND visit_day = ? AND voided_at IS NULL
       LIMIT 1`
  ).bind(userId, todayISO(timeZone)).first();
  return !!row;
}
function calculateInvoicePoints(input) {
  const { amount, settings } = input;
  if (settings.points_mode === "invoice") {
    return {
      points: amount > 0 ? settings.invoice_points : 0,
      note: `\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062B\u0627\u0628\u062A \u0647\u0631 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 (${toPersianDigits(settings.invoice_points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632\u061B \u0645\u0633\u062A\u0642\u0644 \u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A)`,
      visitDay: false
    };
  }
  if (settings.points_mode === "visit") {
    if (amount < settings.visit_min_amount) {
      return {
        points: 0,
        note: `\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u06A9\u0645\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u062D\u062F\u0627\u0642\u0644 ${formatNumberFa(settings.visit_min_amount)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0631\u0648\u0632\u0627\u0646\u0647 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F.`,
        visitDay: false
      };
    }
    if (input.visitAlreadyEarned) {
      return {
        points: 0,
        note: "\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0636\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.",
        visitDay: false
      };
    }
    return {
      points: settings.visit_points,
      note: `\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0636\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 (${toPersianDigits(settings.visit_points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632)`,
      visitDay: true
    };
  }
  const points = calculatePoints(amount, settings);
  return {
    points,
    note: points > 0 ? `${toPersianDigits(points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u0627\u0633 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F` : "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0627\u0641\u06CC \u0646\u0628\u0648\u062F.",
    visitDay: false
  };
}
async function invoiceResultFromRow(db, row, customer, settings) {
  const after = await db.prepare("SELECT points, tier FROM users WHERE id = ?").bind(customer.id).first();
  return {
    ok: true,
    invoiceId: row.id,
    subtotal: row.subtotal,
    discount: row.discount,
    amount: row.amount,
    pointsEarned: row.points_earned,
    totalPoints: after?.points ?? customer.points,
    tier: after?.tier ?? customer.tier,
    tierChanged: false,
    customerName: customer.name,
    pointsNote: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0628\u0648\u062F\u061B \u0627\u0632 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062C\u0644\u0648\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC \u0634\u062F.",
    duplicate: true,
    paymentMethod: row.payment_method ?? null,
    paidCash: row.paid_cash ?? 0,
    paidCard: row.paid_card ?? 0,
    paidOnline: row.paid_online ?? 0,
    change: row.change_amount ?? 0
  };
}
async function recordInvoice(env, settings, input) {
  const customer = await findUserById(env.DB, input.userId);
  if (!customer) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F.", code: "USER_NOT_FOUND" };
  }
  if (customer.role !== "customer") {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u06A9\u0631\u062F.", code: "INVALID_ROLE" };
  }
  if (customer.is_active !== 1) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "\u062D\u0633\u0627\u0628 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0627\u0628\u062A\u062F\u0627 \u0622\u0646 \u0631\u0627 \u0627\u0632 \u067E\u0646\u0644 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A \u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.",
      code: "INACTIVE_CUSTOMER"
    };
  }
  const requestId = (input.clientRequestId ?? "").trim().slice(0, 64) || null;
  if (requestId) {
    const existing = await env.DB.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM invoices WHERE client_request_id = ? LIMIT 1"
    ).bind(requestId).first();
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.user_id !== customer.id) return { ok: false, error: "\u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u0647 \u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062F\u06CC\u06AF\u0631\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "REQUEST_ID_CONFLICT" };
      return invoiceResultFromRow(env.DB, existing, customer, settings);
    }
  }
  if (input.items.length > 50 || input.items.some((item) => !item || typeof item.name !== "string" || !String(item.name ?? "").replace(/[<>]/g, "").trim() || String(item.name).length > 60 || !Number.isSafeInteger(Number(item.qty)) || Number(item.qty) < 1 || Number(item.qty) > 99 || !Number.isSafeInteger(Number(item.price)) || Number(item.price) < 0 || Number(item.price) > 1e9)) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0646\u0627\u0645\u060C \u0642\u06CC\u0645\u062A \u06CC\u0627 \u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A (\u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u06F1 \u062A\u0627 \u06F9\u06F9).", code: "INVALID_ITEM" };
  }
  const items = normalizeItems(input.items);
  const subtotal = items.length > 0 ? itemsSubtotal(items) : Math.floor(Number(input.amount) || 0);
  if (!Number.isFinite(subtotal) || subtotal <= 0) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u0632\u0631\u06AF\u200C\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u0635\u0641\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.", code: "INVALID_AMOUNT" };
  }
  if (subtotal > 5e9) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u06CC\u0634 \u0627\u0632 \u062D\u062F \u0645\u062C\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "INVALID_AMOUNT" };
  }
  let discount = 0;
  let couponCode = null;
  let coupon = null;
  if (input.couponCode && input.couponCode.trim()) {
    const couponResult = await evaluateCoupon(env.DB, input.couponCode, customer.id, subtotal);
    if (couponResult.ok === false) {
      return { ok: false, error: couponResult.error, code: "INVALID_COUPON" };
    }
    coupon = couponResult.coupon;
    discount = Math.min(subtotal, couponResult.discount);
    couponCode = coupon.code;
  }
  if (!coupon && input.manualDiscount !== void 0 && input.manualDiscount !== null) {
    const manual = Math.floor(Number(input.manualDiscount) || 0);
    if (!Number.isFinite(manual) || manual < 0) {
      return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "INVALID_DISCOUNT" };
    }
    if (manual >= subtotal) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: "\u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F \u0628\u0631\u0627\u0628\u0631 \u06CC\u0627 \u0628\u06CC\u0634\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u062C\u0645\u0639 \u0627\u0642\u0644\u0627\u0645 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.",
        code: "INVALID_DISCOUNT"
      };
    }
    discount = manual;
  }
  const amount = subtotal - discount;
  const hasPaymentInput = !!input.payment && (input.payment.cash !== void 0 || input.payment.card !== void 0 || input.payment.online !== void 0);
  let payment = null;
  if (hasPaymentInput) {
    const check = resolvePayment(amount, input.payment);
    if (check.ok === false) {
      return { ok: false, error: check.error, code: check.code };
    }
    payment = check.payment;
  }
  const timeZone = resolveTimeZone(env.TZ_NAME);
  const visitAlreadyEarned = settings.points_mode === "visit" ? await hasVisitPointsToday(env.DB, customer.id, timeZone) : false;
  const calc = calculateInvoicePoints({
    amount,
    settings,
    visitAlreadyEarned
  });
  let pointsEarned = calc.points;
  let pointsNote = calc.note;
  let visitDay = calc.visitDay ? todayISO(timeZone) : null;
  const nowISO = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace("T", " ");
  let newPoints = Math.max(0, customer.points + pointsEarned);
  let newTier = calculateTier(
    Math.max(0, customer.lifetime_points + pointsEarned),
    settings
  );
  let tierChanged = newTier !== customer.tier;
  const buildStatements = (earned, day) => {
    const list = [
      env.DB.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO invoices
           (user_id, amount, items, points_earned, registered_by, discount, coupon_code,
            subtotal, created_at, visit_day, client_request_id,
            payment_method, paid_cash, paid_card, paid_online, change_amount, shift_id, replaces_invoice_id)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
      ).bind(
        customer.id,
        amount,
        JSON.stringify(items),
        earned,
        input.registeredBy,
        discount,
        couponCode,
        subtotal,
        nowISO,
        day,
        requestId,
        payment?.method ?? null,
        payment?.cash ?? 0,
        payment?.card ?? 0,
        payment?.online ?? 0,
        payment?.change ?? 0,
        input.shiftId ?? null,
        input.replacesInvoiceId ?? null
      ),
      // افزایش اتمیک امتیاز؛ سطح بر اساس امتیاز «طول عمر» محاسبه می‌شود تا
      // خرج کردن امتیاز باعث افت سطح نشود.
      env.DB.prepare(
        `UPDATE users
         SET points = points + ?,
             lifetime_points = lifetime_points + ?,
             tier = CASE
                      WHEN lifetime_points + ? >= ? THEN 'vip'
                      WHEN lifetime_points + ? >= ? THEN 'gold'
                      WHEN lifetime_points + ? >= ? THEN 'silver'
                      ELSE 'bronze'
                    END,
             last_visit_date = ?,
             first_purchase_at = COALESCE(first_purchase_at, ?)
         WHERE id = ? AND role = 'customer'`
      ).bind(
        earned,
        earned,
        earned,
        settings.tiers.vip,
        earned,
        settings.tiers.gold,
        earned,
        settings.tiers.silver,
        nowISO,
        nowISO,
        customer.id
      )
    ];
    if (coupon) {
      list.push(
        env.DB.prepare("UPDATE coupons SET used_count = used_count + 1 WHERE id = ?").bind(coupon.id)
      );
    }
    return list;
  };
  let invoiceId = 0;
  try {
    const results = await env.DB.batch(buildStatements(pointsEarned, visitDay));
    invoiceId = Number(results[0]?.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0);
  } catch (error) {
    const message = String(error);
    if (requestId && message.includes("client_request_id")) {
      const existing = await env.DB.prepare(
        "SELECT * FROM invoices WHERE client_request_id = ? LIMIT 1"
      ).bind(requestId).first();
      if (existing) {
        if (existing.user_id !== customer.id) return { ok: false, error: "\u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u0647 \u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062F\u06CC\u06AF\u0631\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "REQUEST_ID_CONFLICT" };
        return invoiceResultFromRow(env.DB, existing, customer, settings);
      }
    }
    if (visitDay && (message.includes("visit_day") || message.includes("ux_invoices_visit_day"))) {
      try {
        const results = await env.DB.batch(buildStatements(0, null));
        invoiceId = Number(results[0]?.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0);
        pointsEarned = 0;
        pointsNote = "\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0636\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.";
        visitDay = null;
      } catch (retryError) {
        console.error("recordInvoice retry failed:", retryError);
        return { ok: false, error: "\u062E\u0637\u0627 \u062F\u0631 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631.", code: "DB_ERROR" };
      }
    } else {
      console.error("recordInvoice batch failed:", error);
      return { ok: false, error: "\u062E\u0637\u0627 \u062F\u0631 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631.", code: "DB_ERROR" };
    }
  }
  const after = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT points, tier FROM users WHERE id = ?").bind(customer.id).first();
  if (after) {
    newPoints = after.points;
    newTier = after.tier;
    tierChanged = newTier !== customer.tier;
  }
  await recomputeAvgInterval(env.DB, customer.id);
  if (customer.referred_by && customer.referral_rewarded !== 1) {
    await rewardReferrer(env, settings, customer);
  }
  if (!input.deferReceipt) await sendInvoiceReceipt(env, settings, invoiceId);
  return {
    ok: true,
    invoiceId,
    subtotal,
    discount,
    amount,
    pointsEarned,
    totalPoints: newPoints,
    tier: newTier,
    tierChanged,
    customerName: customer.name,
    pointsNote,
    duplicate: false,
    paymentMethod: payment?.method ?? null,
    paidCash: payment?.cash ?? 0,
    paidCard: payment?.card ?? 0,
    paidOnline: payment?.online ?? 0,
    change: payment?.change ?? 0
  };
}
async function sendInvoiceReceipt(env, settings, invoiceId) {
  try {
    const row = await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT i.amount,i.discount,i.coupon_code,i.points_earned,
      u.id AS user_id,u.name,u.phone,u.points,u.tier FROM invoices i JOIN users u ON u.id=i.user_id
      WHERE i.id=? AND i.voided_at IS NULL`).bind(invoiceId).first();
    if (!row) return null;
    await sendSms(env, settings, {
      userId: row.user_id,
      phone: row.phone,
      type: "invoice",
      message: renderSms(settings, "invoice", {
        name: row.name,
        amount: formatNumberFa(row.amount),
        points: toPersianDigits(row.points_earned),
        total_points: toPersianDigits(row.points),
        tier: tierLabel(row.tier),
        discount: formatNumberFa(row.discount),
        code: row.coupon_code ?? ""
      })
    });
    return { pointsEarned: row.points_earned, totalPoints: row.points };
  } catch {
    console.error("INVOICE_RECEIPT_FAILED", invoiceId);
    return null;
  }
}
async function rewardReferrer(env, settings, customer) {
  if (!customer.referred_by) return;
  const referrer = await findUserById(env.DB, customer.referred_by);
  if (!referrer || referrer.is_active !== 1) return;
  const reward = Math.max(0, settings.referral_reward_points);
  const claim = await env.DB.prepare(
    "UPDATE users SET referral_rewarded = 1 WHERE id = ? AND referral_rewarded = 0"
  ).bind(customer.id).run();
  if (!claim.meta?.changes) return;
  if (reward > 0) {
    const updated = await env.DB.prepare(
      `UPDATE users
       SET points = points + ?, lifetime_points = lifetime_points + ?
       WHERE id = ? AND role = 'customer'
       RETURNING points, lifetime_points`
    ).bind(reward, reward, referrer.id).first();
    if (updated) {
      await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE users SET tier = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(calculateTier(updated.lifetime_points, settings), referrer.id).run();
    }
    await sendSms(env, settings, {
      userId: referrer.id,
      phone: referrer.phone,
      type: "referral",
      message: renderSms(settings, "welcome", {
        name: referrer.name,
        points: reward,
        referral_code: customer.name
      })
    });
  }
}
async function recomputeAvgInterval(db, userId) {
  const { results } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT created_at FROM invoices
       WHERE user_id = ? AND voided_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY created_at DESC
       LIMIT 100`
  ).bind(userId).all();
  const days = Array.from(
    new Set(
      (results ?? []).map((row) => row.created_at.slice(0, 10)).filter((value) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value))
    )
  ).sort();
  let avg = 0;
  if (days.length >= 2) {
    const msList = days.map((d) => Date.parse(`${d}T00:00:00Z`));
    let total = 0;
    let count = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < msList.length; i += 1) {
      const diff = Math.round((msList[i] - msList[i - 1]) / 864e5);
      if (diff > 0) {
        total += diff;
        count += 1;
      }
    }
    avg = count > 0 ? Math.round(total / count * 10) / 10 : 0;
  }
  await db.prepare("UPDATE users SET avg_visit_interval = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(avg, userId).run();
  return avg;
}
async function getDashboardStats(db, settings, dayStartUtc, dayEndUtc, monthStartUtc, daysAgo30Utc, seriesDays) {
  const [
    totalCustomers,
    newCustomers30d,
    totalInvoices,
    todaySales,
    monthSales,
    todayInvoices,
    pointsOutstanding,
    smsSentToday,
    activeCustomers30d
  ] = await Promise.all([
    db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users WHERE role = 'customer'`).first(),
    db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users WHERE role = 'customer' AND created_at >= ?`).bind(daysAgo30Utc).first(),
    db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NULL").first(),
    db.prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s, COUNT(*) AS c FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?").bind(dayStartUtc, dayEndUtc).first(),
    db.prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ?").bind(monthStartUtc).first(),
    db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?").bind(dayStartUtc, dayEndUtc).first(),
    db.prepare(`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(points), 0) AS s FROM users WHERE role = 'customer'`).first(),
    db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM sms_logs WHERE sent_at >= ? AND sent_at < ? AND status = 'sent'`).bind(dayStartUtc, dayEndUtc).first(),
    db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users WHERE role = 'customer' AND last_visit_date >= ?`).bind(daysAgo30Utc).first()
  ]);
  const { results: sleepRows } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT u.id, u.name, u.last_visit_date, u.avg_visit_interval,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoices i WHERE i.user_id = u.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL) AS visits
       FROM users u
       WHERE u.role = 'customer' AND u.is_active = 1 AND u.last_visit_date IS NOT NULL
       LIMIT 5000`
  ).all();
  const sleeping = (sleepRows ?? []).filter((row) => {
    const ms = sqliteToMs(row.last_visit_date);
    if (ms === null) return false;
    const days = Math.floor((Date.now() - ms) / 864e5);
    const reliable = row.visits >= settings.churn_min_visits && row.avg_visit_interval > 0;
    const baseline = reliable ? row.avg_visit_interval : CHURN_DEFAULT_INTERVAL;
    return days > baseline + settings.churn_grace_days;
  });
  const { results: top } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT id, name, phone, points, tier FROM users
       WHERE role = 'customer' AND is_active = 1
       ORDER BY points DESC LIMIT 5`
  ).all();
  const { results: recent } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT i.id, i.amount, i.created_at, u.name AS customer_name, u.id AS customer_id
       FROM invoices i JOIN users u ON u.id = i.user_id
       WHERE i.voided_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY i.created_at DESC, i.id DESC LIMIT 8`
  ).all();
  let salesSeries = [];
  if (seriesDays && seriesDays.length > 0) {
    const totals = await Promise.all(
      seriesDays.map(
        (range) => db.prepare(
          "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?"
        ).bind(range.start, range.end).first()
      )
    );
    salesSeries = seriesDays.map((range, index) => ({
      date: range.date,
      total: totals[index]?.s ?? 0
    }));
  } else {
    const { results: seriesRows } = await db.prepare(
      `SELECT substr(created_at, 1, 10) AS day, COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS total
         FROM invoices
         WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= datetime('now', '-6 days')
         GROUP BY day ORDER BY day ASC`
    ).all();
    const salesByDay = new Map((seriesRows ?? []).map((r) => [r.day, r.total]));
    for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
      const d = new Date(Date.now() - i * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      salesSeries.push({ date: d, total: salesByDay.get(d) ?? 0 });
    }
  }
  return {
    totalCustomers: totalCustomers?.c ?? 0,
    newCustomers30d: newCustomers30d?.c ?? 0,
    totalInvoices: totalInvoices?.c ?? 0,
    todaySales: todaySales?.s ?? 0,
    monthSales: monthSales?.s ?? 0,
    todayInvoices: todayInvoices?.c ?? 0,
    sleepingCustomers: sleeping.length,
    activeCustomers30d: activeCustomers30d?.c ?? 0,
    pointsOutstanding: pointsOutstanding?.s ?? 0,
    smsSentToday: smsSentToday?.c ?? 0,
    topCustomers: top ?? [],
    recentInvoices: recent ?? [],
    salesSeries
  };
}
async function exportCustomers(db) {
  const { results } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT u.id, u.name, u.phone, u.birth_date, u.points, u.tier, u.last_visit_date,
              u.avg_visit_interval, u.created_at, u.referral_code, u.is_active,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoices i WHERE i.user_id = u.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL) AS visits,
              (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) FROM invoices i WHERE i.user_id = u.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL) AS total_spent
       FROM users u
       WHERE u.role = 'customer'
       ORDER BY u.created_at DESC
       LIMIT 20000`
  ).all();
  return results ?? [];
}
async function listCustomers(db, params) {
  const page = Math.max(1, Math.floor(params.page ?? 1));
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(5, Math.floor(params.pageSize ?? 20)));
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  const where = [`u.role = 'customer'`];
  const binds = [];
  const q = String(params.q ?? "").trim().slice(0, 60);
  if (q) {
    where.push("(u.name LIKE ? OR u.phone LIKE ? OR u.referral_code LIKE ?)");
    const like = `%${q}%`;
    binds.push(like, like, like);
  }
  if (params.tier && ["bronze", "silver", "gold", "vip"].includes(params.tier)) {
    where.push("u.tier = ?");
    binds.push(params.tier);
  }
  if (params.status === "sleeping" || params.status === "active") {
    const baseline = `(CASE WHEN u.avg_visit_interval > 0
             AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoices i WHERE i.user_id = u.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL) >= ?
        THEN u.avg_visit_interval ELSE ? END)`;
    const comparison = params.status === "sleeping" ? ">" : "<=";
    where.push(
      `u.last_visit_date IS NOT NULL
       AND (julianday('now') - julianday(u.last_visit_date)) ${comparison} (${baseline} + ?)`
    );
    binds.push(
      Math.max(1, Math.floor(params.minVisits ?? 3)),
      CHURN_DEFAULT_INTERVAL,
      params.graceDays ?? 2
    );
  } else if (params.status === "no_visit") {
    where.push("u.last_visit_date IS NULL");
  }
  const orderBy = params.sort === "points" ? "u.points DESC" : params.sort === "name" ? "u.name ASC" : params.sort === "created" ? "u.created_at DESC" : "COALESCE(u.last_visit_date, u.created_at) DESC";
  const whereSql = where.join(" AND ");
  const countRow = await db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users u WHERE ${whereSql}`).bind(...binds).first();
  const { results } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT u.id, u.name, u.phone, u.birth_date, u.points, u.tier, u.last_visit_date,
              u.avg_visit_interval, u.created_at, u.referral_code, u.is_active,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoices i WHERE i.user_id = u.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL) AS visits,
              (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) FROM invoices i WHERE i.user_id = u.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL) AS total_spent
       FROM users u
       WHERE ${whereSql}
       ORDER BY ${orderBy}
       LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`
  ).bind(...binds, pageSize, offset).all();
  return {
    items: results ?? [],
    total: countRow?.c ?? 0,
    page,
    pageSize
  };
}
async function getCustomerDetail(db, id) {
  const user = await findUserById(db, id);
  if (!user || user.role !== "customer") {
    return { user: null, invoices: [], coupons: [], referrer: null, referrals: [], totalSpent: 0, visitCount: 0 };
  }
  const { results: invoices } = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM invoices WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50").bind(id).all();
  const { results: coupons } = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM coupons WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50").bind(id).all();
  const referrer = user.referred_by ? await db.prepare("SELECT id, name, phone FROM users WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1").bind(user.referred_by).first() : null;
  const { results: referrals } = await db.prepare(
    "SELECT id, name, created_at, referral_rewarded AS rewarded FROM users WHERE referred_by = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50"
  ).bind(id).all();
  const totalRow = await db.prepare("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s, COUNT(*) AS c FROM invoices WHERE user_id = ? AND voided_at IS NULL").bind(id).first();
  return {
    user,
    invoices: invoices ?? [],
    coupons: coupons ?? [],
    referrer: referrer ?? null,
    referrals: (referrals ?? []).map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      name: r.name,
      created_at: r.created_at,
      rewarded: !!r.rewarded
    })),
    totalSpent: totalRow?.s ?? 0,
    visitCount: totalRow?.c ?? 0
  };
}
async function createCoupon(db, input) {
  const code = (input.code ?? generateCouponCode("CAFE")).toUpperCase();
  try {
    const result = await db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO coupons (code, user_id, kind, value, reason, max_uses, expires_at)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    ).bind(
      code,
      input.userId,
      input.kind,
      input.value,
      input.reason,
      input.maxUses ?? 1,
      input.expiresAt
    ).run();
    return await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM coupons WHERE id = ?").bind(Number(result.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0)).first();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("createCoupon failed:", error);
    return null;
  }
}
function customerActivityStatus(user, graceDays, minVisits = 3) {
  const ms = sqliteToMs(user.last_visit_date);
  if (ms === null) return { label: "\u0628\u062F\u0648\u0646 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F", tone: "muted" };
  const days = Math.floor((Date.now() - ms) / 864e5);
  const reliable = (user.visits ?? 0) >= minVisits && user.avg_visit_interval > 0;
  const baseline = reliable ? user.avg_visit_interval : CHURN_DEFAULT_INTERVAL;
  const threshold = baseline + graceDays;
  if (days > threshold + 14) return { label: "\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0628\u06CC\u062F\u0647", tone: "danger" };
  if (days > threshold) return { label: "\u062F\u0631 \u062E\u0637\u0631 \u0631\u06CC\u0632\u0634", tone: "warning" };
  return { label: "\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644", tone: "success" };
}
function describeItems(raw2) {
  const items = safeJsonParse(raw2, []);
  if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) return "\u2014";
  return items.slice(0, 4).map((item) => `${item.name} \xD7${toPersianDigits(item.qty)}`).join("\u060C ");
}
async function listInvoices(db, params) {
  const page = Math.max(1, Math.floor(params.page ?? 1));
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(5, Math.floor(params.pageSize ?? 25)));
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  const where = ["1 = 1"];
  const binds = [];
  const q = String(params.q ?? "").trim().slice(0, 60);
  if (q) {
    const latin = toLatinDigits(q);
    const onlyDigits = /^\d{1,12}$/.test(latin);
    where.push(
      onlyDigits ? "(u.name LIKE ? OR u.phone LIKE ? OR CAST(i.id AS TEXT) = ? OR i.items LIKE ?)" : "(u.name LIKE ? OR u.phone LIKE ? OR i.items LIKE ?)"
    );
    binds.push(`%${q}%`, `%${latin}%`);
    if (onlyDigits) binds.push(latin);
    binds.push(`%${q}%`);
  }
  const tz = params.timeZone ?? DEFAULT_TIMEZONE;
  if (params.from && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(params.from)) {
    const startMs = zonedDateStartMs(params.from, tz);
    if (startMs !== null) {
      where.push("i.created_at >= ?");
      binds.push(utcStamp(startMs));
    }
  }
  if (params.to && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(params.to)) {
    const startMs = zonedDateStartMs(params.to, tz);
    if (startMs !== null) {
      where.push("i.created_at < ?");
      binds.push(utcStamp(startMs + 864e5));
    }
  }
  if (params.staffId) {
    where.push("i.registered_by = ?");
    binds.push(params.staffId);
  }
  const status = params.status ?? "all";
  if (status === "valid") where.push("i.voided_at IS NULL");
  else if (status === "voided") where.push("i.voided_at IS NOT NULL");
  const paymentMethod = String(params.paymentMethod ?? "").trim();
  if (["cash", "card", "online", "mixed"].includes(paymentMethod)) {
    where.push("i.payment_method = ?");
    binds.push(paymentMethod);
  } else if (paymentMethod === "unknown") {
    where.push("i.payment_method IS NULL");
  }
  const whereSql = where.join(" AND ");
  const totalRow = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS c,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN i.voided_at IS NULL THEN i.amount ELSE 0 END), 0) AS s,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN i.voided_at IS NULL THEN i.discount ELSE 0 END), 0) AS d,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN i.voided_at IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS v,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN i.voided_at IS NULL THEN i.paid_cash ELSE 0 END), 0) AS pc,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN i.voided_at IS NULL THEN i.paid_card ELSE 0 END), 0) AS pcard,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN i.voided_at IS NULL THEN i.paid_online ELSE 0 END), 0) AS po
       FROM invoices i JOIN users u ON u.id = i.user_id
       WHERE ${whereSql}`
  ).bind(...binds).first();
  const { results } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT i.*, u.name AS customer_name, u.phone AS customer_phone, s.name AS staff_name
       FROM invoices i
       JOIN users u ON u.id = i.user_id
       LEFT JOIN users s ON s.id = i.registered_by
       WHERE ${whereSql}
       ORDER BY i.id DESC
       LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`
  ).bind(...binds, pageSize, offset).all();
  return {
    items: results ?? [],
    total: totalRow?.c ?? 0,
    sum: totalRow?.s ?? 0,
    discount: totalRow?.d ?? 0,
    page,
    pageSize,
    voidedCount: totalRow?.v ?? 0,
    paymentTotals: {
      cash: totalRow?.pc ?? 0,
      card: totalRow?.pcard ?? 0,
      online: totalRow?.po ?? 0
    }
  };
}
async function listStaff(db) {
  const { results } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT u.id, u.name, u.phone, u.role, u.is_active, u.created_at,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoices i WHERE i.registered_by = u.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL) AS invoices_count
       FROM users u
       WHERE u.role IN ('staff', 'admin')
       ORDER BY u.role ASC, u.id ASC`
  ).all();
  return results ?? [];
}
async function listSmsLogs(db, params) {
  const page = Math.max(1, Math.floor(params.page ?? 1));
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(10, Math.floor(params.pageSize ?? 30)));
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  const where = ["1 = 1"];
  const binds = [];
  if (params.type && ["welcome", "invoice", "birthday", "churn", "weather", "referral", "admin"].includes(params.type)) {
    where.push("l.type = ?");
    binds.push(params.type);
  }
  if (params.status && ["sent", "failed", "skipped", "pending"].includes(params.status)) {
    where.push("l.status = ?");
    binds.push(params.status);
  }
  const whereSql = where.join(" AND ");
  const totalRow = await db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM sms_logs l WHERE ${whereSql}`).bind(...binds).first();
  const { results } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT l.*, u.name AS user_name, u.phone AS user_phone
       FROM sms_logs l
       LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = l.user_id
       WHERE ${whereSql}
       ORDER BY l.id DESC
       LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`
  ).bind(...binds, pageSize, offset).all();
  return {
    items: results ?? [],
    total: totalRow?.c ?? 0,
    page,
    pageSize
  };
}
async function listCoupons(db, params) {
  const page = Math.max(1, Math.floor(params.page ?? 1));
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(10, Math.floor(params.pageSize ?? 25)));
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  const where = ["1 = 1"];
  const binds = [];
  if (params.status === "active") {
    where.push(`c.is_active = 1 AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at >= datetime('now')) AND c.used_count < c.max_uses`);
  } else if (params.status === "expired") {
    where.push(`(c.is_active = 0 OR (c.expires_at IS NOT NULL AND c.expires_at < datetime('now')))`);
  } else if (params.status === "used") {
    where.push("c.used_count >= c.max_uses");
  }
  const whereSql = where.join(" AND ");
  const totalRow = await db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM coupons c WHERE ${whereSql}`).bind(...binds).first();
  const { results } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT c.*, u.name AS user_name, u.phone AS user_phone
       FROM coupons c
       LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = c.user_id
       WHERE ${whereSql}
       ORDER BY c.id DESC
       LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`
  ).bind(...binds, pageSize, offset).all();
  return {
    items: results ?? [],
    total: totalRow?.c ?? 0,
    page,
    pageSize
  };
}
async function adjustPoints(db, settings, userId, delta) {
  const user = await findUserById(db, userId);
  if (!user) return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0628\u0631 \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F.", code: "NOT_FOUND" };
  if (user.role !== "customer")
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0628\u0631 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.", code: "NOT_CUSTOMER" };
  const signed = Math.trunc(delta);
  const row = await db.prepare(
    `UPDATE users
       SET points = MAX(0, points + ?),
           lifetime_points = MAX(0, lifetime_points + ?)
       WHERE id = ? AND role = 'customer'
       RETURNING points, lifetime_points`
  ).bind(signed, signed, userId).first();
  if (!row) return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0628\u0631 \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F.", code: "NOT_FOUND" };
  const tier = calculateTier(row.lifetime_points, settings);
  await db.prepare("UPDATE users SET tier = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(tier, userId).run();
  return { ok: true, points: row.points, tier };
}
async function voidInvoice(env, settings, invoiceId, adminId, options = {}) {
  const invoice = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM invoices WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1").bind(invoiceId).first();
  if (!invoice) return { ok: false, error: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F.", code: "NOT_FOUND" };
  if (invoice.voided_at) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "ALREADY_VOID" };
  }
  let closedShift = false;
  if (invoice.shift_id) {
    const shift = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT status FROM shifts WHERE id = ?").bind(invoice.shift_id).first();
    if (shift?.status === "closed") {
      closedShift = true;
      if (options.allowClosedShift !== true) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0645\u0631\u0628\u0648\u0637 \u0628\u0647 \u06CC\u06A9 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A. \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644\u060C \u0627\u0632 \xAB\u0627\u0635\u0644\u0627\u062D \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\xBB \u06CC\u0627 \u062A\u0623\u06CC\u06CC\u062F \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631 \u0627\u0635\u0644\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.",
          code: "SHIFT_CLOSED"
        };
      }
    }
  }
  const customer = await findUserById(env.DB, invoice.user_id);
  if (!customer) return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F.", code: "NOT_FOUND" };
  const nowISO = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace("T", " ");
  const reason = String(options.reason ?? "").trim().slice(0, 200) || null;
  const claim = await env.DB.prepare(
    `UPDATE invoices
     SET voided_at = ?, voided_by = ?, visit_day = NULL, void_reason = ?
     WHERE id = ? AND voided_at IS NULL`
  ).bind(nowISO, adminId, reason, invoiceId).run();
  if (!claim.meta?.changes) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "ALREADY_VOID" };
  }
  const points = Math.max(0, Math.floor(invoice.points_earned));
  const statements = [
    env.DB.prepare(
      `UPDATE users
       SET points = MAX(0, points - ?),
           lifetime_points = MAX(0, lifetime_points - ?)
       WHERE id = ?`
    ).bind(points, points, invoice.user_id)
  ];
  if (invoice.coupon_code) {
    statements.push(
      env.DB.prepare(
        `UPDATE coupons SET used_count = MAX(0, used_count - 1)
         WHERE UPPER(code) = UPPER(?)`
      ).bind(invoice.coupon_code)
    );
  }
  if (options.replacementInvoiceId && settings.points_mode === "visit" && invoice.visit_day === todayISO(resolveTimeZone(env.TZ_NAME))) {
    statements.push(
      env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE invoices SET points_earned=?,visit_day=?
        WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND replaces_invoice_id=? AND voided_at IS NULL
          AND points_earned=0 AND visit_day IS NULL AND amount>=?
          AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM invoices WHERE user_id=? AND visit_day=? AND voided_at IS NULL)`).bind(
        settings.visit_points,
        invoice.visit_day,
        options.replacementInvoiceId,
        invoice.user_id,
        invoiceId,
        settings.visit_min_amount,
        invoice.user_id,
        invoice.visit_day
      ),
      // changes() is from the immediately preceding UPDATE, within this batch transaction.
      env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE users SET points=points+?,lifetime_points=lifetime_points+?
        WHERE id=? AND changes()=1`).bind(settings.visit_points, settings.visit_points, invoice.user_id)
    );
  }
  try {
    await env.DB.batch(statements);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("voidInvoice: reversal batch failed, rolling back:", error);
    try {
      await env.DB.prepare(
        `UPDATE invoices
         SET voided_at = NULL, voided_by = NULL, void_reason = NULL, visit_day = ?
         WHERE id = ? AND voided_at = ?`
      ).bind(invoice.visit_day, invoiceId, nowISO).run();
    } catch (rollbackError) {
      console.error("voidInvoice: rollback failed:", rollbackError);
    }
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "\u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0646\u0634\u062F \u0648 \u0647\u06CC\u0686 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631\u06CC \u0627\u0639\u0645\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u0634\u062F. \u0644\u0637\u0641\u0627\u064B \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.",
      code: "DB_ERROR"
    };
  }
  const lifeRow = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT lifetime_points FROM users WHERE id = ?").bind(invoice.user_id).first();
  if (lifeRow) {
    await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE users SET tier = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(calculateTier(lifeRow.lifetime_points, settings), invoice.user_id).run();
  }
  const lastRow = await env.DB.prepare(
    "SELECT MAX(created_at) AS d FROM invoices WHERE user_id = ? AND voided_at IS NULL"
  ).bind(invoice.user_id).first();
  await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE users SET last_visit_date = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(lastRow?.d ?? null, invoice.user_id).run();
  await recomputeAvgInterval(env.DB, invoice.user_id);
  return {
    ok: true,
    pointsReversed: points,
    customerId: invoice.user_id,
    customerName: customer.name,
    amount: invoice.amount,
    closedShift
  };
}
async function redeemPointsForCoupon(env, settings, userId) {
  if (settings.redeem_enabled !== 1) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u062A\u0628\u062F\u06CC\u0644 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0628\u0647 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u062D\u0627\u0636\u0631 \u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.", code: "REDEEM_DISABLED" };
  }
  const user = await findUserById(env.DB, userId);
  if (!user || user.role !== "customer") {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0628\u0631 \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F.", code: "NOT_FOUND" };
  }
  if (user.points < settings.redeem_points) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: `\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u0632\u0647 ${toPersianDigits(settings.redeem_points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0644\u0627\u0632\u0645 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0645\u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F\u06CC \u0634\u0645\u0627 ${toPersianDigits(user.points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0633\u062A.`,
      code: "NOT_ENOUGH_POINTS"
    };
  }
  const deducted = await env.DB.prepare(
    `UPDATE users SET points = points - ?
     WHERE id = ? AND role = 'customer' AND points >= ?
     RETURNING points`
  ).bind(settings.redeem_points, userId, settings.redeem_points).first();
  if (!deducted) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0627\u0641\u06CC \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.", code: "NOT_ENOUGH_POINTS" };
  }
  const expiresAt = utcStamp(Date.now() + settings.redeem_expiry_days * 864e5);
  const coupon = await createCoupon(env.DB, {
    userId,
    kind: "amount",
    value: settings.redeem_value,
    reason: "redeem",
    expiresAt,
    maxUses: 1
  });
  if (!coupon) {
    await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE users SET points = points + ? WHERE id = ?").bind(settings.redeem_points, userId).run();
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642 \u0628\u0648\u062F\u061B \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0628\u0631\u06AF\u0634\u062A \u062F\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u0634\u062F.", code: "COUPON_FAILED" };
  }
  await sendSms(env, settings, {
    userId,
    phone: user.phone,
    type: "redeem",
    message: renderSms(settings, "redeem", {
      name: user.name,
      points: toPersianDigits(settings.redeem_points),
      coupon: coupon.code,
      value: formatNumberFa(settings.redeem_value),
      expiry: formatJalali(expiresAt)
    })
  });
  return {
    ok: true,
    code: coupon.code,
    value: coupon.value,
    expiresAt,
    pointsLeft: deducted.points
  };
}
async function getCustomerPanelData(db, userId, settings) {
  const user = await findUserById(db, userId);
  if (!user || user.role !== "customer") return null;
  const { results: invoices } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT * FROM invoices
       WHERE user_id = ? AND voided_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 20`
  ).bind(userId).all();
  const { results: coupons } = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM coupons WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50").bind(userId).all();
  const totalRow = await db.prepare(
    "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s, COUNT(*) AS c FROM invoices WHERE user_id = ? AND voided_at IS NULL"
  ).bind(userId).first();
  const refRow = await db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users WHERE referred_by = ?").bind(userId).first();
  const order2 = [
    { key: "silver", label: "\u0646\u0642\u0631\u0647\u200C\u0627\u06CC", threshold: settings.tiers.silver },
    { key: "gold", label: "\u0637\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u06CC", threshold: settings.tiers.gold },
    { key: "vip", label: "\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0627\u0633\u06CC", threshold: settings.tiers.vip }
  ];
  const next = order2.find((tier) => user.lifetime_points < tier.threshold) ?? null;
  const currentFloor = [...order2].reverse().find((tier) => user.lifetime_points >= tier.threshold)?.threshold ?? 0;
  return {
    user,
    invoices: invoices ?? [],
    coupons: coupons ?? [],
    activeCoupons: (coupons ?? []).filter((coupon) => {
      if (coupon.is_active !== 1) return false;
      if (coupon.used_count >= coupon.max_uses) return false;
      if (!coupon.expires_at) return true;
      const ms = sqliteToMs(coupon.expires_at);
      return ms === null || ms >= Date.now();
    }),
    totalSpent: totalRow?.s ?? 0,
    visits: totalRow?.c ?? 0,
    referrals: refRow?.c ?? 0,
    nextTier: next ? {
      key: next.key,
      label: next.label,
      current: Math.max(0, user.lifetime_points - currentFloor),
      target: Math.max(1, next.threshold - currentFloor),
      need: Math.max(0, next.threshold - user.lifetime_points)
    } : null
  };
}
async function getPopularItems(db, limit = 8) {
  const safeLimit = Math.min(20, Math.max(1, Math.floor(limit)));
  const { results } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT items FROM invoices
       WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND items <> '[]'
       ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 300`
  ).all();
  const counts = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const row of results ?? []) {
    const parsed = safeJsonParse(row.items, []);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) continue;
    for (const item of parsed) {
      const name = String(item?.name ?? "").trim();
      if (!name) continue;
      const qty = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item?.qty) || 0)) || 1;
      counts.set(name, (counts.get(name) ?? 0) + qty);
    }
  }
  return Array.from(counts.entries()).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1] || a[0].localeCompare(b[0], "fa")).slice(0, safeLimit).map(([name]) => name);
}
async function logAdminAction(db, input) {
  try {
    const result = await db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO admin_logs (admin_id, admin_name, action, target_type, target_id, details, ip)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    ).bind(
      input.adminId,
      String(input.adminName).slice(0, 80),
      String(input.action).slice(0, 60),
      input.targetType ? String(input.targetType).slice(0, 40) : null,
      input.targetId === null || input.targetId === void 0 ? null : String(input.targetId).slice(0, 40),
      input.details ? String(input.details).slice(0, 500) : null,
      input.ip ? String(input.ip).slice(0, 60) : null
    ).run();
    return Number(result.meta?.last_row_id) || null;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("logAdminAction failed:", error);
    return null;
  }
}
async function listAdminLogs(db, params = {}) {
  const page = Math.max(1, Math.floor(params.page ?? 1));
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(10, Math.floor(params.pageSize ?? 50)));
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  const where = ["1 = 1"];
  const binds = [];
  const action = String(params.action ?? "").trim().slice(0, 60);
  if (action) {
    where.push("action = ?");
    binds.push(action);
  }
  const q = String(params.q ?? "").trim().slice(0, 60);
  if (q) {
    where.push("(admin_name LIKE ? OR details LIKE ? OR target_id LIKE ?)");
    binds.push(`%${q}%`, `%${q}%`, `%${q}%`);
  }
  const whereSql = where.join(" AND ");
  const totalRow = await db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM admin_logs WHERE ${whereSql}`).bind(...binds).first();
  const { results } = await db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM admin_logs WHERE ${whereSql} ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`).bind(...binds, pageSize, offset).all();
  return { items: results ?? [], total: totalRow?.c ?? 0, page, pageSize };
}
async function exportAdminLogs(db, limit = 5e3) {
  const safeLimit = Math.min(2e4, Math.max(1, Math.floor(limit)));
  const { results } = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM admin_logs ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?").bind(safeLimit).all();
  return results ?? [];
}
function zoneModifier(timeZone) {
  const now = /* @__PURE__ */ new Date();
  const p = zonedParts(now, timeZone);
  const asUTC = Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day, p.hour, p.minute);
  const offsetMinutes = Math.round((asUTC - Math.floor(now.getTime() / 6e4) * 6e4) / 6e4);
  const sign3 = offsetMinutes >= 0 ? "+" : "-";
  return `${sign3}${Math.abs(offsetMinutes)} minutes`;
}
async function getReportsData(db, settings, timeZone = DEFAULT_TIMEZONE) {
  const month = jalaliMonthRangeAt(0, timeZone);
  const prevMonth = jalaliMonthRangeAt(1, timeZone);
  const r90 = zonedDayRange(timeZone, 89);
  const today = zonedDayRange(timeZone, 0);
  const day30 = zonedDayRange(timeZone, 29);
  const TZ_MODIFIER = zoneModifier(timeZone);
  const [
    thisRow,
    prevRow,
    thisCustomers,
    prevCustomers,
    staffRows,
    retentionRow,
    newThisRow,
    newPrevRow,
    itemRows,
    totalsRow,
    pointsRow,
    paymentRow,
    voidRow,
    dailyRows,
    todayRow
  ] = await Promise.all([
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s, COUNT(*) AS c
         FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?`
    ).bind(month.start, month.end).first(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s, COUNT(*) AS c
         FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?`
    ).bind(prevMonth.start, prevMonth.end).first(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) AS c FROM invoices
         WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?`
    ).bind(month.start, month.end).first(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) AS c FROM invoices
         WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?`
    ).bind(prevMonth.start, prevMonth.end).first(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT i.registered_by AS staff_id,
                COALESCE(u.name, '\u062B\u0628\u062A \u0633\u06CC\u0633\u062A\u0645\u06CC') AS staff_name,
                COUNT(*) AS invoices,
                COALESCE(SUM(i.amount), 0) AS amount,
                COALESCE(SUM(i.points_earned), 0) AS points
         FROM invoices i LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = i.registered_by
         WHERE i.voided_at IS NULL AND i.created_at >= ? AND i.created_at < ?
         GROUP BY i.registered_by
         ORDER BY amount DESC`
    ).bind(month.start, month.end).all(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COUNT(*) AS buyers,
                SUM(CASE WHEN visits >= 2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS repeat_buyers
         FROM (
           SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) AS visits
           FROM invoices
           WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ?
           GROUP BY user_id
         )`
    ).bind(r90.start).first(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users
         WHERE role = 'customer' AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?`
    ).bind(month.start, month.end).first(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users
         WHERE role = 'customer' AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?`
    ).bind(prevMonth.start, prevMonth.end).first(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT items FROM invoices
         WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ?
         ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 2000`
    ).bind(r90.start).all(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COUNT(*) AS all_customers,
                SUM(CASE WHEN last_visit_date IS NOT NULL
                          AND last_visit_date >= datetime('now', '-30 days') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS active
         FROM users WHERE role = 'customer'`
    ).first(),
    db.prepare(`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(points), 0) AS p FROM users WHERE role = 'customer'`).first(),
    // تفکیک فروش بر اساس روش پرداخت (این ماه)
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(paid_cash), 0) AS cash,
                COALESCE(SUM(paid_card), 0) AS card,
                COALESCE(SUM(paid_online), 0) AS online,
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN payment_method IS NULL THEN amount ELSE 0 END), 0) AS unknown,
                COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS total
         FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?`
    ).bind(month.start, month.end).first(),
    // فاکتورهای باطل‌شدهٔ این ماه
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COUNT(*) AS c, COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s
         FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NOT NULL AND voided_at >= ? AND voided_at < ?`
    ).bind(month.start, month.end).first(),
    // فروش روزانه ۳۰ روز اخیر، یک کوئری با گروه‌بندی روی تاریخ محلی
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT date(created_at, ?) AS d,
                COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s,
                COUNT(*) AS c
         FROM invoices
         WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ?
         GROUP BY d ORDER BY d ASC`
    ).bind(TZ_MODIFIER, day30.start).all(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s, COUNT(*) AS c
         FROM invoices WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ? AND created_at < ?`
    ).bind(today.start, today.end).first()
  ]);
  const monthRanges = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((offset) => jalaliMonthRangeAt(offset, timeZone));
  const sixMonthStart = monthRanges[monthRanges.length - 1].start;
  const monthDaysRes = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT date(created_at, ?) AS d,
              COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s,
              COUNT(*) AS c
       FROM invoices
       WHERE voided_at IS NULL AND created_at >= ?
       GROUP BY d`
  ).bind(TZ_MODIFIER, sixMonthStart).all();
  const localDateOf = (range) => {
    const p = zonedParts(new Date(range.startMs), timeZone);
    const mm = String(p.month).padStart(2, "0");
    const dd = String(p.day).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${p.year}-${mm}-${dd}`;
  };
  const monthRows = monthRanges.map((range, index) => {
    const from = localDateOf(range);
    const to = index === 0 ? "9999-12-31" : localDateOf(monthRanges[index - 1]);
    const inRange = (monthDaysRes.results ?? []).filter((row) => row.d >= from && row.d < to);
    return {
      s: inRange.reduce((sum, row) => sum + (row.s ?? 0), 0),
      c: inRange.reduce((sum, row) => sum + (row.c ?? 0), 0)
    };
  });
  const itemMap = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const row of itemRows.results ?? []) {
    const parsed = safeJsonParse(
      row.items,
      []
    );
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) continue;
    for (const item of parsed) {
      const name = String(item?.name ?? "").trim().slice(0, 60);
      if (!name) continue;
      const qty = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item?.qty) || 0));
      const price = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item?.price) || 0));
      const current = itemMap.get(name) ?? { name, qty: 0, amount: 0 };
      current.qty += qty;
      current.amount += qty * price;
      itemMap.set(name, current);
    }
  }
  const topItems = Array.from(itemMap.values()).sort((a, b) => b.qty - a.qty || b.amount - a.amount).slice(0, 10);
  const thisSales = thisRow?.s ?? 0;
  const prevSales = prevRow?.s ?? 0;
  const thisCount = thisRow?.c ?? 0;
  const prevCount = prevRow?.c ?? 0;
  const pct = (current, previous) => {
    if (!previous) return current > 0 ? 100 : null;
    return Math.round((current - previous) / previous * 1e3) / 10;
  };
  const buyers = retentionRow?.buyers ?? 0;
  const repeatBuyers = retentionRow?.repeat_buyers ?? 0;
  const sleepingRow = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users u
       WHERE u.role = 'customer' AND u.last_visit_date IS NOT NULL
         AND julianday('now') - julianday(u.last_visit_date) >
             (CASE WHEN (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoices i
                          WHERE i.user_id = u.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL) >= ?
                     AND u.avg_visit_interval > 0
                   THEN u.avg_visit_interval ELSE ? END) + ?`
  ).bind(settings.churn_min_visits, CHURN_DEFAULT_INTERVAL, settings.churn_grace_days).first();
  const dayMap = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const row of dailyRows.results ?? []) {
    dayMap.set(String(row.d), { s: row.s ?? 0, c: row.c ?? 0 });
  }
  const daily = zonedDaySeries(timeZone, 30).map((day) => {
    const hit = dayMap.get(day.date);
    return {
      date: day.date,
      label: formatJalali(day.date, false),
      amount: hit?.s ?? 0,
      invoices: hit?.c ?? 0
    };
  });
  const weekly = [];
  const weekCount = Math.ceil(daily.length / 7);
  for (let i = 0; i < weekCount; i += 1) {
    const end = daily.length - i * 7;
    const chunk = daily.slice(Math.max(0, end - 7), end);
    if (chunk.length === 0) continue;
    weekly.push({
      label: `${chunk[0].label} \u062A\u0627 ${chunk[chunk.length - 1].label}`,
      amount: chunk.reduce((sum, day) => sum + day.amount, 0),
      invoices: chunk.reduce((sum, day) => sum + day.invoices, 0)
    });
  }
  weekly.reverse();
  return {
    paymentSplit: {
      cash: paymentRow?.cash ?? 0,
      card: paymentRow?.card ?? 0,
      online: paymentRow?.online ?? 0,
      unknown: paymentRow?.unknown ?? 0,
      total: paymentRow?.total ?? 0
    },
    voids: { count: voidRow?.c ?? 0, amount: voidRow?.s ?? 0 },
    daily,
    weekly,
    todayTotals: { sales: todayRow?.s ?? 0, invoices: todayRow?.c ?? 0 },
    thisMonth: {
      label: month.label,
      sales: thisSales,
      invoices: thisCount,
      customers: thisCustomers?.c ?? 0,
      avgBasket: thisCount > 0 ? Math.round(thisSales / thisCount) : 0
    },
    lastMonth: {
      label: prevMonth.label,
      sales: prevSales,
      invoices: prevCount,
      customers: prevCustomers?.c ?? 0,
      avgBasket: prevCount > 0 ? Math.round(prevSales / prevCount) : 0
    },
    growth: { salesPct: pct(thisSales, prevSales), invoicesPct: pct(thisCount, prevCount) },
    staff: staffRows.results ?? [],
    retention: {
      buyers,
      repeatBuyers,
      rate: buyers > 0 ? Math.round(repeatBuyers / buyers * 1e3) / 10 : null,
      newThisMonth: newThisRow?.c ?? 0,
      newLastMonth: newPrevRow?.c ?? 0
    },
    topItems,
    months: monthRanges.map((range, index) => ({
      label: range.label,
      amount: monthRows[index]?.s ?? 0,
      invoices: monthRows[index]?.c ?? 0
    })),
    totals: {
      customers: totalsRow?.all_customers ?? 0,
      active: totalsRow?.active ?? 0,
      sleeping: sleepingRow?.c ?? 0,
      points: pointsRow?.p ?? 0
    }
  };
}

      card: "\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A\u200C\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646",
      online: "\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646",
      mixed: "\u062A\u0631\u06A9\u06CC\u0628\u06CC",
      unknown: "\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647"
    };
  }
});

// src/lib/otp.ts
var otp_exports = {};
__export(otp_exports, {
  OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS: () => OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS,
  OTP_TTL_SECONDS: () => OTP_TTL_SECONDS,
  dbRateLimit: () => dbRateLimit,
  generateOtpCode: () => generateOtpCode,
  hashOtp: () => hashOtp,
  pruneRateLimits: () => pruneRateLimits,
  requestOtpCode: () => requestOtpCode,
  verifyOtpCode: () => verifyOtpCode
});
async function dbRateLimit(db, key, limit, windowSeconds) {
  try {
    const row = await db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO rate_limits (key, count, reset_at)
         VALUES (?, 1, datetime('now', ?))
         ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET
           count = CASE WHEN reset_at <= datetime('now') THEN 1 ELSE count + 1 END,
           reset_at = CASE WHEN reset_at <= datetime('now') THEN datetime('now', ?) ELSE reset_at END
         RETURNING count, reset_at`
    ).bind(key, `+${Math.max(1, Math.floor(windowSeconds))} seconds`, `+${Math.max(1, Math.floor(windowSeconds))} seconds`).first();
    if (!row) return null;
    const resetMs = Date.parse(`${String(row.reset_at).replace(" ", "T")}Z`);
    const retryAfter = Number.isFinite(resetMs) ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil((resetMs - Date.now()) / 1e3)) : windowSeconds;
    return { allowed: row.count <= limit, retryAfter };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("dbRateLimit failed:", error);
    return null;
  }
}
async function pruneRateLimits(db) {
  try {
    await db.prepare("DELETE FROM rate_limits WHERE reset_at < datetime('now', '-1 day')").run();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("pruneRateLimits failed:", error);
  }
}
function generateOtpCode() {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(4);
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  const value = (bytes[0] << 24 | bytes[1] << 16 | bytes[2] << 8 | bytes[3]) >>> 0;
  return String(value % 1e6).padStart(6, "0");
}
async function hashOtp(code, phone, secret) {
  const data = new TextEncoder().encode(`otp:${phone}:${code}:${secret}`);
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}
function safeEqual(a, b) {
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let diff = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i += 1) diff |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
  return diff === 0;
}
async function requestOtpCode(env, settings, phone, clientIp) {
  if (settings.otp_login_enabled !== 1) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9\u06CC \u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0628\u0627 \u067E\u0631\u0633\u0646\u0644 \u06A9\u0627\u0641\u0647 \u062A\u0645\u0627\u0633 \u0628\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062F.", code: "OTP_DISABLED" };
  }
  const perIp = await dbRateLimit(env.DB, `otp:ip:${clientIp}`, 15, 3600);
  if (perIp && !perIp.allowed) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "\u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627 \u0627\u0632 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0632\u06CC\u0627\u062F \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u06A9\u0645\u06CC \u0628\u0639\u062F \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.",
      code: "RATE_LIMITED",
      retryAfter: perIp.retryAfter
    };
  }
  const user = await findUserByPhone(env.DB, phone);
  if (!user || user.role !== "customer" || user.is_active !== 1) {
    return { ok: true, ttl: 0, sent: false };
  }
  const perPhone = await dbRateLimit(env.DB, `otp:phone:${phone}`, 3, 600);
  if (perPhone && !perPhone.allowed) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "\u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0627\u062E\u06CC\u0631\u0627\u064B \u0686\u0646\u062F \u0628\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0686\u0646\u062F \u062F\u0642\u06CC\u0642\u0647 \u0628\u0639\u062F \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.",
      code: "RATE_LIMITED",
      retryAfter: perPhone.retryAfter
    };
  }
  const perHour = await dbRateLimit(env.DB, `otp:hour:${phone}`, 8, 3600);
  if (perHour && !perHour.allowed) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "\u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0632\u06CC\u0627\u062F \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u06A9\u0645\u06CC \u0628\u0639\u062F \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.",
      code: "RATE_LIMITED",
      retryAfter: perHour.retryAfter
    };
  }
  await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE otp_codes SET used_at = ? WHERE phone = ? AND used_at IS NULL").bind(utcStamp(Date.now()), phone).run();
  const code = generateOtpCode();
  const secret = env.JWT_SECRET ?? "otp-secret";
  const codeHash = await hashOtp(code, phone, secret);
  const expiresAt = utcStamp(Date.now() + OTP_TTL_SECONDS * 1e3);
  await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO otp_codes (phone, code_hash, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)`
  ).bind(phone, codeHash, expiresAt).run();
  const delivery = await sendSms(env, settings, {
    expiresAt: Date.parse(expiresAt.replace(" ", "T") + "Z"),
    userId: user.id,
    phone,
    type: "otp",
    message: `\u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0628\u0647 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 ${settings.cafe_name}: ${code}
\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u062F \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u06F2 \u062F\u0642\u06CC\u0642\u0647 \u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u062F\u0627\u0631\u062F\u061B \u0622\u0646 \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0627 \u06A9\u0633\u06CC \u0628\u0647 \u0627\u0634\u062A\u0631\u0627\u06A9 \u0646\u06AF\u0630\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062F.`
  });
  if (delivery.status === "failed" || delivery.status === "skipped") {
    await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE otp_codes SET used_at = ? WHERE phone = ? AND code_hash = ? AND used_at IS NULL").bind(utcStamp(Date.now()), phone, codeHash).run();
    return { ok: false, code: "OTP_SEND_FAILED", error: "\u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0645\u0645\u06A9\u0646 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u061B \u0628\u0627 \u067E\u0631\u0633\u0646\u0644 \u06A9\u0627\u0641\u0647 \u062A\u0645\u0627\u0633 \u0628\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062F." };
  }
  return { ok: true, ttl: OTP_TTL_SECONDS, sent: delivery.status === "sent" && settings.sms_provider !== "console", queued: delivery.status === "pending" };
}
async function verifyOtpCode(env, phone, code) {
  if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(code)) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u06F6 \u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.", code: "INVALID_CODE" };
  }
  const row = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT id, code_hash, expires_at, attempts
     FROM otp_codes
     WHERE phone = ? AND used_at IS NULL
     ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1`
  ).bind(phone).first();
  if (!row) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "\u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631\u06CC \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0635\u0627\u062F\u0631 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.",
      code: "NO_CODE"
    };
  }
  if (row.attempts >= OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "\u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u0634\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0647 \u0632\u06CC\u0627\u062F \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u06CC\u06A9 \u06A9\u062F \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062F.",
      code: "TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS"
    };
  }
  const expiresMs = Date.parse(`${String(row.expires_at).replace(" ", "T")}Z`);
  if (!Number.isFinite(expiresMs) || expiresMs < Date.now()) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0646\u0642\u0636\u06CC \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u06A9\u062F \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062F.", code: "EXPIRED" };
  }
  await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE otp_codes SET attempts = attempts + 1 WHERE id = ?").bind(row.id).run();
  const secret = env.JWT_SECRET ?? "otp-secret";
  const expected = await hashOtp(code, phone, secret);
  if (!safeEqual(expected, row.code_hash)) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0635\u062D\u06CC\u062D \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.", code: "WRONG_CODE" };
  }
  const user = await findUserByPhone(env.DB, phone);
  if (!user || user.role !== "customer" || user.is_active !== 1) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u062D\u0633\u0627\u0628 \u062F\u0631 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u0631\u0633 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.", code: "ACCOUNT_DISABLED" };
  }
  await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE otp_codes SET used_at = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(utcStamp(Date.now()), row.id).run();
  return { ok: true, userId: user.id };
}
var OTP_TTL_SECONDS, OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS;
var init_otp = __esm({
  "src/lib/otp.ts"() {
    "use strict";
    init_db();
    init_sms();
    init_utils();
    OTP_TTL_SECONDS = 120;
    OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5;
  }
});

  const path = ICONS[name] ?? ICONS.sparkles;
  return `<svg class="${className}" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">${path}</svg>`;
}
