var AlgorithmTypes = /* @__PURE__ */ (function(AlgorithmTypes2) {
  AlgorithmTypes2["HS256"] = "HS256";
  AlgorithmTypes2["HS384"] = "HS384";
  AlgorithmTypes2["HS512"] = "HS512";
  AlgorithmTypes2["RS256"] = "RS256";
  AlgorithmTypes2["RS384"] = "RS384";
  AlgorithmTypes2["RS512"] = "RS512";
  AlgorithmTypes2["PS256"] = "PS256";
  AlgorithmTypes2["PS384"] = "PS384";
  AlgorithmTypes2["PS512"] = "PS512";
  AlgorithmTypes2["ES256"] = "ES256";
  AlgorithmTypes2["ES384"] = "ES384";
  AlgorithmTypes2["ES512"] = "ES512";
  AlgorithmTypes2["EdDSA"] = "EdDSA";
  return AlgorithmTypes2;
})({});

// node_modules/hono/dist/utils/cookie.js
var validCookieNameRegEx = /^[\w!#$%&'*.^`|~+-]+$/;
var relaxedCookieNameRegEx = /^[!#-:<>-[\]-~]+$/;
var validCookieValueRegEx = /^[ !#-:<-[\]-~]*$/;
var trimCookieWhitespace = (value) => {
  let start = 0;
  let end = value.length;
  while (start < end) {
    const charCode = value.charCodeAt(start);
    if (charCode !== 32 && charCode !== 9) break;
    start++;
  }
  while (end > start) {
    const charCode = value.charCodeAt(end - 1);
    if (charCode !== 32 && charCode !== 9) break;
    end--;
  }
  return start === 0 && end === value.length ? value : value.slice(start, end);
};
var parse = (cookie, name) => {
  if (name && cookie.indexOf(name) === -1) return {};
  const pairs = cookie.split(";");
  const parsedCookie = /* @__PURE__ */ Object.create(null);
  for (const pairStr of pairs) {
    const valueStartPos = pairStr.indexOf("=");
    if (valueStartPos === -1) continue;
    const cookieName = trimCookieWhitespace(pairStr.substring(0, valueStartPos));
    if (name && name !== cookieName || !relaxedCookieNameRegEx.test(cookieName) || cookieName in parsedCookie) continue;
    let cookieValue = trimCookieWhitespace(pairStr.substring(valueStartPos + 1));
    if (cookieValue.startsWith('"') && cookieValue.endsWith('"')) cookieValue = cookieValue.slice(1, -1);
    if (validCookieValueRegEx.test(cookieValue)) {
      parsedCookie[cookieName] = tryDecodeURIComponent(cookieValue);
      if (name) break;
    }
  }
  return parsedCookie;
};
var _serialize = (name, value, opt = {}) => {
  if (!validCookieNameRegEx.test(name)) throw new Error("Invalid cookie name");
  let cookie = `${name}=${value}`;
  if (name.startsWith("__Secure-") && !opt.secure) throw new Error("__Secure- Cookie must have Secure attributes");
  if (name.startsWith("__Host-")) {
    if (!opt.secure) throw new Error("__Host- Cookie must have Secure attributes");
    if (opt.path !== "/") throw new Error('__Host- Cookie must have Path attributes with "/"');
    if (opt.domain) throw new Error("__Host- Cookie must not have Domain attributes");
  }
  for (const key of [
    "domain",
    "path",
    "sameSite",
    "priority"
  ]) if (opt[key] && /[;\r\n]/.test(opt[key])) throw new Error(`${key} must not contain ";", "\\r", or "\\n"`);
  if (opt && typeof opt.maxAge === "number" && opt.maxAge >= 0) {
    if (opt.maxAge > 3456e4) throw new Error("Cookies Max-Age SHOULD NOT be greater than 400 days (34560000 seconds) in duration.");
    cookie += `; Max-Age=${opt.maxAge | 0}`;
  }
  if (opt.domain && opt.prefix !== "host") cookie += `; Domain=${opt.domain}`;
  if (opt.path) cookie += `; Path=${opt.path}`;
  if (opt.expires) {
    if (opt.expires.getTime() - Date.now() > 3456e7) throw new Error("Cookies Expires SHOULD NOT be greater than 400 days (34560000 seconds) in the future.");
    cookie += `; Expires=${opt.expires.toUTCString()}`;
  }
  if (opt.httpOnly) cookie += "; HttpOnly";
  if (opt.secure) cookie += "; Secure";
  if (opt.sameSite) cookie += `; SameSite=${opt.sameSite.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + opt.sameSite.slice(1)}`;
  if (opt.priority) cookie += `; Priority=${opt.priority.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + opt.priority.slice(1)}`;
  if (opt.partitioned) {
    if (!opt.secure) throw new Error("Partitioned Cookie must have Secure attributes");
    cookie += "; Partitioned";
  }
  return cookie;
};
var serialize = (name, value, opt) => {
  value = encodeURIComponent(value);
  return _serialize(name, value, opt);
};

// node_modules/hono/dist/helper/cookie/index.js
var getCookie = (c, key, prefix) => {
  const cookie = c.req.raw.headers.get("Cookie");
  if (typeof key === "string") {
    if (!cookie) return;
    let finalKey = key;
    if (prefix === "secure") finalKey = "__Secure-" + key;
    else if (prefix === "host") finalKey = "__Host-" + key;
    return parse(cookie, finalKey)[finalKey];
  }
  if (!cookie) return {};
  return parse(cookie);
};
var generateCookie = (name, value, opt) => {
  let cookie;
  if (opt?.prefix === "secure") cookie = serialize("__Secure-" + name, value, {
    path: "/",
    ...opt,
    secure: true
  });
  else if (opt?.prefix === "host") cookie = serialize("__Host-" + name, value, {
    ...opt,
    path: "/",
    secure: true,
    domain: void 0
  });
  else cookie = serialize(name, value, {
    path: "/",
    ...opt
  });
  return cookie;
};
var setCookie = (c, name, value, opt) => {
  const cookie = generateCookie(name, value, opt);
  c.header("Set-Cookie", cookie, { append: true });
};
var deleteCookie = (c, name, opt) => {
  const deletedCookie = getCookie(c, name, opt?.prefix);
  setCookie(c, name, "", {
    ...opt,
    maxAge: 0
  });
  return deletedCookie;
};

// node_modules/hono/dist/utils/encode.js
var decodeBase64Url = (str2) => {
  return decodeBase64(str2.replace(/_|-/g, (m) => ({
    _: "/",
    "-": "+"
  })[m] ?? m));
};
var encodeBase64Url = (buf) => encodeBase64(buf).replace(/\/|\+/g, (m) => ({
  "/": "_",
  "+": "-"
})[m] ?? m);
var encodeBase64 = (buf) => {
  let binary = "";
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buf);
  for (let i = 0, len = bytes.length; i < len; i++) binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
  return btoa(binary);
};
var decodeBase64 = (str2) => {
  const binary = atob(str2);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(new ArrayBuffer(binary.length));
  const half = binary.length / 2;
  for (let i = 0, j = binary.length - 1; i <= half; i++, j--) {
    bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    bytes[j] = binary.charCodeAt(j);
  }
  return bytes;
};

// node_modules/hono/dist/utils/jwt/types.js
var JwtAlgorithmNotImplemented = class extends Error {
  constructor(alg) {
    super(`${alg} is not an implemented algorithm`);
    this.name = "JwtAlgorithmNotImplemented";
  }
};
var JwtAlgorithmRequired = class extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super('JWT verification requires "alg" option to be specified');
    this.name = "JwtAlgorithmRequired";
  }
};
var JwtAlgorithmMismatch = class extends Error {
  constructor(expected, actual) {
    super(`JWT algorithm mismatch: expected "${expected}", got "${actual}"`);
    this.name = "JwtAlgorithmMismatch";
  }
};
var JwtTokenInvalid = class extends Error {
  constructor(token) {
    super(`invalid JWT token: ${token}`);
    this.name = "JwtTokenInvalid";
  }
};
var JwtTokenNotBefore = class extends Error {
  constructor(token) {
    super(`token (${token}) is being used before it's valid`);
    this.name = "JwtTokenNotBefore";
  }
};
var JwtTokenExpired = class extends Error {
  constructor(token) {
    super(`token (${token}) expired`);
    this.name = "JwtTokenExpired";
  }
};
var JwtTokenIssuedAt = class extends Error {
  constructor(currentTimestamp, iat) {
    super(`Invalid "iat" claim, must be a valid number lower than "${currentTimestamp}" (iat: "${iat}")`);
    this.name = "JwtTokenIssuedAt";
  }
};
var JwtTokenIssuer = class extends Error {
  constructor(expected, iss) {
    super(`expected issuer "${expected}", got ${iss ? `"${iss}"` : "none"} `);
    this.name = "JwtTokenIssuer";
  }
};
var JwtHeaderInvalid = class extends Error {
  constructor(header) {
    super(`jwt header is invalid: ${JSON.stringify(header)}`);
    this.name = "JwtHeaderInvalid";
  }
};
var JwtHeaderRequiresKid = class extends Error {
  constructor(header) {
    super(`required "kid" in jwt header: ${JSON.stringify(header)}`);
    this.name = "JwtHeaderRequiresKid";
  }
};
var JwtSymmetricAlgorithmNotAllowed = class extends Error {
  constructor(alg) {
    super(`symmetric algorithm "${alg}" is not allowed for JWK verification`);
    this.name = "JwtSymmetricAlgorithmNotAllowed";
  }
};
var JwtAlgorithmNotAllowed = class extends Error {
  constructor(alg, allowedAlgorithms) {
    super(`algorithm "${alg}" is not in the allowed list: [${allowedAlgorithms.join(", ")}]`);
    this.name = "JwtAlgorithmNotAllowed";
  }
};
var JwtTokenSignatureMismatched = class extends Error {
  constructor(token) {
    super(`token(${token}) signature mismatched`);
    this.name = "JwtTokenSignatureMismatched";
  }
};
var JwtPayloadRequiresAud = class extends Error {
  constructor(payload) {
    super(`required "aud" in jwt payload: ${JSON.stringify(payload)}`);
    this.name = "JwtPayloadRequiresAud";
  }
};
var JwtTokenAudience = class extends Error {
  constructor(expected, aud) {
    super(`expected audience "${Array.isArray(expected) ? expected.join(", ") : expected}", got "${aud}"`);
    this.name = "JwtTokenAudience";
  }
};

// node_modules/hono/dist/utils/jwt/utf8.js
var utf8Encoder = new TextEncoder();
var utf8Decoder = new TextDecoder();

// node_modules/hono/dist/helper/adapter/index.js
var knownUserAgents = {
  deno: "Deno",
  bun: "Bun",
  workerd: "Cloudflare-Workers",
  node: "Node.js"
};
var getRuntimeKey = () => {
  const global = globalThis;
  if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && typeof navigator.userAgent === "string") {
    for (const [runtimeKey, userAgent] of Object.entries(knownUserAgents)) if (checkUserAgentEquals(userAgent)) return runtimeKey;
  }
  if (typeof global?.EdgeRuntime === "string") return "edge-light";
  if (global?.fastly !== void 0) return "fastly";
  if (global?.process?.release?.name === "node") return "node";
  return "other";
};
var checkUserAgentEquals = (platform) => {
  return navigator.userAgent.startsWith(platform);
};

// node_modules/hono/dist/utils/jwt/jws.js
async function signing(privateKey, alg, data) {
  const algorithm = getKeyAlgorithm(alg);
  const cryptoKey = await importPrivateKey(privateKey, algorithm);
  return await crypto.subtle.sign(algorithm, cryptoKey, data);
}
async function verifying(publicKey, alg, signature, data) {
  const algorithm = getKeyAlgorithm(alg);
  const cryptoKey = await importPublicKey(publicKey, algorithm);
  return await crypto.subtle.verify(algorithm, cryptoKey, signature, data);
}
function pemToBinary(pem) {
  return decodeBase64(pem.replace(/-+(BEGIN|END).*?-+/g, "").replace(/\s/g, ""));
}
async function importPrivateKey(key, alg) {
  if (!crypto.subtle || !crypto.subtle.importKey) throw new Error("`crypto.subtle.importKey` is undefined. JWT auth middleware requires it.");
  if (isCryptoKey(key)) {
    if (key.type !== "private" && key.type !== "secret") throw new Error(`unexpected key type: CryptoKey.type is ${key.type}, expected private or secret`);
    return key;
  }
  const usages = ["sign"];
  if (typeof key === "object") return await crypto.subtle.importKey("jwk", key, alg, false, usages);
  if (key.includes("PRIVATE")) return await crypto.subtle.importKey("pkcs8", pemToBinary(key), alg, false, usages);
  return await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", utf8Encoder.encode(key), alg, false, usages);
}
async function importPublicKey(key, alg) {
  if (!crypto.subtle || !crypto.subtle.importKey) throw new Error("`crypto.subtle.importKey` is undefined. JWT auth middleware requires it.");
  if (isCryptoKey(key)) {
    if (key.type === "public" || key.type === "secret") return key;
    key = await exportPublicJwkFrom(key);
  }
  if (typeof key === "string" && key.includes("PRIVATE")) key = await exportPublicJwkFrom(await crypto.subtle.importKey("pkcs8", pemToBinary(key), alg, true, ["sign"]));
  const usages = ["verify"];
  if (typeof key === "object") return await crypto.subtle.importKey("jwk", key, alg, false, usages);
  if (key.includes("PUBLIC")) return await crypto.subtle.importKey("spki", pemToBinary(key), alg, false, usages);
  return await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", utf8Encoder.encode(key), alg, false, usages);
}
async function exportPublicJwkFrom(privateKey) {
  if (privateKey.type !== "private") throw new Error(`unexpected key type: ${privateKey.type}`);
  if (!privateKey.extractable) throw new Error("unexpected private key is unextractable");
  const jwk = await crypto.subtle.exportKey("jwk", privateKey);
  const { kty } = jwk;
  const { alg, e, n } = jwk;
  const { crv, x, y } = jwk;
  return {
    kty,
    alg,
    e,
    n,
    crv,
    x,
    y,
    key_ops: ["verify"]
  };
}
function getKeyAlgorithm(name) {
  switch (name) {
    case "HS256":
      return {
        name: "HMAC",
        hash: { name: "SHA-256" }
      };
    case "HS384":
      return {
        name: "HMAC",
        hash: { name: "SHA-384" }
      };
    case "HS512":
      return {
        name: "HMAC",
        hash: { name: "SHA-512" }
      };
    case "RS256":
      return {
        name: "RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5",
        hash: { name: "SHA-256" }
      };
    case "RS384":
      return {
        name: "RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5",
        hash: { name: "SHA-384" }
      };
    case "RS512":
      return {
        name: "RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5",
        hash: { name: "SHA-512" }
      };
    case "PS256":
      return {
        name: "RSA-PSS",
        hash: { name: "SHA-256" },
        saltLength: 32
      };
    case "PS384":
      return {
        name: "RSA-PSS",
        hash: { name: "SHA-384" },
        saltLength: 48
      };
    case "PS512":
      return {
        name: "RSA-PSS",
        hash: { name: "SHA-512" },
        saltLength: 64
      };
    case "ES256":
      return {
        name: "ECDSA",
        hash: { name: "SHA-256" },
        namedCurve: "P-256"
      };
    case "ES384":
      return {
        name: "ECDSA",
        hash: { name: "SHA-384" },
        namedCurve: "P-384"
      };
    case "ES512":
      return {
        name: "ECDSA",
        hash: { name: "SHA-512" },
        namedCurve: "P-521"
      };
    case "EdDSA":
      return {
        name: "Ed25519",
        namedCurve: "Ed25519"
      };
    default:
      throw new JwtAlgorithmNotImplemented(name);
  }
}
function isCryptoKey(key) {
  if (getRuntimeKey() === "node" && !!crypto.webcrypto) return key instanceof crypto.webcrypto.CryptoKey;
  return key instanceof CryptoKey;
}

// node_modules/hono/dist/utils/jwt/jwt.js
var encodeJwtPart = (part) => encodeBase64Url(utf8Encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(part)).buffer).replace(/=/g, "");
var encodeSignaturePart = (buf) => encodeBase64Url(buf).replace(/=/g, "");
var decodeJwtPart = (part) => JSON.parse(utf8Decoder.decode(decodeBase64Url(part)));
function isTokenHeader(obj) {
  if (typeof obj === "object" && obj !== null) {
    const objWithAlg = obj;
    return "alg" in objWithAlg && Object.values(AlgorithmTypes).includes(objWithAlg.alg) && (!("typ" in objWithAlg) || objWithAlg.typ === "JWT");
  }
  return false;
}
var sign = async (payload, privateKey, alg = "HS256") => {
  const encodedPayload = encodeJwtPart(payload);
  let encodedHeader;
  if (typeof privateKey === "object" && "alg" in privateKey) {
    alg = privateKey.alg;
    encodedHeader = encodeJwtPart({
      alg,
      typ: "JWT",
      kid: privateKey.kid
    });
  } else encodedHeader = encodeJwtPart({
    alg,
    typ: "JWT"
  });
  const partialToken = `${encodedHeader}.${encodedPayload}`;
  const signaturePart = await signing(privateKey, alg, utf8Encoder.encode(partialToken));
  return `${partialToken}.${encodeSignaturePart(signaturePart)}`;
};
var verify = async (token, publicKey, algOrOptions) => {
  if (!algOrOptions) throw new JwtAlgorithmRequired();
  const { alg, iss, nbf = true, exp = true, iat = true, aud } = typeof algOrOptions === "string" ? { alg: algOrOptions } : algOrOptions;
  if (!alg) throw new JwtAlgorithmRequired();
  const tokenParts = token.split(".");
  if (tokenParts.length !== 3) throw new JwtTokenInvalid(token);
  const { header, payload } = decode(token);
  if (!isTokenHeader(header)) throw new JwtHeaderInvalid(header);
  if (header.alg !== alg) throw new JwtAlgorithmMismatch(alg, header.alg);
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3);
  if (nbf && payload.nbf !== void 0) {
    if (typeof payload.nbf !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(payload.nbf) || payload.nbf > now) throw new JwtTokenNotBefore(token);
  }
  if (exp && payload.exp !== void 0) {
    if (typeof payload.exp !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(payload.exp) || payload.exp <= now) throw new JwtTokenExpired(token);
  }
  if (iat && payload.iat !== void 0) {
    if (typeof payload.iat !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(payload.iat) || now < payload.iat) throw new JwtTokenIssuedAt(now, payload.iat);
  }
  if (iss) {
    if (!payload.iss) throw new JwtTokenIssuer(iss, null);
    if (typeof iss === "string" && payload.iss !== iss) throw new JwtTokenIssuer(iss, payload.iss);
    if (iss instanceof RegExp && !iss.test(payload.iss)) throw new JwtTokenIssuer(iss, payload.iss);
  }
  if (aud) {
    if (!payload.aud) throw new JwtPayloadRequiresAud(payload);
    if (!(Array.isArray(payload.aud) ? payload.aud : [payload.aud]).some((payloadAud) => aud instanceof RegExp ? aud.test(payloadAud) : typeof aud === "string" ? payloadAud === aud : Array.isArray(aud) && aud.includes(payloadAud))) throw new JwtTokenAudience(aud, payload.aud);
  }
  const headerPayload = token.substring(0, token.lastIndexOf("."));
  let signature;
  try {
    signature = decodeBase64Url(tokenParts[2]);
  } catch {
    throw new JwtTokenInvalid(token);
  }
  if (!await verifying(publicKey, alg, signature, utf8Encoder.encode(headerPayload))) throw new JwtTokenSignatureMismatched(token);
  return payload;
};
var symmetricAlgorithms = [
  "HS256",
  "HS384",
  "HS512"
];
var verifyWithJwks = async (token, options, init) => {
  const verifyOpts = options.verification || {};
  const header = decodeHeader(token);
  if (!isTokenHeader(header)) throw new JwtHeaderInvalid(header);
  if (!header.kid) throw new JwtHeaderRequiresKid(header);
  if (symmetricAlgorithms.includes(header.alg)) throw new JwtSymmetricAlgorithmNotAllowed(header.alg);
  if (!options.allowedAlgorithms.includes(header.alg)) throw new JwtAlgorithmNotAllowed(header.alg, options.allowedAlgorithms);
  let verifyKeys = options.keys ? [...options.keys] : void 0;
  if (options.jwks_uri) {
    const response = await fetch(options.jwks_uri, init);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`failed to fetch JWKS from ${options.jwks_uri}`);
    const data = await response.json();
    if (!data.keys) throw new Error('invalid JWKS response. "keys" field is missing');
    if (!Array.isArray(data.keys)) throw new Error('invalid JWKS response. "keys" field is not an array');
    verifyKeys ??= [];
    verifyKeys.push(...data.keys);
  } else if (!verifyKeys) throw new Error('verifyWithJwks requires options for either "keys" or "jwks_uri" or both');
  const matchingKey = verifyKeys.find((key) => key.kid === header.kid);
  if (!matchingKey) throw new JwtTokenInvalid(token);
  if (matchingKey.alg && matchingKey.alg !== header.alg) throw new JwtAlgorithmMismatch(matchingKey.alg, header.alg);
  return await verify(token, matchingKey, {
    alg: header.alg,
    ...verifyOpts
  });
};
var decode = (token) => {
  const parts = token.split(".");
  if (parts.length !== 3) throw new JwtTokenInvalid(token);
  try {
    return {
      header: decodeJwtPart(parts[0]),
      payload: decodeJwtPart(parts[1])
    };
  } catch {
    throw new JwtTokenInvalid(token);
  }
};
var decodeHeader = (token) => {
  const parts = token.split(".");
  if (parts.length !== 3) throw new JwtTokenInvalid(token);
  try {
    return decodeJwtPart(parts[0]);
  } catch {
    throw new JwtTokenInvalid(token);
  }
};

// node_modules/hono/dist/utils/jwt/index.js
var Jwt = {
  sign,
  verify,
  decode,
  verifyWithJwks
};

// node_modules/hono/dist/middleware/jwt/jwt.js
var verifyWithJwks2 = Jwt.verifyWithJwks;
var verify2 = Jwt.verify;
var decode2 = Jwt.decode;
var sign2 = Jwt.sign;

// src/lib/schema.ts
init_db();
var SCHEMA_VERSION = "site-recovery-v4";
var readyCache = /* @__PURE__ */ new WeakMap();
var controlRows = [
  { table: "fallback_dispatch", key: "state:fallback_dispatch", condition: "id=1", sql: "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fallback_dispatch(id,until_ms) VALUES(1,0)" },
  { table: "fallback_channel_limits", key: "state:telegram", condition: "channel='telegram'", sql: "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fallback_channel_limits(channel,until_ms) VALUES('telegram',0)" },
  { table: "fallback_channel_limits", key: "state:bale", condition: "channel='bale'", sql: "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fallback_channel_limits(channel,until_ms) VALUES('bale',0)" }
];
function invalidateSchemaCache(db) {
  readyCache.delete(db);
}
async function schemaStatus(db, fresh = false) {
  if (!fresh && (readyCache.get(db) ?? 0) > Date.now()) return { ready: true, pending: [], blocked: [] };
  const names = SCHEMA_TABLES.map((t) => t.name);
  const indexes = SCHEMA_INDEXES.map((i) => i.name);
  const result = await db.prepare(`
    SELECT 'column' AS kind, m.name AS table_name, p.name AS name
    FROM sqlite_master m JOIN pragma_table_info(m.name) p
    WHERE m.type = 'table' AND m.name IN (${names.map(() => "?").join(",")})
    UNION ALL SELECT 'index' AS kind, tbl_name AS table_name, name
    FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'index' AND name IN (${indexes.map(() => "?").join(",")})
  `).bind(...names, ...indexes).all();
  const columns = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const foundIndexes = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  for (const row of result.results ?? []) {
    if (row.kind === "index") {
      foundIndexes.add(row.name);
      continue;
    }
    const table = row.table_name.toLowerCase();
    if (!columns.has(table)) columns.set(table, /* @__PURE__ */ new Set());
    columns.get(table).add(row.name.toLowerCase());
  }
  const pending = [];
  const tables = [...SCHEMA_TABLES].sort((a, b) => Number(b.name === "admin_logs") - Number(a.name === "admin_logs"));
  for (const table of tables) {
    if (!columns.has(table.name)) {
      pending.push({ key: "table:" + table.name, sql: table.sql, blocked: table.name === "users" || table.name === "settings" });
      continue;
    }
    for (const col of table.columns) {
      if (columns.get(table.name).has(col.name)) continue;
      const definition = col.definition.replace(/\s+UNIQUE\b/i, "");
      const blocked = /PRIMARY KEY|DEFAULT\s*\(|REFERENCES/i.test(definition) || /NOT NULL/i.test(definition) && !/DEFAULT\b/i.test(definition);
      pending.push({
        key: "column:" + table.name + "." + col.name,
        sql: `ALTER TABLE ${table.name} ADD COLUMN ${col.name} ${definition}`,
        blocked,
        baseline: table.name === "users" && col.name === "lifetime_points"
      });
    }
  }
  const presentStates = controlRows.filter((row) => columns.get(row.table)?.has(row.table === "fallback_dispatch" ? "id" : "channel"));
  const foundStates = presentStates.length ? await db.prepare(presentStates.map(
    (row) => `SELECT '${row.key}' AS key, EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM ${row.table} WHERE ${row.condition}) AS present`
  ).join(" UNION ALL ")).all() : { results: [] };
  const activeStates = new Set((foundStates.results ?? []).filter((r) => r.present).map((r) => r.key));
  for (const row of controlRows) if (!activeStates.has(row.key)) pending.push({ key: row.key, sql: row.sql });
  for (const index of SCHEMA_INDEXES) if (!foundIndexes.has(index.name)) pending.push({ key: "index:" + index.name, sql: index.sql });
  const status = { ready: pending.length === 0, pending, blocked: pending.filter((s) => s.blocked).map((s) => s.key) };
  if (status.ready) readyCache.set(db, Date.now() + 3e4);
  else readyCache.delete(db);
  return status;
}
async function applySchemaSteps(db, actor) {
  invalidateSchemaCache(db);
  const status = await schemaStatus(db, true);
  if (status.blocked.length) throw new Error("SCHEMA_MANUAL_REVIEW_REQUIRED");
  for (const step of status.pending.slice(0, 4)) {
    try {
      if (step.baseline) {
        await db.batch([
          db.prepare(step.sql),
          db.prepare("UPDATE users SET lifetime_points = MAX(0, COALESCE(points, 0))")
        ]);
      } else await db.prepare(step.sql).run();
    } catch (error) {
      const name = step.key.startsWith("column:") ? step.key.split(".").pop() : "";
      if (!name || !new RegExp("duplicate column name:\\s*" + name + "\\b", "i").test(String(error))) throw error;
    }
    console.info("SCHEMA_ADDITIVE_UPGRADE", step.key);
    await logAdminAction(db, {
      adminId: actor.uid,
      adminName: actor.name,
      action: "database_upgrade",
      targetType: "schema",
      targetId: step.key,
      details: "\u0627\u0641\u0632\u0648\u062F\u0646 \u0633\u0627\u0632\u06AF\u0627\u0631 \u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0627\u0631: " + step.key
    });
  }
  invalidateSchemaCache(db);
  return schemaStatus(db, true);
}
async function upgradeBackup(db) {
  const schema = await db.prepare(`SELECT type, name, sql FROM sqlite_master
    WHERE type IN ('table','index','trigger','view') AND substr(name,1,7) <> 'sqlite_'
    AND substr(name,1,4) <> '_cf_' AND sql IS NOT NULL ORDER BY type, name`).all();
  const tables = (schema.results ?? []).filter((s) => s.type === "table");
  if (!tables.some((t) => t.name === "users") || !tables.some((t) => t.name === "settings")) throw new Error("BACKUP_CORE_TABLE_MISSING");
  if (tables.length > 24) throw new Error("BACKUP_TOO_LARGE");
  const data = {};
  let bytes = 0;
  for (const table of tables) {
    const identifier2 = '"' + table.name.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
    const rows = await db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM ${identifier2} LIMIT 20001`).all();
    if ((rows.results?.length ?? 0) > 2e4) throw new Error("BACKUP_TOO_LARGE");
    data[table.name] = rows.results ?? [];
    bytes += new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(data[table.name])).byteLength;
    if (bytes > 8 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error("BACKUP_TOO_LARGE");
  }
  const sequences = tables.some((t) => /AUTOINCREMENT/i.test(t.sql)) ? (await db.prepare("SELECT name, seq FROM sqlite_sequence").all()).results ?? [] : [];
  return JSON.stringify({
    app: "cafe-club",
    format: "private-upgrade-snapshot-v1",
    version: SCHEMA_VERSION,
    generated_at: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(),
    consistency: "application writes blocked while schema is incomplete; use D1 Time Travel for a point-in-time backup",
    schema: schema.results ?? [],
    sequences,
    data
  });
}
async function backupReceipt(secret, uid) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3);
  return sign2({ aud: "schema-backup", uid, version: SCHEMA_VERSION, iat: now, exp: now + 900 }, secret, "HS256");
}
async function verifyBackupReceipt(secret, uid, receipt) {
  if (typeof receipt !== "string" || receipt.length > 2048) return false;
  try {
    const p = await verify2(receipt, secret, { alg: "HS256", aud: "schema-backup" });
    return p.uid === uid && p.version === SCHEMA_VERSION;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
function isSchemaError(error) {
  return /no such (?:table|column)|has no column named|database schema has changed/i.test(String(error));
}

// node_modules/hono/dist/middleware/body-limit/index.js
var ERROR_MESSAGE = "Payload Too Large";
var bodyLimit = (options) => {
  const onError = options.onError || (() => {
    const res = new Response(ERROR_MESSAGE, { status: 413 });
    throw new HTTPException(413, { res });
  });
  const maxSize = options.maxSize;
  return async function bodyLimit2(c, next) {
    if (!c.req.raw.body) return next();
    const hasTransferEncoding = c.req.raw.headers.has("transfer-encoding");
    if (c.req.raw.headers.has("content-length") && !hasTransferEncoding) return parseInt(c.req.raw.headers.get("content-length") || "0", 10) > maxSize ? onError(c) : next();
    let size = 0;
    const chunks = [];
    const rawReader = c.req.raw.body.getReader();
    for (; ; ) {
      const { done, value } = await rawReader.read();
      if (done) break;
      size += value.length;
      if (size > maxSize) return onError(c);
      chunks.push(value);
    }
    const requestInit = {
      body: new ReadableStream({ start(controller) {
        for (const chunk of chunks) controller.enqueue(chunk);
        controller.close();
      } }),
      duplex: "half"
    };
    c.req.raw = new Request(c.req.raw, requestInit);
    return next();
  };
};

  });

async function notifyAdminAction(env, log) {
  const title = ACTIONS[log.action];
  if (!title) return;
  try {
    await enqueueFallback(env, {
      phone: "",
      userId: null,
      type: log.action.startsWith("invoice_") ? "report_invoice" : "report_action",
      title: `\u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A | ${title}`,
      eventKey: `audit:${log.id}`,
      message: [
        `\u0627\u0646\u062C\u0627\u0645\u200C\u062F\u0647\u0646\u062F\u0647: ${log.actor.slice(0, 80)}`,
        log.targetId != null ? `\u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u0647 \u0645\u0631\u062A\u0628\u0637: ${log.targetId}` : "",
        (log.details ?? "").slice(0, 700),
        `\u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u0647 \u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634: ${log.id}`
      ].filter(Boolean).join("\n")
    }, "event");
  } catch (error) {
    await reportFailure(env, "audit", log.id, error);
  }
}
async function notifyAdminInvoice(env, invoiceId) {
  try {
    const config = await loadFallbackConfig(env);
    if (!config.notifyEnabled || config.notifyInvoices === false) return;
    const row = await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT i.*,u.name AS customer_name,u.phone,s.name AS staff_name
      FROM invoices i JOIN users u ON u.id=i.user_id LEFT JOIN users s ON s.id=i.registered_by
      WHERE i.id=? AND i.voided_at IS NULL`).bind(invoiceId).first();
    if (!row) return;
    const parsed = safeJsonParse(row.items, []);
    const items = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((i) => i && typeof i.name === "string") : [];
    const lines = (Array.isArray(items) ? items : []).slice(0, 10).map((i) => `\u2022 ${String(i.name).slice(0, 60)} \xD7 ${i.qty} | ${formatNumberFa(i.price * i.qty)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646`);
    if (items.length > 10) lines.push(`\u0648 ${formatNumberFa(items.length - 10)} \u0631\u062F\u06CC\u0641 \u062F\u06CC\u06AF\u0631\u061B \u062C\u0632\u0626\u06CC\u0627\u062A \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u062F\u0631 \u067E\u0646\u0644.`);
    const message = [
      `\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 #${row.id} | \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC: ${row.customer_name}`,
      `\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u0646\u062F\u0647: ${row.staff_name ?? "\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0628\u0631 \u062D\u0630\u0641\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647"}`,
      `\u0632\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u062B\u0628\u062A: ${formatJalaliDateTime(row.created_at)}`,
      `\u062C\u0645\u0639 \u0627\u0642\u0644\u0627\u0645: ${formatNumberFa(row.subtotal)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646`,
      `\u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641: ${formatNumberFa(row.discount)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646`,
      `\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0646\u0647\u0627\u06CC\u06CC: ${formatNumberFa(row.amount)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646`,
      row.payment_method ? `\u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u0645\u0624\u062B\u0631: \u0646\u0642\u062F ${formatNumberFa(row.paid_cash)} | \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A ${formatNumberFa(row.paid_card)} | \u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646 ${formatNumberFa(row.paid_online)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646` : "\u0627\u0637\u0644\u0627\u0639\u0627\u062A \u0631\u0648\u0634 \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.",
      row.change_amount ? `\u0628\u0627\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647 \u0628\u0631\u06AF\u0634\u062A\u06CC: ${formatNumberFa(row.change_amount)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 (\u0627\u0632 \u0646\u0642\u062F \u0645\u0624\u062B\u0631 \u06A9\u0633\u0631 \u0634\u062F\u0647)` : "",
      `\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631: ${formatNumberFa(row.points_earned)}`,
      ...lines
    ].filter(Boolean).join("\n");
    await enqueueFallback(env, {
      phone: row.phone,
      userId: row.user_id,
      type: "report_invoice",
      title: "\u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A | \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F",
      eventKey: `invoice:${row.id}`,
      message
    }, "event");
  } catch (error) {
    await reportFailure(env, "invoice", invoiceId, error);
  }
}

// src/lib/auth.ts
init_utils();
init_db();
var SESSION_COOKIE = "cc_session";
var SESSION_TTL_SECONDS = 12 * 60 * 60;
function customerSessionTtl(env) {
  const days = Number(env.CUSTOMER_SESSION_DAYS);
  const safe = Number.isFinite(days) && days > 0 ? Math.floor(days) : 30;
  return Math.min(90, Math.max(1, safe)) * 86400;
}
function sessionTtl(env) {
  const hours = Number(env.SESSION_HOURS);
  if (!Number.isFinite(hours) || hours <= 0) return SESSION_TTL_SECONDS;
  return Math.min(168, Math.max(1, Math.floor(hours))) * 3600;
}
async function createToken(secret, user, ttlSeconds = SESSION_TTL_SECONDS) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3);
  const payload = {
    sub: String(user.id),
    uid: user.id,
    role: user.role,
    name: user.name,
    tv: user.token_version ?? 0,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + ttlSeconds
  };
  return sign2(payload, secret, "HS256");
}
function setSessionCookie(c, token, isSecure, ttlSeconds = SESSION_TTL_SECONDS) {
  dropSessionCookieHeaders(c);
  setCookie(c, SESSION_COOKIE, token, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: isSecure,
    sameSite: "Lax",
    path: "/",
    maxAge: ttlSeconds
  });
}
function dropSessionCookieHeaders(c) {
  try {
    const headers = c.res.headers;
    const existing = typeof headers.getSetCookie === "function" ? headers.getSetCookie() : (headers.get("Set-Cookie") ?? "").split(/,(?=\s*[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+=)/);
    if (existing.length === 0) return;
    const keep = existing.filter((value) => !String(value).trim().startsWith(`${SESSION_COOKIE}=`));
    headers.delete("Set-Cookie");
    for (const value of keep) headers.append("Set-Cookie", value);
  } catch {
  }
}
function clearSessionCookie(c, isSecure) {
  deleteCookie(c, SESSION_COOKIE, {
    path: "/",
    secure: isSecure,
    sameSite: "Lax"
  });
}
function isSecureRequest(c) {
  try {
    return new URL(c.req.url).protocol === "https:";
  } catch {
    return true;
  }
}
async function readSession(c, secret) {
  const token = getCookie(c, SESSION_COOKIE);
  if (!token || token.length > 2048) return null;
  try {
    const payload = await verify2(token, secret, "HS256");
    const uid = Number(payload.uid ?? payload.sub);
    const role = payload.role;
    if (!Number.isFinite(uid) || uid <= 0) return null;
    if (role !== "admin" && role !== "staff" && role !== "customer") return null;
    return {
      sub: String(payload.sub ?? uid),
      uid,
      role,
      name: typeof payload.name === "string" ? payload.name : "",
      tv: typeof payload.tv === "number" ? payload.tv : 0,
      exp: typeof payload.exp === "number" ? payload.exp : void 0,
      iat: typeof payload.iat === "number" ? payload.iat : void 0
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
async function getActiveUser(db, uid) {
  const user = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1").bind(uid).first();
  if (!user || user.is_active !== 1) return null;
  const ready = await ensureSessionVersion(db, user);
  return ready?.is_active === 1 ? ready : null;
}
async function ensureSessionVersion(db, user) {
  if (Number.isSafeInteger(user.token_version) && user.token_version >= 0) return user;
  if (user.token_version !== void 0) throw new Error("AUTH_SESSION_VERSION_INVALID");
  try {
    await db.prepare("ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN token_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0").run();
    console.info("AUTH_SCHEMA_UPGRADE: users.token_version added");
    await logAdminAction(db, {
      adminId: null,
      adminName: "\u0633\u06CC\u0633\u062A\u0645",
      action: "auth_schema_upgrade",
      targetType: "schema",
      targetId: "users.token_version",
      details: "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN token_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (!/duplicate column name:\s*token_version\b/i.test(String(error))) throw error;
  }
  const current = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1").bind(user.id).first();
  if (current && (!Number.isSafeInteger(current.token_version) || current.token_version < 0)) {
    throw new Error("AUTH_SESSION_VERSION_INVALID");
  }
  return current;
}
function loginDestination(next, role) {
  const home = homeForRole(role);
  if (!next || next.length > 200 || /[\\\x00-\x20\x7f]/.test(next)) return home;
  try {
    const url = new URL(next, "https://navigation.invalid");
    if (!next.startsWith("/") || url.origin !== "https://navigation.invalid") return home;
    if (url.pathname !== home && !url.pathname.startsWith(home + "/")) return home;
    return url.pathname + url.search + url.hash;
  } catch {
    return home;
  }
}
var attachUser = async (c, next) => {
  const secret = c.env.JWT_SECRET;
  if (!secret) {
    await next();
    return;
  }
  const payload = await readSession(c, secret);
  if (!payload) {
    if (getCookie(c, SESSION_COOKIE)) {
      c.set("sessionInvalid", true);
      clearSessionCookie(c, isSecureRequest(c));
    }
    c.set("user", null);
    await next();
    return;
  }
  let user = null;
  try {
    user = await getActiveUser(c.env.DB, payload.uid);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("attachUser:getActiveUser failed:", error);
    c.set("sessionLookupFailed", true);
    c.set("user", null);
    await next();
    return;
  }
  if (!user || (payload.tv ?? 0) !== (user.token_version ?? 0)) {
    c.set("sessionInvalid", true);
    clearSessionCookie(c, isSecureRequest(c));
    c.set("user", null);
    await next();
    return;
  }
  c.set("user", { ...payload, role: user.role, name: user.name });
  await next();
};
function unauthorized(c) {
  if (c.get("sessionLookupFailed")) return fail(c, "\u062F\u06CC\u062A\u0627\u0628\u06CC\u0633 \u0645\u0648\u0642\u062A\u0627\u064B \u062F\u0631 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u0631\u0633 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A\u061B \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.", 503, "SESSION_UNAVAILABLE");
  return fail(c, "\u0644\u0637\u0641\u0627\u064B \u0627\u0628\u062A\u062F\u0627 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u062D\u0633\u0627\u0628 \u062E\u0648\u062F \u0634\u0648\u06CC\u062F.", 401, "UNAUTHORIZED");
}
function forbidden(c) {
  return fail(c, "\u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0628\u0647 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0628\u062E\u0634 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u0631\u0633\u06CC \u0646\u062F\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062F.", 403, "FORBIDDEN");
}
function requireApiRole(...roles) {
  return async (c, next) => {
    const user = c.get("user");
    if (!user) return unauthorized(c);
    if (!roles.includes(user.role)) return forbidden(c);
    await next();
  };
}
function requirePageRole(...roles) {
  return async (c, next) => {
    const user = c.get("user");
    const url = new URL(c.req.url);
    if (!user) {
      if (c.get("sessionLookupFailed")) {
        c.header("Retry-After", "5");
        return c.html(errorPage(503, "\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0646 \u062D\u0633\u0627\u0628 \u0627\u0632 \u062F\u06CC\u062A\u0627\u0628\u06CC\u0633 \u0645\u0648\u0642\u062A\u0627\u064B \u0645\u0645\u06A9\u0646 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A. \u06A9\u0645\u06CC \u0628\u0639\u062F \u0635\u0641\u062D\u0647 \u0631\u0627 \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.", c.get("settings")?.cafe_name), 503);
      }
      const expired = c.get("sessionInvalid") ? "&expired=1" : "";
      return c.redirect(`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(url.pathname + url.search)}${expired}`, 302);
    }
    if (!roles.includes(user.role)) {
      return c.redirect(user.role === "admin" ? "/admin" : "/staff", 302);
    }
    await next();
  };
}
function homeForRole(role) {
  if (role === "admin") return "/admin";
  if (role === "staff") return "/staff";
  return "/me";
}
var buckets = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
var lastPrune = Date.now();
function pruneBuckets(now) {
  if (now - lastPrune < 6e4) return;
  lastPrune = now;
  for (const [key, bucket] of buckets) {
    if (bucket.resetAt <= now) buckets.delete(key);
  }
}
function clientKey(c) {
  return c.req.header("CF-Connecting-IP") || c.req.header("X-Forwarded-For")?.split(",")[0]?.trim() || "local";
}
function checkRateLimit(key, limit, windowMs) {
  const now = Date.now();
  pruneBuckets(now);
  const bucket = buckets.get(key);
  if (!bucket || bucket.resetAt <= now) {
    buckets.set(key, { count: 1, resetAt: now + windowMs });
    return { allowed: true, retryAfter: 0 };
  }
  bucket.count += 1;
  if (bucket.count > limit) {
    return { allowed: false, retryAfter: Math.ceil((bucket.resetAt - now) / 1e3) };
  }
  return { allowed: true, retryAfter: 0 };
}
function resetRateLimit(key) {
  buckets.delete(key);
}
function assertJwtSecret(env) {
  const secret = env.JWT_SECRET;
  if (!secret || secret.length < 16) return null;
  if (secret.length < 32) {
    console.warn(
      "JWT_SECRET \u06A9\u0648\u062A\u0627\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u06CC\u06A9 \u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F \u062A\u0635\u0627\u062F\u0641\u06CC \u06F3\u06F2 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u06CC\u0627 \u0628\u06CC\u0634\u062A\u0631 \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F: wrangler secret put JWT_SECRET"
    );
  }
  return secret;
}
function sessionInfo(user) {
  if (!user) return { authenticated: false, userId: null, role: null, name: null, expiresAt: null };
  return {
    authenticated: true,
    userId: user.uid,
    role: user.role,
    name: user.name,
    expiresAt: user.exp ? user.exp * 1e3 : null
  };
}

// src/lib/jobs.ts
init_utils();
init_sms();
init_db();
init_settings();
init_otp();
var MAX_SMS_PER_RUN = 6;
var CHURN_COOLDOWN_DAYS = 30;
var JOB_RUN_PREFIX = "job_last_run:";
async function alreadyRanToday(env, job, todayISO2) {
  const raw2 = await getRawSetting(env, `${JOB_RUN_PREFIX}${job}`);
  if (!raw2) return false;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw2);
    return parsed.date === todayISO2;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
async function markRan(env, job, dateISO) {
  await saveSetting(env, `${JOB_RUN_PREFIX}${job}`, {
    date: dateISO,
    at: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()
  });
}
async function getJobRuns(env) {
  const jobs = ["churn", "birthday", "weather", "maintenance", "monthly"];
  const out = {};
  for (const job of jobs) {
    const raw2 = await getRawSetting(env, `${JOB_RUN_PREFIX}${job}`);
    if (!raw2) {
      out[job] = null;
      continue;
    }
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw2);
      out[job] = { date: parsed.date ?? "", at: parsed.at ?? "" };
    } catch {
      out[job] = null;
    }
  }
  return out;
}
async function runChurnJob(env, settings, force = false) {
  const dateKey = todayISO();
  if (!force && await alreadyRanToday(env, "churn", dateKey)) {
    return {
      job: "churn",
      ok: true,
      sent: 0,
      skipped: 0,
      failed: 0,
      message: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u062C\u0627\u0628 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0627\u062C\u0631\u0627 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A."
    };
  }
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT u.id, u.name, u.phone, u.last_visit_date, u.avg_visit_interval, u.last_churn_sms_at, u.tier,
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoices i WHERE i.user_id = u.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL) AS visits
     FROM users u
     WHERE u.role = 'customer' AND u.is_active = 1 AND u.sms_opt_in = 1
       AND u.last_visit_date IS NOT NULL
     LIMIT 3000`
  ).all();
  const candidates = (results ?? []).filter((row) => {
    const days = daysSince(row.last_visit_date);
    const hasReliableAverage = row.visits >= settings.churn_min_visits && row.avg_visit_interval > 0;
    const baseline = hasReliableAverage ? row.avg_visit_interval : CHURN_DEFAULT_INTERVAL;
    const threshold = baseline + settings.churn_grace_days;
    if (days <= threshold) return false;
    if (row.last_churn_sms_at) {
      const lastMs = sqliteToMs(row.last_churn_sms_at);
      if (lastMs !== null && Date.now() - lastMs < CHURN_COOLDOWN_DAYS * 864e5) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  });
  let sent = 0;
  let queued = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  let skipped = 0;
  for (const row of candidates.slice(0, MAX_SMS_PER_RUN)) {
    const days = daysSince(row.last_visit_date);
    const message = renderSms(settings, "churn", {
      name: row.name,
      last_visit: toPersianDigits(days),
      days: toPersianDigits(days),
      avg: toPersianDigits(
        Math.round(
          row.visits >= settings.churn_min_visits && row.avg_visit_interval > 0 ? row.avg_visit_interval : CHURN_DEFAULT_INTERVAL
        )
      ),
      tier: tierLabel(row.tier),
      cafe: settings.cafe_name
    });
    const result = await sendSms(env, settings, {
      userId: row.id,
      phone: row.phone,
      type: "churn",
      message
    });
    if (result.status === "sent" || result.status === "pending") {
      if (result.status === "sent") sent += 1;
      else queued += 1;
      await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE users SET last_churn_sms_at = ? WHERE id = ?").bind((/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace("T", " "), row.id).run();
    } else if (result.status === "failed") {
      failed += 1;
    } else {
      skipped += 1;
    }
  }
  await markRan(env, "churn", dateKey);
  console.log(`[job:churn] candidates=${candidates.length} sent=${sent} failed=${failed}`);
  return {
    job: "churn",
    ok: true,
    sent,
    queued,
    skipped,
    failed,
    message: `${toPersianDigits(candidates.length)} \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062F\u0631 \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0636 \u0631\u06CC\u0632\u0634 \u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u0627\u06CC\u06CC \u0634\u062F\u061B ${toPersianDigits(sent)} \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F. \u0645\u0633\u06CC\u0631 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646: ${toPersianDigits(queued)} \u062F\u0631 \u0635\u0641 (\u0646\u0647 \u062A\u062D\u0648\u06CC\u0644 \u0628\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06A9).`,
    details: { candidates: candidates.length }
  };
}
async function runBirthdayJob(env, settings, force = false) {
  const dateKey = todayISO();
  if (!force && await alreadyRanToday(env, "birthday", dateKey)) {
    return {
      job: "birthday",
      ok: true,
      sent: 0,
      skipped: 0,
      failed: 0,
      message: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u062C\u0627\u0628 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0627\u062C\u0631\u0627 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A."
    };
  }
  const today = todayJalali();
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT * FROM users
     WHERE role = 'customer' AND is_active = 1 AND sms_opt_in = 1 AND birth_date IS NOT NULL
     LIMIT 5000`
  ).all();
  const birthdays = (results ?? []).filter((user) => {
    const iso = String(user.birth_date ?? "").slice(0, 10);
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(iso)) return false;
    const [gy, gm, gd] = iso.split("-").map((n) => parseInt(n, 10));
    const jalali = toJalali(gy, gm, gd);
    return jalali.jm === today.jm && jalali.jd === today.jd;
  });
  let sent = 0;
  let queued = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  let skipped = 0;
  const yearStartG = toGregorian(today.jy, 1, 1);
  const yearStart = utcStamp(Date.UTC(yearStartG.gy, yearStartG.gm - 1, yearStartG.gd) - 3.5 * 36e5);
  const { results: knownCoupons } = await env.DB.prepare(
    "SELECT DISTINCT user_id FROM coupons WHERE reason = 'birthday' AND created_at >= ?"
  ).bind(yearStart).all();
  const alreadyIssued = new Set((knownCoupons ?? []).map((row) => row.user_id));
  let attempts = 0;
  for (const user of birthdays) {
    if (alreadyIssued.has(user.id)) {
      skipped += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (attempts >= MAX_SMS_PER_RUN) break;
    attempts += 1;
    let coupon = null;
    coupon = await createCoupon(env.DB, {
      userId: user.id,
      kind: "percent",
      value: settings.birthday_coupon_percent,
      reason: "birthday",
      expiresAt: utcStamp(Date.now() + 14 * 864e5),
      maxUses: 1
    });
    if (!coupon) {
      failed += 1;
      continue;
    }
    const result = await sendSms(env, settings, {
      userId: user.id,
      phone: user.phone,
      type: "birthday",
      message: renderSms(settings, "birthday", {
        name: user.name,
        coupon: coupon.code,
        percent: toPersianDigits(settings.birthday_coupon_percent)
      })
    });
    if (result.status === "sent") sent += 1;
    else if (result.status === "pending") queued += 1;
    else if (result.status === "failed") failed += 1;
    else skipped += 1;
  }
  await markRan(env, "birthday", dateKey);
  console.log(`[job:birthday] found=${birthdays.length} sent=${sent} failed=${failed}`);
  return {
    job: "birthday",
    ok: true,
    sent,
    queued,
    skipped,
    failed,
    message: `${toPersianDigits(birthdays.length)} \u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632\u061B ${toPersianDigits(sent)} \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0648 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F. \u0645\u0633\u06CC\u0631 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646: ${toPersianDigits(queued)} \u062F\u0631 \u0635\u0641 (\u0646\u0647 \u062A\u062D\u0648\u06CC\u0644 \u0628\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06A9).`,
    details: { birthdays: birthdays.length }
  };
}
async function runWeatherJob(env, settings, force = false) {
  const dateKey = todayISO();
  if (!force && await alreadyRanToday(env, "weather", dateKey)) {
    return {
      job: "weather",
      ok: true,
      sent: 0,
      skipped: 0,
      failed: 0,
      message: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u062C\u0627\u0628 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0627\u062C\u0631\u0627 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A."
    };
  }
  const weather = await fetchWeather(env, settings);
  if (!weather.ok) {
    await markRan(env, "weather", dateKey);
    return {
      job: "weather",
      ok: false,
      sent: 0,
      skipped: 0,
      failed: 0,
      message: `\u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0622\u0628\u200C\u0648\u0647\u0648\u0627 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642 \u0628\u0648\u062F: ${weather.error ?? "\u062E\u0637\u0627\u06CC \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0634\u062E\u0635"}`
    };
  }
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT id, name, phone, tier FROM users
     WHERE role = 'customer' AND is_active = 1 AND sms_opt_in = 1
       AND (last_visit_date >= datetime('now', '-30 days') OR created_at >= datetime('now', '-30 days'))
     ORDER BY last_visit_date DESC
     LIMIT ?`
  ).bind(MAX_SMS_PER_RUN).all();
  let sent = 0;
  let queued = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  for (const user of results ?? []) {
    const result = await sendSms(env, settings, {
      userId: user.id,
      phone: user.phone,
      type: "weather",
      message: renderSms(settings, "weather", {
        name: user.name,
        city: weather.city,
        weather: `${weather.label} ${weather.emoji}`.trim(),
        temp: toPersianDigits(weather.temp),
        advice: weather.advice,
        tier: tierLabel(user.tier)
      })
    });
    if (result.status === "sent") sent += 1;
    else if (result.status === "pending") queued += 1;
    else if (result.status === "failed") failed += 1;
  }
  await markRan(env, "weather", dateKey);
  console.log(`[job:weather] ${weather.label} ${weather.temp}C sent=${sent}`);
  return {
    job: "weather",
    ok: true,
    sent,
    queued,
    skipped: 0,
    failed,
    message: `\u0647\u0648\u0627: ${weather.label} ${toPersianDigits(weather.temp)}\xB0C \u2014 ${toPersianDigits(sent)} \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F. \u0645\u0633\u06CC\u0631 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646: ${toPersianDigits(queued)} \u062F\u0631 \u0635\u0641 (\u0646\u0647 \u062A\u062D\u0648\u06CC\u0644 \u0628\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06A9).`,
    details: { weather: weather.label, temp: weather.temp }
  };
}
async function runMaintenanceJob(env) {
  const { results: users } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT id FROM users
     WHERE last_visit_date IS NOT NULL
     ORDER BY last_visit_date DESC
     LIMIT 14`
  ).all();
  for (const user of users ?? []) {
    await recomputeAvgInterval(env.DB, user.id);
  }
  const expiredCoupons = await env.DB.prepare(
    `UPDATE coupons SET is_active = 0
     WHERE is_active = 1 AND expires_at IS NOT NULL AND expires_at < datetime('now')`
  ).run();
  const oldLogs = await env.DB.prepare(
    `DELETE FROM sms_logs WHERE sent_at < datetime('now', '-180 days')`
  ).run();
  const oldOtps = await env.DB.prepare(
    "DELETE FROM otp_codes WHERE created_at < datetime('now', '-2 days')"
  ).run();
  await pruneRateLimits(env.DB);
  await markRan(env, "maintenance", todayISO());
  return {
    job: "maintenance",
    ok: true,
    sent: 0,
    skipped: 0,
    failed: 0,
    message: `\u0645\u06CC\u0627\u0646\u06AF\u06CC\u0646 ${toPersianDigits(users?.length ?? 0)} \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0628\u0627\u0632\u0645\u062D\u0627\u0633\u0628\u0647 \u0634\u062F\u061B ${toPersianDigits(
      expiredCoupons.meta?.changes ?? 0
    )} \u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0646\u0642\u0636\u06CC\u060C ${toPersianDigits(oldLogs.meta?.changes ?? 0)} \u0644\u0627\u06AF \u0642\u062F\u06CC\u0645\u06CC \u0648 ${toPersianDigits(
      oldOtps.meta?.changes ?? 0
    )} \u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0645\u0646\u0642\u0636\u06CC \u067E\u0627\u06A9 \u0634\u062F.`
  };
}
async function runMonthlyReport(env, settings, force = false) {
  const dateKey = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString().slice(0, 8);
  if (!force && await alreadyRanToday(env, "monthly", dateKey)) {
    return {
      job: "monthly",
      ok: true,
      sent: 0,
      skipped: 0,
      failed: 0,
      message: "\u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0645\u0627\u0647 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A."
    };
  }
  const today = todayJalali();
  const startG = toGregorian(today.jy, today.jm, 1);
  const offset = 3.5 * 36e5;
  const monthStart = utcStamp(Date.UTC(startG.gy, startG.gm - 1, startG.gd) - offset);
  const stats = await getDashboardStats(
    env.DB,
    settings,
    utcStamp(Date.now() - offset),
    utcStamp(Date.now() - offset + 864e5),
    monthStart,
    utcStamp(Date.now() - 30 * 864e5)
  );
  const { results: admins } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT id, name, phone FROM users WHERE role = 'admin' AND is_active = 1 LIMIT 6`
  ).all();
  const message = `\u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 ${formatJalali((/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString().slice(0, 10))}
\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646: ${toPersianDigits(stats.totalCustomers)}
\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632: ${formatNumberFa(stats.todaySales)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646
\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u0645\u0627\u0647: ${formatNumberFa(stats.monthSales)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646
\u062F\u0631 \u062E\u0637\u0631 \u0631\u06CC\u0632\u0634: ${toPersianDigits(stats.sleepingCustomers)}
${settings.cafe_name}`;
  let sent = 0;
  let queued = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  let skipped = 0;
  for (const admin of admins ?? []) {
    const result = await sendSms(env, settings, {
      userId: admin.id,
      phone: admin.phone,
      type: "admin",
      message
    });
    if (result.status === "sent") sent += 1;
    else if (result.status === "pending") queued += 1;
    else if (result.status === "failed") failed += 1;
    else skipped += 1;
  }
  await markRan(env, "monthly", dateKey);
  return {
    job: "monthly",
    ok: true,
    sent,
    queued,
    skipped,
    failed,
    message: `\u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0645\u0627\u0647\u0627\u0646\u0647 \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC ${toPersianDigits(sent)} \u0627\u062F\u0645\u06CC\u0646 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F. \u0645\u0633\u06CC\u0631 \u062C\u0627\u06CC\u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646: ${toPersianDigits(queued)} \u062F\u0631 \u0635\u0641 (\u0646\u0647 \u062A\u062D\u0648\u06CC\u0644 \u0628\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06A9).`
  };
}
function isJobName(value) {
  return ["churn", "birthday", "weather", "maintenance", "monthly"].includes(value);
}
async function runJobByName(env, settings, name, force = true) {
  switch (name) {
    case "churn":
      return runChurnJob(env, settings, force);
    case "birthday":
      return runBirthdayJob(env, settings, force);
    case "weather":
      return runWeatherJob(env, settings, force);
    case "maintenance":
      return runMaintenanceJob(env);
    case "monthly":
      return runMonthlyReport(env, settings, force);
  }
}
async function dispatchCron(env, settings, cron) {
  const normalized = cron.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (normalized === "30 19 * * *") {
    return [await runChurnJob(env, settings)];
  }
  if (normalized === "30 4 * * *") {
    return [await runBirthdayJob(env, settings)];
  }
  if (normalized === "0 5 * * *") {
    return [await runWeatherJob(env, settings)];
  }
  if (normalized === "0 * * * *") {
    return [await runMaintenanceJob(env)];
  }
  if (normalized === "0 20 1 * *") {
    return [await runMonthlyReport(env, settings)];
  }
  const results = [];
  results.push(await runBirthdayJob(env, settings));
  results.push(await runWeatherJob(env, settings));
  results.push(await runChurnJob(env, settings));
  results.push(await runMaintenanceJob(env));
  if ((/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).getUTCDate() <= 2) {
    results.push(await runMonthlyReport(env, settings));
  }
  return results;
}

// src/lib/shifts.ts
init_utils();
async function getOpenShift(db, staffId) {
  const row = await db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM shifts WHERE staff_id = ? AND status = 'open' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1`).bind(staffId).first();
  return row ?? null;
}
async function listOpenShifts(db) {
  const { results } = await db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM shifts WHERE status = 'open' ORDER BY opened_at ASC`).all();
  return results ?? [];
}
async function getShiftSummary(db, shift) {
  const invoices = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS c,
              COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS total,
              COALESCE(SUM(discount), 0) AS discount,
              COALESCE(SUM(paid_cash), 0) AS cash,
              COALESCE(SUM(paid_card), 0) AS card,
              COALESCE(SUM(paid_online), 0) AS online
       FROM invoices
       WHERE shift_id = ? AND voided_at IS NULL`
  ).bind(shift.id).first();
  const voided = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS c, COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS s
       FROM invoices WHERE shift_id = ? AND voided_at IS NOT NULL`
  ).bind(shift.id).first();
  const movements = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT
         COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN kind = 'in'  THEN amount ELSE 0 END), 0) AS in_sum,
         COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN kind = 'out' THEN amount ELSE 0 END), 0) AS out_sum
       FROM cash_movements WHERE shift_id = ?`
  ).bind(shift.id).first();
  const cashSales = invoices?.cash ?? 0;
  const movementsIn = movements?.in_sum ?? 0;
  const movementsOut = movements?.out_sum ?? 0;
  const expectedCash = shift.opening_cash + cashSales + movementsIn - movementsOut;
  const counted = shift.counted_cash;
  return {
    invoiceCount: invoices?.c ?? 0,
    cashSales,
    cardSales: invoices?.card ?? 0,
    onlineSales: invoices?.online ?? 0,
    totalSales: invoices?.total ?? 0,
    totalDiscount: invoices?.discount ?? 0,
    voidCount: voided?.c ?? 0,
    voidAmount: voided?.s ?? 0,
    movementsIn,
    movementsOut,
    expectedCash,
    difference: counted === null || counted === void 0 ? null : counted - expectedCash
  };
}
async function getShiftDetail(db, shiftId) {
  const shift = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM shifts WHERE id = ?").bind(shiftId).first();
  if (!shift) return null;
  const [summary, movementsRes, invoicesRes] = await Promise.all([
    getShiftSummary(db, shift),
    db.prepare("SELECT * FROM cash_movements WHERE shift_id = ? ORDER BY id ASC").bind(shiftId).all(),
    db.prepare(
      `SELECT i.id, i.amount, i.discount, i.payment_method, i.paid_cash, i.paid_card, i.paid_online,
                i.change_amount, i.points_earned, i.created_at, i.voided_at,
                u.name AS customer_name, u.phone AS customer_phone
         FROM invoices i
         JOIN users u ON u.id = i.user_id
         WHERE i.shift_id = ?
         ORDER BY i.id DESC
         LIMIT 300`
    ).bind(shiftId).all()
  ]);
  return {
    shift,
    summary,
    movements: movementsRes.results ?? [],
    invoices: invoicesRes.results ?? []
  };
}
async function listShifts(db, params = {}) {
  const page = Math.max(1, Math.floor(params.page ?? 1));
  const pageSize = Math.min(100, Math.max(5, Math.floor(params.pageSize ?? 20)));
  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  const where = ["1 = 1"];
  const binds = [];
  if (params.status === "open" || params.status === "closed") {
    where.push("s.status = ?");
    binds.push(params.status);
  }
  if (params.staffId) {
    where.push("s.staff_id = ?");
    binds.push(params.staffId);
  }
  if (params.from && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(params.from)) {
    where.push("s.opened_at >= ?");
    binds.push(`${params.from} 00:00:00`);
  }
  if (params.to && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(params.to)) {
    where.push("s.opened_at <= ?");
    binds.push(`${params.to} 23:59:59`);
  }
  const whereSql = where.join(" AND ");
  const totalRow = await db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM shifts s WHERE ${whereSql}`).bind(...binds).first();
  const { results } = await db.prepare(
    `SELECT s.*,
              (s.opening_cash
               + COALESCE((SELECT SUM(i.paid_cash) FROM invoices i
                           WHERE i.shift_id = s.id AND i.voided_at IS NULL), 0)
               + COALESCE((SELECT SUM(m.amount) FROM cash_movements m
                           WHERE m.shift_id = s.id AND m.kind = 'in'), 0)
               - COALESCE((SELECT SUM(m.amount) FROM cash_movements m
                           WHERE m.shift_id = s.id AND m.kind = 'out'), 0)
              ) AS expected_live
       FROM shifts s
       WHERE ${whereSql}
       ORDER BY s.id DESC
       LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`
  ).bind(...binds, pageSize, offset).all();
  return { items: results ?? [], total: totalRow?.c ?? 0, page, pageSize };
}
async function openShift(env, input) {
  const openingCash = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(input.openingCash) || 0));
  if (!Number.isFinite(openingCash) || openingCash > 5e9) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0647\u0654 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "INVALID_AMOUNT" };
  }
  const existing = await getOpenShift(env.DB, input.staffId);
  if (existing) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: `\u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0627\u0632 \u0642\u0628\u0644 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u062F\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062F (\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A #${toPersianDigits(existing.id)}). \u0627\u0648\u0644 \u0622\u0646 \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0628\u0646\u062F\u06CC\u062F.`,
      code: "ALREADY_OPEN"
    };
  }
  try {
    const result = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO shifts (staff_id, staff_name, opened_at, opening_cash, status)
       VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'), ?, 'open')`
    ).bind(input.staffId, input.staffName.slice(0, 80), openingCash).run();
    const id = Number(result.meta?.last_row_id ?? 0);
    const shift = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM shifts WHERE id = ?").bind(id).first();
    if (!shift) return { ok: false, error: "\u062E\u0637\u0627 \u062F\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642.", code: "DB_ERROR" };
    return { ok: true, shift };
  } catch (error) {
    if (String(error).includes("UNIQUE")) {
      return { ok: false, error: "\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0627\u0632 \u0642\u0628\u0644 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "ALREADY_OPEN" };
    }
    console.error("openShift failed:", error);
    return { ok: false, error: "\u062E\u0637\u0627 \u062F\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642.", code: "DB_ERROR" };
  }
}
async function addCashMovement(env, input) {
  const amount = Math.floor(Number(input.amount) || 0);
  if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u0632\u0631\u06AF\u200C\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u0635\u0641\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.", code: "INVALID_AMOUNT" };
  }
  if (amount > 1e9) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0628\u06CC\u0634 \u0627\u0632 \u062D\u062F \u0645\u062C\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "INVALID_AMOUNT" };
  }
  if (input.kind !== "in" && input.kind !== "out") {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0646\u0648\u0639 \u062A\u0631\u0627\u06A9\u0646\u0634 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "INVALID_KIND" };
  }
  const shift = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM shifts WHERE id = ?").bind(input.shiftId).first();
  if (!shift) return { ok: false, error: "\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F.", code: "NOT_FOUND" };
  if (shift.status !== "open") {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u062B\u0628\u062A \u062A\u0631\u0627\u06A9\u0646\u0634 \u0645\u0645\u06A9\u0646 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.", code: "SHIFT_CLOSED" };
  }
  await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO cash_movements (shift_id, kind, amount, reason, created_at, created_by)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), ?)`
  ).bind(input.shiftId, input.kind, amount, String(input.reason ?? "").slice(0, 120) || null, input.actorId).run();
  return { ok: true, summary: await getShiftSummary(env.DB, shift) };
}
async function closeShift(env, input) {
  const counted = Math.floor(Number(input.countedCash));
  if (!Number.isFinite(counted) || counted < 0) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0634\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "INVALID_AMOUNT" };
  }
  if (counted > 5e9) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0634\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0628\u06CC\u0634 \u0627\u0632 \u062D\u062F \u0645\u062C\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "INVALID_AMOUNT" };
  }
  const shift = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM shifts WHERE id = ?").bind(input.shiftId).first();
  if (!shift) return { ok: false, error: "\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F.", code: "NOT_FOUND" };
  if (shift.status === "closed") {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", code: "ALREADY_CLOSED" };
  }
  if (shift.staff_id !== input.closedBy && input.allowOtherStaff !== true) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0635\u0627\u062D\u0628 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u06CC\u0627 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F \u0622\u0646 \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0628\u0646\u062F\u062F.", code: "FORBIDDEN" };
  }
  const summary = await getShiftSummary(env.DB, shift);
  const difference = counted - summary.expectedCash;
  const claim = await env.DB.prepare(
    `UPDATE shifts
     SET status = 'closed',
         closed_at = datetime('now'),
         closed_by = ?,
         closed_by_name = ?,
         counted_cash = ?,
         expected_cash = ?,
         difference = ?,
         notes = ?,
         invoice_count = ?,
         cash_sales = ?,
         card_sales = ?,
         online_sales = ?,
         total_sales = ?,
         void_count = ?
     WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open'`
  ).bind(
    input.closedBy,
    input.closedByName.slice(0, 80),
    counted,
    summary.expectedCash,
    difference,
    String(input.notes ?? "").slice(0, 300) || null,
    summary.invoiceCount,
    summary.cashSales,
    summary.cardSales,
    summary.onlineSales,
    summary.totalSales,
    summary.voidCount,
    input.shiftId
  ).run();
  if (!claim.meta?.changes) {
    return { ok: false, error: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0647\u0645\u06CC\u0646 \u062D\u0627\u0644\u0627 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647 \u0634\u062F.", code: "ALREADY_CLOSED" };
  }
  const updated = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM shifts WHERE id = ?").bind(input.shiftId).first();
  return {
    ok: true,
    shift: updated ?? shift,
    summary: { ...summary, difference }
  };
}

'<div class="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-rose-500/25 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-200 text-[11.5px] px-3 py-2.5">\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9\u06CC \u0645\u0648\u0642\u062A\u0627\u064B \u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0633\u062A. \u0644\u0637\u0641\u0627\u064B \u0628\u0627 \u067E\u0631\u0633\u0646\u0644 \u06A9\u0627\u0641\u0647 \u062A\u0645\u0627\u0633 \u0628\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062F.</div>' : ""}
