        <a href="/register" class="text-gold/80 hover:text-gold">\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u062F\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647</a>
        <span class="mx-1.5 text-cream/20">|</span>
        <a href="/login" class="hover:text-gold transition">\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u067E\u0631\u0633\u0646\u0644</a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  const scripts = `
  (function () {
    var phoneInput = $('#phone');
    var codeInput = $('#code');
    var stepPhone = $('#step-phone');
    var stepCode = $('#step-code');
    var msg = $('#otp-message');
    var sendBtn = $('#send-code');
    var verifyBtn = $('#verify-code');
    var resendBtn = $('#resend');
    var resendTimer = $('#resend-timer');
    var timerValue = $('#timer-value');
    var countdown = null;
    var secondsLeft = 0;
    var currentPhone = '';
    var form = $('#otp-form');

    function showMessage(text, tone) {
      msg.textContent = text;
      msg.className = 'mt-3 text-[11.5px] leading-6 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 ' +
        (tone === 'error'
          ? 'border border-rose-500/25 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-200'
          : 'border border-emerald-500/25 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-200');
    }

    function normalizePhone(value) {
      var v = String(value || '').replace(/[\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, function (d) {
        return String('\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9'.indexOf(d));
      });
      v = v.replace(/[^0-9+]/g, '');
      if (v.indexOf('+98') === 0) v = '0' + v.slice(3);
      else if (v.indexOf('0098') === 0) v = '0' + v.slice(4);
      else if (v.indexOf('98') === 0 && v.length === 12) v = '0' + v.slice(2);
      else if (v.length === 10 && v.charAt(0) === '9') v = '0' + v;
      return v;
    }

    function startTimer(seconds) {
      secondsLeft = seconds;
      resendBtn.disabled = true;
      resendTimer.classList.remove('hidden');
      timerValue.textContent = fa(secondsLeft);
      if (countdown) clearInterval(countdown);
      countdown = setInterval(function () {
        secondsLeft -= 1;
        if (secondsLeft <= 0) {
          clearInterval(countdown);
          resendBtn.disabled = false;
          resendTimer.classList.add('hidden');
          return;
        }
        timerValue.textContent = fa(secondsLeft);
      }, 1000);
    }

    async function sendCode() {
      var phone = normalizePhone(phoneInput.value);
      if (!/^09\\d{9}$/.test(phone)) {
        showMessage('\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644 \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u0627 \u06F0\u06F9 \u0634\u0631\u0648\u0639 \u0634\u0648\u062F \u0648 \u06F1\u06F1 \u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.', 'error');
        phoneInput.focus();
        return;
      }
      currentPhone = phone;
      setLoading(sendBtn, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644\u2026');
      try {
        var data = await api('/api/auth/otp/request', { method: 'POST', body: { phone: phone } });
        stepPhone.classList.add('hidden');
        stepCode.classList.remove('hidden');
        codeInput.value = '';
        codeInput.focus();
        showMessage(
          data && data.queued ? '\u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A \u06A9\u062F \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06CC \u0628\u0647 \u067E\u0631\u0633\u0646\u0644 \u0627\u0631\u062C\u0627\u0639 \u0634\u062F\u061B \u0628\u0627 \u067E\u0631\u0633\u0646\u0644 \u062A\u0645\u0627\u0633 \u0628\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062F. \u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u06A9\u062F \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u06F2 \u062F\u0642\u06CC\u0642\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.' : data && data.sent
            ? '\u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0634\u062F. \u0627\u06AF\u0631 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0631\u0627 \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0628\u06CC\u0646\u06CC\u062F\u060C \u067E\u0648\u0634\u0647 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062A\u0628\u0644\u06CC\u063A\u0627\u062A\u06CC \u0631\u0627 \u0647\u0645 \u0628\u0628\u06CC\u0646\u06CC\u062F.'
            : '\u0627\u06AF\u0631 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062F\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F\u060C \u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC\u062A\u0627\u0646 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.'
        );
        startTimer(60);
      } catch (error) {
        showMessage(error.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        setLoading(sendBtn, false);
      }
    }

    async function verifyCode() {
      var code = String(codeInput.value || '').replace(/[\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, function (d) {
        return String('\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9'.indexOf(d));
      }).replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
      if (code.length !== 6) {
        showMessage('\u06A9\u062F \u06F6 \u0631\u0642\u0645\u06CC \u0631\u0627 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error');
        return;
      }
      setLoading(verifyBtn, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0628\u0631\u0631\u0633\u06CC\u2026');
      try {
        await api('/api/auth/otp/verify', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: { phone: currentPhone, code: code }
        });
        toast('\u062E\u0648\u0634 \u0622\u0645\u062F\u06CC\u062F \u2615', 'success');
        window.location.href = '/me';
      } catch (error) {
        showMessage(error.message, 'error');
        setLoading(verifyBtn, false);
      }
    }

    phoneInput.addEventListener('input', function () {
      var v = normalizePhone(phoneInput.value);
      phoneInput.value = v ? fa(v) : '';
    });

    form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      if (stepCode.classList.contains('hidden')) sendCode();
      else verifyCode();
    });

    resendBtn.addEventListener('click', sendCode);

    $('#change-phone').addEventListener('click', function () {
      stepCode.classList.add('hidden');
      stepPhone.classList.remove('hidden');
      msg.classList.add('hidden');
      if (countdown) clearInterval(countdown);
      phoneInput.focus();
    });
  })();`;
  return publicShell({
    title: "\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0628\u0647 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0645\u0646",
    body,
    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function customerPanelPage(settings, data, opts = {}) {
  const { user } = data;
  const percent = data.nextTier ? Math.max(4, Math.min(100, Math.round(data.nextTier.current / data.nextTier.target * 100))) : 100;
  const couponCards = data.activeCoupons.map((coupon) => {
    const value = coupon.kind === "percent" ? `${toPersianDigits(coupon.value)}\u066A \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641` : `${formatNumberFa(coupon.value)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641`;
    return `<div class="rounded-2xl border border-gold/20 bg-gold/[0.06] p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div class="min-w-0">
          <div class="font-mono text-[14px] font-bold tracking-wider text-amber-100" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(coupon.code)}</div>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/50 mt-1">${value}${coupon.expires_at ? ` \u2014 \u062A\u0627 ${formatJalali(coupon.expires_at)}` : ""}</div>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm shrink-0" data-copy="${escapeHtml(coupon.code)}">
          ${icon("copy", "w-4 h-4")}
          <span>\u06A9\u067E\u06CC</span>
        </button>
      </div>`;
  }).join("");
  const usedCoupons = data.coupons.filter((coupon) => !data.activeCoupons.some((active) => active.id === coupon.id)).slice(0, 5).map((coupon) => {
    const value = coupon.kind === "percent" ? `${toPersianDigits(coupon.value)}\u066A` : `${formatNumberFa(coupon.value)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646`;
    const reason = coupon.used_count >= coupon.max_uses ? "\u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647" : coupon.is_active !== 1 ? "\u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644" : "\u0645\u0646\u0642\u0636\u06CC";
    return `<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 border-b border-white/5 last:border-0">
        <span class="font-mono text-[11px] text-cream/35 line-through" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(coupon.code)}</span>
        <span class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/30">${value} \u2014 ${reason}</span>
      </div>`;
  }).join("");
  const history = data.invoices.map(
    (invoice) => `<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3 border-b border-white/5 last:border-0">
        <div class="min-w-0">
          <div class="text-[12px] font-medium">${formatNumberFa(invoice.amount)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646</div>
          <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/35 mt-0.5">${formatJalaliDateTime(invoice.created_at)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="text-end shrink-0">
          ${invoice.points_earned > 0 ? `<span class="badge tone-success">+${toPersianDigits(invoice.points_earned)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</span>` : '<span class="text-[10px] text-cream/25">\u0628\u062F\u0648\u0646 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</span>'}
          ${invoice.discount > 0 ? `<div class="text-[9.5px] text-emerald-300/60 mt-1">${formatNumberFa(invoice.discount)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641</div>` : ""}
        </div>
      </div>`
  ).join("");
  const redeemBox = settings.redeem_enabled === 1 ? `<div class="glass rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
            <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gold/10 border border-gold/20 text-gold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              ${icon("gift", "w-5 h-5")}
            </div>
            <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div class="text-[13px] font-bold">\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632\u062A \u0631\u0627 \u062E\u0631\u062C \u06A9\u0646</div>
              <p class="text-[11px] text-cream/45 mt-1 leading-6">
                ${toPersianDigits(settings.redeem_points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0647 \u06CC\u06A9 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641
                ${formatNumberFa(settings.redeem_value)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646\u06CC \u062A\u0628\u062F\u06CC\u0644 \u06A9\u0646
                (\u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 ${toPersianDigits(settings.redeem_expiry_days)} \u0631\u0648\u0632).
              </p>
              <button type="button" id="redeem-btn"
                class="btn btn-primary btn-sm mt-3"
                ${user.points < settings.redeem_points ? "disabled" : ""}>
                ${icon("gift", "w-4 h-4")}
                <span>${user.points < settings.redeem_points ? `${toPersianDigits(settings.redeem_points - user.points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062F\u06CC\u06AF\u0631 \u0644\u0627\u0632\u0645 \u062F\u0627\u0631\u06CC` : "\u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641"}</span>
              </button>
              ${opts.justRedeemed ? `<div class="mt-3 text-[11px] text-emerald-300">\u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0647 \u0634\u062F: <span class="font-mono" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(opts.justRedeemed)}</span></div>` : ""}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>` : "";
  const body = `
  <div class="p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
    <div class="glass rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-7 relative overflow-hidden mb-4">
      <div class="absolute -top-20 -left-16 w-56 h-56 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 blur-3xl pointer-events-none"></div>
      <div class="relative flex items-start justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap">
        <div>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/40">\u062E\u0648\u0634 \u0622\u0645\u062F\u06CC</div>
          <div class="text-lg font-extrabold mt-0.5">${escapeHtml(user.name)}</div>
          <div class="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-2.5 py-1 text-[10.5px] text-cream/60">
            <span>${TIER_EMOJI[user.tier] ?? "\u{1F949}"}</span>
            <span>\u0633\u0637\u062D ${tierLabel(user.tier)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="text-end">
          <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/40">\u0645\u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</div>
          <div class="text-3xl font-extrabold gold-text leading-tight mt-1">${formatNumberFa(user.points)}</div>
          <div class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/35 mt-1">\u0627\u0632 \u0645\u062C\u0645\u0648\u0639 ${formatNumberFa(user.lifetime_points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0633\u0628\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      ${data.nextTier ? `<div class="relative mt-5">
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 mb-1.5">
                <span>\u062A\u0627 \u0633\u0637\u062D ${data.nextTier.label}</span>
                <span>${formatNumberFa(data.nextTier.need)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647</span>
              </div>
              <div class="h-2 rounded-full bg-white/10 overflow-hidden">
                <div class="h-full rounded-full gold-bg" style="width:${percent}%"></div>
              </div>
            </div>` : '<div class="relative mt-5 text-[11px] text-amber-200/80">\u{1F3C6} \u0634\u0645\u0627 \u062F\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0644\u0627\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0646 \u0633\u0637\u062D \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0647\u0633\u062A\u06CC\u062F.</div>'}

      <div class="relative grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mt-5">
        <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/25 border border-white/5 p-3 text-center">
          <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/35">\u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u0647\u0627</div>
          <div class="text-[15px] font-bold mt-0.5">${formatNumberFa(data.visits)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/25 border border-white/5 p-3 text-center">

          <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/35">\u062F\u0639\u0648\u062A\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647\u200C\u0647\u0627</div>
          <div class="text-[15px] font-bold mt-0.5">${formatNumberFa(data.referrals)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="glass rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-6" id="referral">
        <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
          <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-gold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            ${icon("users", "w-5 h-5")}
          </div>
          <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div class="text-[13px] font-bold">\u062F\u0639\u0648\u062A \u062F\u0648\u0633\u062A\u0627\u0646</div>

              \u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641\u062A \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0647 \u062F\u0648\u0633\u062A\u0627\u0646\u062A \u0628\u062F\u0647\u061B \u0628\u0627 \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0646 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u0634\u0627\u0646
              ${toPersianDigits(settings.referral_reward_points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0647\u062F\u06CC\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC.
            </p>
            <div class="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <code class="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-black/25 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-[12.5px] tracking-wider text-amber-100 text-center" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(user.referral_code ?? "\u2014")}</code>
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-copy="${escapeHtml(user.referral_code ?? "")}">
                ${icon("copy", "w-4 h-4")}
                <span>\u06A9\u067E\u06CC</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <section class="mt-4 glass rounded-3xl overflow-hidden" id="coupons">
      <div class="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center gap-2">
        ${icon("gift", "w-4 h-4 text-gold")}
        <span class="text-[12.5px] font-bold">\u06A9\u062F\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0645\u0646</span>
        <span class="badge tone-muted ms-auto">${formatNumberFa(data.activeCoupons.length)} \u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644</span>
      </div>
      <div class="p-4 space-y-2.5">
        ${couponCards || '<p class="text-[11.5px] text-cream/35 text-center py-4">\u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644\u06CC \u0646\u062F\u0627\u0631\u06CC. \u0628\u0627 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632\u062A \u06CC\u06A9\u06CC \u0628\u0633\u0627\u0632.</p>'}
        ${usedCoupons ? `<div class="mt-2 rounded-2xl border border-white/5 overflow-hidden">${usedCoupons}</div>` : ""}
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="mt-4 glass rounded-3xl overflow-hidden" id="history">
      <div class="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center gap-2">
        ${icon("receipt", "w-4 h-4 text-gold")}
        <span class="text-[12.5px] font-bold">\u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0645\u0646</span>
      </div>
      ${history || emptyState({ title: "\u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647", message: "\u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0646 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u062A \u0631\u0627 \u0627\u0646\u062C\u0627\u0645 \u0628\u062F\u0647 \u062A\u0627 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0628\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC.", iconName: "receipt" })}
    </section>

    <section class="mt-4 glass rounded-3xl p-5" id="settings">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          ${icon("chat", "w-4 h-4 text-gold")}
          <div>
            <div class="text-[12.5px] font-bold">\u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062A\u0628\u0644\u06CC\u063A\u0627\u062A\u06CC</div>
            <div class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/40 mt-0.5">\u067E\u06CC\u0634\u0646\u0647\u0627\u062F\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0648\u06CC\u0698\u0647\u060C \u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F \u0648 \u062F\u0644\u062A\u0646\u06AF\u06CC</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <label class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
          <input type="checkbox" id="sms-opt-in" class="w-4 h-4 accent-amber-500" ${user.sms_opt_in === 1 ? "checked" : ""} />
          <span class="text-[11.5px] text-cream/60">\u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u06A9\u0646\u0645</span>
        </label>
      </div>
      <p class="text-[10px] text-cream/25 mt-3 leading-5">
        \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0631\u0633\u06CC\u062F \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F \u0648 \u06A9\u062F \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0647\u0645\u06CC\u0634\u0647 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F\u061B \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06AF\u0632\u06CC\u0646\u0647 \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0645\u0631\u0628\u0648\u0637 \u0628\u0647 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062A\u0628\u0644\u06CC\u063A\u0627\u062A\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A.
      </p>
    </section>

    <div class="text-center mt-6">
      <button type="button" onclick="logout()" class="text-[11px] text-rose-300/70 hover:text-rose-200 transition">
        \u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C \u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0633\u0627\u0628
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  const scripts = `
  (function () {
    var redeemBtn = $('#redeem-btn');
    if (redeemBtn) {
      redeemBtn.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        if (!confirm('\u0628\u0627 \u062A\u0628\u062F\u06CC\u0644 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632\u060C \u06CC\u06A9 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F. \u0627\u062F\u0627\u0645\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062F\u0647\u06CC\u061F')) return;
        setLoading(redeemBtn, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A \u06A9\u062F\u2026');
        try {
          var data = await api('/api/me/redeem', { method: 'POST' });
          toast('\u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0647 \u0634\u062F: ' + data.code, 'success');
          setTimeout(function () { window.location.reload(); }, 1200);
        } catch (error) {
          toast(error.message, 'error');
          setLoading(redeemBtn, false);
        }
      });
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-copy]').forEach(function (button) {
      button.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var code = button.getAttribute('data-copy');
        if (!code) return;
        if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
          navigator.clipboard.writeText(code).then(function () {
            toast('\u06A9\u067E\u06CC \u0634\u062F: ' + code, 'success');
          }).catch(function () {
            toast('\u06A9\u067E\u06CC \u0646\u0634\u062F\u061B \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06CC \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error');
          });
        } else {
          toast('\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0631\u06AF\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u067E\u06CC \u067E\u0634\u062A\u06CC\u0628\u0627\u0646\u06CC \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u062F.', 'error');
        }
      });
    });

    var optIn = $('#sms-opt-in');
    if (optIn) {
      optIn.addEventListener('change', async function () {
        var value = optIn.checked ? 1 : 0;
        try {
          await api('/api/me/sms-opt-in', { method: 'POST', body: { optIn: value } });
          toast(value ? '\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u067E\u0633 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u062A\u0628\u0644\u06CC\u063A\u0627\u062A\u06CC \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC.' : '\u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u062A\u0628\u0644\u06CC\u063A\u0627\u062A\u06CC \u062F\u06CC\u06AF\u0631 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.', 'success');
        } catch (error) {
          optIn.checked = !optIn.checked;
          toast(error.message, 'error');
        }
      });
    }
  })();`;
  return appShell({
    title: "\u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0645\u0646",
    role: "customer",
    userName: user.name,
    active: "home",
    body,
    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}

init_utils();
function landingPage(settings) {
  const cafe = escapeHtml(settings.cafe_name);
  const unit = formatNumberFa(settings.currency_unit);
  const ratio = toPersianDigits(settings.points_per_currency);
  const reward = toPersianDigits(settings.referral_reward_points);
  const birthday = toPersianDigits(settings.birthday_coupon_percent);
  const benefits = [
    {
      icon: "star",

    },

      icon: "gift",
      title: "\u0647\u062F\u06CC\u0647 \u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F",
      desc: `\u062F\u0631 \u0631\u0648\u0632 \u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F\u062A \u06CC\u06A9 \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 ${birthday} \u062F\u0631\u0635\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0635\u06CC \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC\u062A \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.`

          <h2 class="font-bold text-sm mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
            ${icon("gift", "w-4 h-4 text-gold")}
            \u0628\u0627 \u0639\u0636\u0648\u06CC\u062A \u0686\u0647 \u0686\u06CC\u0632\u06CC \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u061F
          </h2>
          <ul class="space-y-3.5">
            ${[
    settings.points_mode === "visit" ? `\u0647\u0631 \u0631\u0648\u0632 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F = ${toPersianDigits(settings.visit_points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 (\u062D\u062F\u0627\u0642\u0644 ${formatNumberFa(settings.visit_min_amount)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646)` : settings.points_mode === "invoice" ? `\u0647\u0631 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0627 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0645\u062B\u0628\u062A\u060C ${toPersianDigits(settings.invoice_points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632\u061B \u0645\u0633\u062A\u0642\u0644 \u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A` : `\u0628\u0647 \u0627\u0632\u0627\u06CC \u0647\u0631 ${formatNumberFa(settings.currency_unit)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u060C ${toPersianDigits(settings.points_per_currency)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632`,
    `\u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 ${toPersianDigits(settings.birthday_coupon_percent)} \u062F\u0631\u0635\u062F\u06CC \u062F\u0631 \u0631\u0648\u0632 \u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F`,
    `${toPersianDigits(settings.referral_reward_points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u067E\u0627\u062F\u0627\u0634 \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0647\u0631 \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641\u06CC \u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642`,
    "\u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A \u0622\u0628\u200C\u0648\u0647\u0648\u0627 \u0648 \u067E\u06CC\u0634\u0646\u0647\u0627\u062F\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0648\u06CC\u0698\u0647",
    "\u0627\u0631\u062A\u0642\u0627\u06CC \u0633\u0637\u062D \u0648 \u0645\u0632\u0627\u06CC\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0635\u06CC \u0647\u0631 \u0633\u0637\u062D"
  ].map(
    (item) => `<li class="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-[12.5px] text-cream/60 leading-6">
                  <span class="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-1">${icon("check", "w-3 h-3")}</span>
                  <span>${item}</span>
                </li>`
  ).join("")}
          </ul>
        </div>

        <div class="rounded-3xl border border-gold/20 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-900/25 to-transparent p-6">
          <div class="text-3xl mb-3">\u2615</div>
          <h3 class="font-bold text-sm mb-2">\u0633\u0637\u062D\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0639\u0636\u0648\u06CC\u062A</h3>
          <div class="space-y-2 text-[11.5px] text-cream/55">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span>\u{1F949} \u0628\u0631\u0646\u0632\u06CC</span><span>\u0634\u0631\u0648\u0639</span></div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span>\u{1F948} \u0646\u0642\u0631\u0647\u200C\u0627\u06CC</span><span>${toPersianDigits(settings.tiers.silver)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</span></div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span>\u{1F947} \u0637\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u06CC</span><span>${toPersianDigits(settings.tiers.gold)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</span></div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span>\u{1F48E} \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0627\u0633\u06CC</span><span>${toPersianDigits(settings.tiers.vip)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="glass rounded-3xl p-5 flex gap-3 items-start">
          <span class="text-gold mt-0.5">${icon("shield", "w-4 h-4")}</span>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-cream/40 leading-6">
            \u0627\u0637\u0644\u0627\u0639\u0627\u062A \u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F \u0648 \u062F\u0631 \u0627\u062E\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0631 \u0634\u062E\u0635 \u0633\u0648\u0645\u06CC \u0642\u0631\u0627\u0631 \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u062F.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Success modal -->
  <div id="success-modal" class="hidden modal-backdrop">
    <div class="modal-panel glass-strong rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 sm:p-7 relative overflow-hidden">
      <div class="absolute -top-16 -left-16 w-48 h-48 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/10 blur-3xl"></div>
      <div class="text-center relative">
        <div class="w-16 h-16 mx-auto rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-400/30 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
          ${icon("check", "w-7 h-7")}
        </div>
        <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold mb-1">\u062E\u0648\u0634 \u0622\u0645\u062F\u06CC\u062F! \u{1F389}</h2>
        <p class="text-xs text-cream/50 mb-5">\u0639\u0636\u0648\u06CC\u062A \u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0628\u0627 \u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642\u06CC\u062A \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="rounded-2xl border border-gold/25 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-800/25 via-coffee-800/30 to-black/30 p-5 relative overflow-hidden">
        <div class="absolute -right-8 -bottom-8 w-32 h-32 rounded-full bg-gold/10 blur-2xl"></div>
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg gold-bg text-coffee-950 flex items-center justify-center">${icon("coffee", "w-4 h-4")}</div>
            <span class="text-[11px] font-semibold">${cafe}</span>
          </div>
          <span class="badge tone-warning">\u{1F949} \u0628\u0631\u0646\u0632\u06CC</span>
        </div>
        <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/40 mb-1">\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC</div>
        <div id="s-name" class="font-bold text-sm mb-3">\u2014</div>
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/40 mb-1">\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0639\u0636\u0648\u06CC\u062A</div>
            <div id="s-phone" class="font-mono text-xs tracking-wider" dir="ltr">\u2014</div>
          </div>
          <div class="text-left">
            <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/40 mb-1">\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</div>
            <div id="s-points" class="font-extrabold gold-text text-lg">\u06F0</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-white/10">
          <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/40 mb-1.5">\u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641 \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0635\u06CC \u0634\u0645\u0627</div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <code id="s-referral" class="flex-1 text-xs font-mono tracking-widest bg-black/30 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-center" dir="ltr">\u2014</code>
            <button type="button" id="copy-ref" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" title="\u06A9\u067E\u06CC \u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641">${icon("copy", "w-4 h-4")}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-5 flex items-center gap-2">
        <button type="button" id="copy-link" class="btn btn-ghost flex-1 btn-sm">${icon("copy", "w-4 h-4")} \u06A9\u067E\u06CC \u0644\u06CC\u0646\u06A9 \u062F\u0639\u0648\u062A</button>
        <button type="button" id="register-again" class="btn btn-primary flex-1 btn-sm">${icon("plus", "w-4 h-4")} \u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0628\u0639\u062F\u06CC</button>
      </div>
      <a href="/" class="block text-center text-[11px] text-cream/40 hover:text-gold mt-4">\u0628\u0627\u0632\u06AF\u0634\u062A \u0628\u0647 \u0635\u0641\u062D\u0647 \u0627\u0635\u0644\u06CC</a>
    </div>
  </div>
  `;
  const scripts = `
  (function () {
    var daySel = $('#birth-day');
    var monSel = $('#birth-month');
    var yearSel = $('#birth-year');
    var form = $('#register-form');
    var submitBtn = $('#submit-btn');
    var modal = $('#success-modal');
    var lastReferral = '';

    initJalaliPickers(daySel, monSel, yearSel, '', 90);

    function normalizePhone(value) {
      var v = String(value || '').replace(/[\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, function (d) { return String('\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9'.indexOf(d)); });
      v = v.replace(/[\\s\\-()]/g, '');
      if (v.indexOf('+98') === 0) v = '0' + v.slice(3);
      else if (v.indexOf('0098') === 0) v = '0' + v.slice(4);
      else if (v.indexOf('98') === 0 && v.length === 12) v = '0' + v.slice(2);
      else if (v.indexOf('9') === 0 && v.length === 10) v = '0' + v;
      return v;
    }

    form.addEventListener('submit', async function (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      var name = $('#name').value.trim();
      var phone = normalizePhone($('#phone').value);
      var referral = $('#referral').value.trim();
      var terms = $('#terms').checked;
      var smsOptIn = $('#sms-opt-in').checked;

      if (name.length < 2) { toast('\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0631\u0627 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); $('#name').focus(); return; }
      if (!/^09\\d{9}$/.test(phone)) { toast('\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644 \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u0627 \u06F0\u06F9 \u0634\u0631\u0648\u0639 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0648 \u06F1\u06F1 \u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.', 'error'); $('#phone').focus(); return; }
      if (!terms) { toast('\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u062F\u0627\u0645\u0647\u060C \u0645\u0648\u0627\u0641\u0642\u062A \u0628\u0627 \u0642\u0648\u0627\u0646\u06CC\u0646 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0631\u0627 \u062A\u06CC\u06A9 \u0628\u0632\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); return; }

      var birth = readJalaliPickers(daySel, monSel, yearSel);
      if (!birth) { toast('\u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E \u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.', 'error'); return; }

      setLoading(submitBtn, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u062B\u0628\u062A\u2026');
      try {
        var data = await api('/api/auth/register', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: {
            name: name,
            phone: phone,
            birth_date: birth,
            referral: referral,
            sms_opt_in: smsOptIn ? 1 : 0
          }
        });
        lastReferral = data.referralCode;
        $('#s-name').textContent = data.name;
        $('#s-phone').textContent = data.phone;
        $('#s-points').textContent = fa(data.points);
        $('#s-referral').textContent = data.referralCode;
        modal.classList.remove('hidden');
        toast(data.referrerName ? ('\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642! \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641 \u0634\u0645\u0627: ' + data.referrerName) : '\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0628\u0627 \u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642\u06CC\u062A \u0627\u0646\u062C\u0627\u0645 \u0634\u062F.', 'success');
        form.reset();
        $('#referral').value = '';
        initJalaliPickers(daySel, monSel, yearSel, '', 90);
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        setLoading(submitBtn, false);
      }
    });

    $('#copy-ref').addEventListener('click', function () {
      if (lastReferral) copyText(lastReferral, '\u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641 \u06A9\u067E\u06CC \u0634\u062F.');
    });
    $('#copy-link').addEventListener('click', function () {
      var link = window.location.origin + '/register?ref=' + encodeURIComponent(lastReferral);
      copyText(link, '\u0644\u06CC\u0646\u06A9 \u062F\u0639\u0648\u062A \u06A9\u067E\u06CC \u0634\u062F.');
    });
    $('#register-again').addEventListener('click', function () {
      modal.classList.add('hidden');
      $('#name').focus();
    });
    modal.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      if (event.target === modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
    });

    $('#phone').addEventListener('input', function (event) {
      var pos = event.target.selectionStart;
      var normalized = event.target.value.replace(/[\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, function (d) { return String('\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9'.indexOf(d)); });
      if (normalized !== event.target.value) {
        event.target.value = normalized;
        try { event.target.setSelectionRange(pos, pos); } catch (e) {}
      }
    });
  })();
  `;
  return publicShell({
    title: "\u0639\u0636\u0648\u06CC\u062A",
    body,
    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function loginPage(settings, next, expired = false, notice = "", feedback) {
  const cafe = escapeHtml(settings.cafe_name);
  const safeNext = next.startsWith("/") && !next.startsWith("//") ? next : "";
  const noticeBox = (() => {
    if (notice === "loggedout") {
      return `<div class="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] p-3.5 text-[11px] text-cream/60 leading-6">
        \u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0633\u0627\u0628 \u062E\u0627\u0631\u062C \u0634\u062F\u06CC\u062F. \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u062F\u0627\u0645\u0647 \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u0634\u0648\u06CC\u062F.
      </div>`;
    }
    if (expired) {
      return `<div class="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/25 bg-amber-500/[0.07] p-3.5 flex items-start gap-2.5">
        <span class="text-amber-300 mt-0.5">${icon("clock", "w-3.5 h-3.5")}</span>
        <p class="text-[11px] text-amber-100/70 leading-6">
          \u0646\u0634\u0633\u062A \u0642\u0628\u0644\u06CC \u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0645\u0646\u0642\u0636\u06CC \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A (\u06CC\u0627 \u0631\u0645\u0632 \u0627\u0645\u0646\u06CC\u062A\u06CC \u0633\u0631\u0648\u0631 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0647).
          \u0644\u0637\u0641\u0627\u064B \u06CC\u06A9\u200C\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u062F\u06CC\u06AF\u0631 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u0634\u0648\u06CC\u062F.
        </p>
      </div>`;
    }
    return "";
  })();
  const body = `
  <div class="min-h-[calc(100vh-4rem)] flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-10">
    <div class="w-full max-w-4xl grid lg:grid-cols-2 rounded-3xl overflow-hidden glass">
      <!-- Art -->
      <div class="hidden lg:flex flex-col justify-between p-8 relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-900/30 via-coffee-900/40 to-black/40 border-e border-white/10">
        <div class="absolute -top-20 -right-20 w-64 h-64 rounded-full bg-gold/10 blur-3xl"></div>
        <div class="relative">
          <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl gold-bg text-coffee-950 flex items-center justify-center mb-6">
            ${icon("coffee", "w-6 h-6")}
          </div>
          <h2 class="text-2xl font-extrabold leading-relaxed mb-3">\u067E\u0646\u0644 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A<br /><span class="gold-text">\u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646</span></h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-cream/50 leading-7 max-w-xs">
            \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u060C \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632\u0647\u0627\u060C \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062E\u0648\u062F\u06A9\u0627\u0631 \u0648 \u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u2014 \u0647\u0645\u0647 \u062F\u0631 \u06CC\u06A9 \u062C\u0627.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div class="relative space-y-2.5 mt-10">
          ${[
    { icon: "receipt", text: "\u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u062F\u0631 \u0686\u0646\u062F \u062B\u0627\u0646\u06CC\u0647" },
    { icon: "users", text: "\u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646" },
    { icon: "chart", text: "\u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u0648 \u0631\u06CC\u0632\u0634" }
  ].map(
    (item) => `<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[11.5px] text-cream/55">
                <span class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-white/5 border border-white/10 flex items-center justify-center text-gold">${icon(item.icon, "w-3.5 h-3.5")}</span>
                ${item.text}
              </div>`
  ).join("")}
        </div>
        <div class="relative text-[10px] text-cream/30 mt-8">${cafe} \xB7 \u0646\u0633\u062E\u0647 \u06F1.\u06F0</div>
      </div>

      <!-- Form -->
      <div class="p-6 sm:p-9">
        <div class="lg:hidden flex items-center gap-3 mb-7">
          <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl gold-bg text-coffee-950 flex items-center justify-center">${icon("coffee", "w-5 h-5")}</div>
          <div>
            <div class="font-bold text-sm gold-text">${cafe}</div>
            <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/40">\u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="mb-7">
          <h1 class="text-xl font-extrabold mb-1.5">\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0628\u0647 \u067E\u0646\u0644</h1>
          <p class="text-[12px] text-cream/45">\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644 \u0648 \u0631\u0645\u0632 \u0639\u0628\u0648\u0631 \u062E\u0648\u062F \u0631\u0627 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.</p>
        </div>

        ${noticeBox}

        ${feedback ? `<div id="login-feedback" role="alert" class="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/25 p-3 text-xs leading-6">${escapeHtml(feedback.message)}</div>` : ""}
        <form id="login-form" method="post" action="/login" target="_top" class="space-y-4">

            </div>
          </div>

              <button type="button" id="toggle-pass" class="absolute left-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-8 h-8 rounded-lg text-cream/40 hover:text-gold hover:bg-white/5 flex items-center justify-center" aria-label="\u0646\u0645\u0627\u06CC\u0634 \u0631\u0645\u0632">
                ${icon("search", "w-4 h-4")}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

            <span>\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0628\u0647 \u067E\u0646\u0644</span>
          </button>
        </form>

        <div class="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-500/[0.05] p-4 flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <span class="text-amber-300/80 mt-0.5">${icon("shield", "w-3.5 h-3.5")}</span>
          <p class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 leading-6">
            \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0628\u062E\u0634 \u0645\u062E\u0635\u0648\u0635 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0645\u0646\u062F\u0627\u0646 \u0648 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u0627\u0646 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A. \u062F\u0631 \u0635\u0648\u0631\u062A \u0641\u0631\u0627\u0645\u0648\u0634\u06CC \u0631\u0645\u0632\u060C \u0628\u0627 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631 \u0627\u0635\u0644\u06CC \u062A\u0645\u0627\u0633 \u0628\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062F.

          </p>
        </div>

        <p class="text-center text-[11.5px] text-cream/40 mt-6">
          \u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u0639\u0636\u0648 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A\u06CC\u062F\u061F
          <a href="/register" class="text-gold hover:underline">\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC</a>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  `;
  const scripts = `
  (function () {
    var form = $('#login-form');
    var submitting = false;
    $('#toggle-pass').addEventListener('click', function () {
      var input = $('#password');
      input.type = input.type === 'password' ? 'text' : 'password';
    });
    form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
      if (submitting) { event.preventDefault(); return; }
      submitting = true;
      setLoading($('#login-btn'), true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F\u2026');
    });
    window.addEventListener('pageshow', function () {
      submitting = false;
      setLoading($('#login-btn'), false);
    });
  })();
  `;
  return publicShell({ title: "\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F", body, scripts, cafeName: settings.cafe_name });
}

          <div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">\u0633\u0637\u062D \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC</span><span id="r-tier" class="font-bold">\u2014</span></div>
          <div id="r-discount-row" class="hidden flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">\u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0627\u0639\u0645\u0627\u0644\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647</span><span id="r-discount" class="font-bold text-emerald-300">\u2014</span></div>
