  const scripts = `
  (function () {
    var MENU = ${jsonForScript(menu)};
    var HAS_OPEN_SHIFT = ${openShift2 ? "true" : "false"};
    var REQUIRE_SHIFT = ${requireShift ? "true" : "false"};
    var POINTS_UNIT = ${Number(settings.currency_unit) || 1e3};
    var POINTS_RATIO = ${Number(settings.points_per_currency) || 1};
    var POINTS_MODE = ${JSON.stringify(settings.points_mode)};
    var INVOICE_POINTS = ${Number(settings.invoice_points) || 0};
    var VISIT_POINTS = ${Number(settings.visit_points) || 0};
    var VISIT_MIN_AMOUNT = ${Number(settings.visit_min_amount) || 0};
    var TIER_LABELS = { bronze: '\u0628\u0631\u0646\u0632\u06CC', silver: '\u0646\u0642\u0631\u0647\u200C\u0627\u06CC', gold: '\u0637\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u06CC', vip: '\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0627\u0633\u06CC' };
    var TIER_CLASSES = { bronze: 'tone-warning', silver: 'tone-muted', gold: 'tone-warning', vip: 'tone-info' };

    var customer = null;
    var selected = Object.create(null);
    var cartItems = Object.create(null);
    var menuVisibleLimit = 40;
    var activeCategory = '__popular__';
    var appliedCoupon = null;
    var requestId = null;
    var appliedDiscount = 0;
    var searchQuery = '';
    var payMode = 'cash';
    var payTender = 0;        // \u067E\u0648\u0644 \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A\u06CC \u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644\u062A \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC/\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A/\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646
    var mixCash = 0;
    var mixCard = 0;
    var mixOnline = 0;
    var submitting = false;

    var phoneInput = $('#phone-input');
    var menuGrid = $('#menu-grid');
    var selectedList = $('#selected-list');
    var manualInput = $('#manual-amount');

    function normalizePhone(value) {
      var v = String(value || '').replace(/[\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, function (d) { return String('\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9'.indexOf(d)); });
      v = v.replace(/[\\s\\-()]/g, '');
      if (v.indexOf('+98') === 0) v = '0' + v.slice(3);
      else if (v.indexOf('0098') === 0) v = '0' + v.slice(4);
      else if (v.indexOf('98') === 0 && v.length === 12) v = '0' + v.slice(2);
      else if (v.indexOf('9') === 0 && v.length === 10) v = '0' + v;
      return v;
    }

    function paymentLabel(method) {
      return { cash: '\u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC', card: '\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A\u200C\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646', online: '\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646', mixed: '\u062A\u0631\u06A9\u06CC\u0628\u06CC' }[method] || '\u2014';
    }

    function latinDigits(value) {
      return String(value || '').replace(/[\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, function(d) { return String('\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9'.indexOf(d)); })
        .replace(/[\u0660-\u0669]/g, function(d) { return String('\u0660\u0661\u0662\u0663\u0664\u0665\u0666\u0667\u0668\u0669'.indexOf(d)); });
    }
    function cartKey(item) { return JSON.stringify([item.name, item.price]); }
    function parseAmount(value) {
      var digits = latinDigits(value).replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
      return Number(digits) || 0;
    }

    function subtotal() {
      var total = 0;
      Object.keys(selected).forEach(function (name) {
        var item = cartItems[name];
        if (item) total += item.price * selected[name];
      });
      return total;
    }

    function itemCount() {
      return Object.keys(selected).reduce(function (sum, key) { return sum + selected[key]; }, 0);
    }

    function matchesSearch(item, query) {
      if (!query) return true;
      var name = String(item.name || '').toLowerCase();
      var cat = String(item.category || '').toLowerCase();
      var q = String(query).toLowerCase().trim();
      if (!q) return true;
      return name.indexOf(q) !== -1 || cat.indexOf(q) !== -1;
    }

    function visibleMenu() {
      return MENU.filter(function (item) {
        var inCategory = (activeCategory === '__popular__' ? !!searchQuery.trim() : (item.category || '\u0633\u0627\u06CC\u0631') === activeCategory);
        return inCategory && matchesSearch(item, searchQuery);
      });
    }

    function renderMenu() {
      var allItems = visibleMenu();
      var items = allItems.slice(0, menuVisibleLimit);
      $('#menu-more').classList.toggle('hidden', allItems.length <= menuVisibleLimit);
      $('#menu-results').textContent = allItems.length ? fa(allItems.length) + ' \u0645\u062D\u0635\u0648\u0644\u061B \u0646\u0645\u0627\u06CC\u0634 ' + fa(items.length) + ' \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F' : (searchQuery || activeCategory !== '__popular__' ? '\u0645\u062D\u0635\u0648\u0644\u06CC \u067E\u06CC\u062F\u0627 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u061B \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0646\u06CC\u062F \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06CC \u0627\u0636\u0627\u0641\u0647 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.' : '\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u062F\u06CC\u062F\u0646 \u0645\u062D\u0635\u0648\u0644\u0627\u062A \u062F\u0633\u062A\u0647 \u0631\u0627 \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F\u061B \u06CC\u0627 \u0627\u0632 \u067E\u0631\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634\u200C\u0647\u0627 \u0648 \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.');
      if (items.length === 0) {
        menuGrid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-[11.5px] text-cream/40">' +
          (activeCategory === '__popular__' && !searchQuery ? '' : '\u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645 \u0631\u0627 \u0627\u0632 \u0628\u062E\u0634 \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06CC \u0627\u0636\u0627\u0641\u0647 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.') + '</div>';
        return;
      }
      menuGrid.innerHTML = items.map(function (item) {
        var qty = selected[cartKey(item)] || 0;
        return '<button type="button" data-menu-index="' + MENU.indexOf(item) + '" class="relative text-right rounded-2xl border p-3 transition ' +
          (qty > 0 ? 'border-gold/50 bg-gold/10' : 'border-white/10 bg-white/[0.03] hover:border-white/20 hover:bg-white/[0.06]') + '">' +
          (qty > 0 ? '<span class="absolute -top-1.5 -left-1.5 w-5 h-5 rounded-full gold-bg text-coffee-950 text-[10px] font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center">' + fa(qty) + '</span>' : '') +
          '<div class="text-[12.5px] font-semibold mb-1.5">' + escapeHtml(item.name) + '</div>' +
          '<div class="text-[11px] text-cream/45">' + moneyFa(item.price) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646</div>' +
        '</button>';
      }).join('');
    }

    function renderSelected() {
      var keys = Object.keys(selected);
      $('#selected-empty').classList.toggle('hidden', keys.length > 0);
      $('#selected-count').textContent = fa(itemCount()) + ' \u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645';

      selectedList.innerHTML = keys.map(function (name) {
        var item = cartItems[name];
        if (!item) return '';
        return '<div class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.03] px-3 py-2">' +
          '<div class="flex-1 min-w-0"><div class="text-[12px] font-medium truncate">' + escapeHtml(item.name) + '</div>' +
          '<div class="text-[10px] text-cream/40">' + moneyFa(item.price) + ' \xD7 ' + fa(selected[name]) + '</div></div>' +
          '<div class="flex items-center gap-1">' +
            '<button type="button" data-dec="' + escapeHtml(name) + '" class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-cream/70 flex items-center justify-center text-sm">\u2212</button>' +
            '<span class="w-8 text-center text-[12px] font-bold">' + fa(selected[name]) + '</span>' +
            '<button type="button" data-inc="' + escapeHtml(name) + '" class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-gold/15 hover:bg-gold/25 text-gold flex items-center justify-center text-sm">+</button>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<button type="button" data-rm="' + escapeHtml(name) + '" class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg text-cream/30 hover:text-rose-300 hover:bg-rose-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">\xD7</button>' +
        '</div>';
      }).join('');
    }

    /** \u0627\u0641\u0632\u0648\u062F\u0646 \u06CC\u06A9 \u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645 (\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06A9 \u0628\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u06A9 \u0648 \u0645\u06CC\u0627\u0646\u200C\u0628\u0631 \u0635\u0641\u062D\u0647\u200C\u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F) */
    function addItem(name) {
      var item = MENU.find(function(m) { return m.name === name; });
      if (item) addCartItem(item, 1);
    }
    function addCartItem(item, qty) {
      var key = cartKey(item);
      if (!selected[key] && Object.keys(selected).length >= 50) { toast('\u062D\u062F\u0627\u06A9\u062B\u0631 \u06F5\u06F0 \u0631\u062F\u06CC\u0641 \u062F\u0631 \u0647\u0631 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631.', 'error'); return false; }
      if ((selected[key] || 0) + qty > 99) { toast('\u062D\u062F\u0627\u06A9\u062B\u0631 \u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u0647\u0631 \u0631\u062F\u06CC\u0641 \u06F9\u06F9 \u0627\u0633\u062A.', 'error'); return false; }
      cartItems[key] = {name:item.name,price:item.price};
      selected[key] = (selected[key] || 0) + qty;
      resetCoupon(); renderMenu(); renderSelected(); refreshTotals();
      return true;
    }
    function addCustomItem() {
      var name = $('#custom-name').value.replace(/[<>]/g, '').trim();
      var priceText = latinDigits($('#custom-price').value).replace(/[,\u060C\u066C\\s]/g, '');
      var qtyText = latinDigits($('#custom-qty').value).trim();
      var price = Number(priceText), qty = Number(qtyText);
      if (!name || name.length > 60) { toast('\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645 \u0631\u0627 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F (\u062D\u062F\u0627\u06A9\u062B\u0631 \u06F6\u06F0 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0631).','error'); return; }
      if (!priceText || !Number.isSafeInteger(price) || price <= 0 || price > 1000000000) { toast('\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u062A \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0639\u062F\u062F \u0635\u062D\u06CC\u062D \u0645\u062B\u0628\u062A \u0628\u0647 \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.','error'); return; }
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(qty) || qty < 1 || qty > 99) { toast('\u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0639\u062F\u062F \u0635\u062D\u06CC\u062D \u0628\u06CC\u0646 \u06F1 \u062A\u0627 \u06F9\u06F9 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.','error'); return; }
      if (addCartItem({name:name,price:price},qty)) {
        $('#custom-name').value=''; $('#custom-price').value=''; $('#custom-qty').value='1'; $('#custom-name').focus();
      }
    }
    $('#custom-add').addEventListener('click', addCustomItem);
    ['custom-name','custom-price','custom-qty'].forEach(function(id) {
      document.getElementById(id).addEventListener('keydown',function(e) { if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();addCustomItem();} });
    });
    $('#menu-more').addEventListener('click',function(){menuVisibleLimit+=40;renderMenu();});

    function currentTotal() {
      return refreshTotals().total;
    }

    function parseMoneyInput(el) {
      return parseAmount(el.value);
    }

    function setMoneyInput(el, value) {
      el.value = value > 0 ? money(value) : '';
    }

    /** \u0645\u062D\u0627\u0633\u0628\u0647\u0654 \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u0627\u0632 \u0631\u0648\u06CC \u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A \u0641\u0639\u0644\u06CC \u0648 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u06A9\u0644 */
    function computePayment(total) {
      if (payMode === 'mixed') {
        var cash = mixCash;
        var card = mixCard;
        var online = mixOnline;
        var tendered = cash + card + online;
        var change = Math.max(0, tendered - total);
        var short = Math.max(0, total - tendered);
        return {
          cash: cash, card: card, online: online,
          tendered: tendered, change: change, short: short,
          valid: short === 0 && change <= cash,
          error: short > 0
            ? '\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A\u06CC ' + moneyFa(short) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u06A9\u0645\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.'
            : (change > cash ? '\u0628\u0627\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647 \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F \u0628\u06CC\u0634\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u067E\u0648\u0644 \u0646\u0642\u062F \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A\u06CC \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.' : '')
        };
      }

      var tender = payTender > 0 ? payTender : total;
      var ch = Math.max(0, tender - total);
      if (tender < total) {
        return {
          cash: payMode === 'cash' ? tender : 0,
          card: payMode === 'card' ? tender : 0,
          online: payMode === 'online' ? tender : 0,
          tendered: tender, change: 0, short: total - tender, valid: false,
          error: '\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A\u06CC ' + moneyFa(total - tender) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u06A9\u0645\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.'
        };
      }
      if (payMode !== 'cash' && ch > 0) {
        return {
          cash: 0,
          card: payMode === 'card' ? tender : 0,
          online: payMode === 'online' ? tender : 0,
          tendered: tender, change: ch, short: 0, valid: false,
          error: '\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A/\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u062F\u0642\u06CC\u0642\u0627\u064B \u0628\u0631\u0627\u0628\u0631 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.'
        };
      }
      return {
        cash: payMode === 'cash' ? tender - ch : 0,
        card: payMode === 'card' ? tender : 0,
        online: payMode === 'online' ? tender : 0,
        tendered: tender, change: ch, short: 0, valid: true, error: ''
      };
    }

    function renderPayment(total) {
      var simple = $('#pay-simple');
      var mixed = $('#pay-mixed');
      var changeRow = $('#pay-change-row');
      var changeEl = $('#pay-change');
      var errorEl = $('#pay-error');
      var label = $('#pay-simple-label');
      var hint = $('#payment-hint');

      document.querySelectorAll('.pay-tab').forEach(function (tab) {
        var active = tab.dataset.pay === payMode;
        tab.className = 'pay-tab btn btn-sm ' + (active ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-ghost');
      });

      if (payMode === 'mixed') {
        simple.classList.add('hidden');
        mixed.classList.remove('hidden');
        hint.textContent = '\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u06A9\u0644: ' + moneyFa(total);
      } else {
        simple.classList.remove('hidden');
        mixed.classList.add('hidden');
        hint.textContent = payMode === 'cash' ? '\u0628\u0627\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647 \u062E\u0648\u062F\u06A9\u0627\u0631 \u062D\u0633\u0627\u0628 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F' : '\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u062F\u0642\u06CC\u0642 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631';
        label.textContent = payMode === 'cash'
          ? '\u067E\u0648\u0644 \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A\u06CC \u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC (\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646)'
          : (payMode === 'card' ? '\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A\u200C\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646 (\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646)' : '\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646 (\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646)');

        // \u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644\u062A \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A/\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0647\u0645\u06CC\u0634\u0647 \u062F\u0642\u06CC\u0642\u0627\u064B \u0628\u0631\u0627\u0628\u0631 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A
        if (payMode !== 'cash') {
          payTender = total;
          setMoneyInput($('#pay-cash-input'), total);
          $('#pay-cash-input').readOnly = true;
          $('#pay-tender-chips') && $('#pay-tender-chips').classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
          $('#pay-cash-input').readOnly = false;
          if (!payTender) payTender = total;
        }
      }

      var result = computePayment(total);

      if (result.change > 0) {
        changeRow.classList.remove('hidden');
        changeEl.textContent = moneyFa(result.change) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646';
      } else {
        changeRow.classList.add('hidden');
      }

      if (!result.valid && result.error && total > 0) {
        errorEl.textContent = result.error;
        errorEl.classList.remove('hidden');
      } else {
        errorEl.textContent = '';
        errorEl.classList.add('hidden');
      }

      return result;
    }

    function refreshTotals() {
      var sub = subtotal();
      var manual = parseAmount(manualInput.value);
      var base = itemCount() > 0 ? sub : manual;
      var discount = 0;

      if (appliedCoupon && appliedCoupon.code) {
        // \u0627\u06AF\u0631 \u06A9\u062F \u0627\u0639\u0645\u0627\u0644\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0628\u0627 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F \u0647\u0645\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A\u060C \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0628\u0631\u0631\u0633\u06CC \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u0645\u061B \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0633\u0642\u0641 \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0631\u0627 \u0631\u0639\u0627\u06CC\u062A \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u0645
        discount = appliedDiscount;
        if (discount > base) discount = base;
      }

      var total = Math.max(0, base - discount);
      var points = 0;
      var note = '';
      if (POINTS_MODE === 'invoice') {
        points = total > 0 ? INVOICE_POINTS : 0; note = '\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062B\u0627\u0628\u062A \u0647\u0631 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u061B \u0645\u0633\u062A\u0642\u0644 \u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A';
      } else if (POINTS_MODE === 'visit') {
        if (total < VISIT_MIN_AMOUNT) {
          points = 0;
          note = '\u06A9\u0645\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u062D\u062F\u0627\u0642\u0644 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0631\u0648\u0632\u0627\u0646\u0647';
        } else if (customer && customer.visit_earned_today) {
          points = 0;
          note = '\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0636\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0642\u0628\u0644\u0627\u064B \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F\u0647';
        } else {
          points = VISIT_POINTS;
          note = '\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0636\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632';
        }
      } else {
        points = Math.floor(total / POINTS_UNIT) * POINTS_RATIO;
        note = points > 0 ? '\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u0627\u0633 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F' : '';
      }
      var pointsNoteEl = $('#sum-points-note');
      if (pointsNoteEl) pointsNoteEl.textContent = note;

      $('#sum-subtotal').textContent = moneyFa(base) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646';
      $('#sum-discount').textContent = moneyFa(discount) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646';
      $('#sum-total').textContent = moneyFa(total) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646';
      $('#sum-points').textContent = '+' + fa(points);
      manualInput.disabled = itemCount() > 0;
      if (itemCount() > 0) manualInput.value = '';

      // \u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062A\u06A9\u200C\u0631\u0648\u0634\u06CC\u060C \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u0628\u0627 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0647\u0645\u200C\u062A\u0631\u0627\u0632 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F
      if (payMode !== 'mixed') {
        if (payMode !== 'cash' || payTender < total) {
          payTender = total;
        }
        var tenderInput = $('#pay-cash-input');
        if (tenderInput && document.activeElement !== tenderInput) {
          setMoneyInput(tenderInput, payTender);
        }
      }
      renderPayment(total);

      return { base: base, discount: discount, total: total, points: points };
    }

    function setCustomer(data) {
      customer = data;
      $('#not-found').classList.add('hidden');
      $('#customer-card').classList.remove('hidden');
      $('#c-avatar').textContent = (data.name || '\u061F').slice(0, 1);
      $('#c-name').textContent = data.name;
      $('#c-phone').textContent = fa(data.phone);
      $('#c-points').textContent = fa(data.points);
      $('#c-last').textContent = data.last_visit_date ? formatJalaliClient(data.last_visit_date) : '\u0628\u062F\u0648\u0646 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F';
      $('#c-avg').textContent = data.avg_visit_interval > 0 ? fa(Math.round(data.avg_visit_interval)) + ' \u0631\u0648\u0632' : '\u2014';
      var tierBadge = $('#c-tier');
      tierBadge.textContent = TIER_LABELS[data.tier] || '\u0628\u0631\u0646\u0632\u06CC';
      tierBadge.className = 'badge ' + (TIER_CLASSES[data.tier] || 'tone-muted');
      var status = $('#c-status');
      status.textContent = data.status_label || '\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644';
      status.className = 'badge ' + (data.status_tone === 'danger' ? 'tone-danger' : data.status_tone === 'warning' ? 'tone-warning' : data.status_tone === 'muted' ? 'tone-muted' : 'tone-success');

      var visitEl = $('#c-visit');
      if (data.points_mode === 'visit') {
        visitEl.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (data.visit_earned_today) {
          visitEl.className = 'mt-2.5 text-[10.5px] rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 border-amber-500/25 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-200';
          visitEl.textContent = '\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0636\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0631\u0627 \u06AF\u0631\u0641\u062A\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u0639\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0646\u062F\u0627\u0631\u062F.';
        } else {
          visitEl.className = 'mt-2.5 text-[10.5px] rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1.5 border-emerald-500/25 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-200';
          visitEl.textContent = '\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0636\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647\u061B \u0628\u0627 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F ' + fa(data.visit_points) + ' \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u062F.';
        }
      } else {
        visitEl.classList.add('hidden');
        visitEl.textContent = '';
      }

      var couponHost = $('#c-coupons');
      if (data.coupons && data.coupons.length > 0) {
        couponHost.classList.remove('hidden');
        couponHost.innerHTML = data.coupons.map(function (c) {
          return '<button type="button" data-coupon="' + escapeHtml(c.code) + '" class="badge tone-info hover:brightness-125 transition" title="\u0627\u0639\u0645\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u062F">' +
            '${icon("gift", "w-3 h-3")}<span class="font-mono" dir="ltr">' + escapeHtml(c.code) + '</span></button>';
        }).join('');
      } else {
        couponHost.classList.add('hidden');
        couponHost.innerHTML = '';
      }
    }

    function clearCustomer() {
      customer = null;
      $('#customer-card').classList.add('hidden');
      $('#not-found').classList.add('hidden');
      $('#search-state').innerHTML = '';
      phoneInput.value = '';
      phoneInput.focus();
    }

    async function searchCustomer() {
      var phone = normalizePhone(phoneInput.value);
      if (!/^09\\d{9}$/.test(phone)) {
        toast('\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644 \u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F (\u06F0\u06F9xxxxxxxxx).', 'error');
        phoneInput.focus();
        return;
      }
      phoneInput.value = phone;
      $('#search-state').innerHTML =
        '<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-cream/40"><span class="w-4 h-4 rounded-full border-2 border-gold border-t-transparent spin"></span> \u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648\u2026</div>';
      try {
        var data = await api('/api/staff/customer?phone=' + encodeURIComponent(phone));
        $('#search-state').innerHTML = '';
        if (data.found) {
          setCustomer(data.customer);
        } else {
          customer = null;
          $('#customer-card').classList.add('hidden');
          $('#not-found').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
      } catch (error) {
        $('#search-state').innerHTML = '';
        toast(error.message, 'error');
      }
    }

    /** \u0628\u0627 \u0647\u0631 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u062F\u0631 \u0633\u0628\u062F\u060C \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0627\u0639\u0645\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u0648\u062F */
    function resetCoupon() {
      appliedCoupon = null;
      appliedDiscount = 0;
      var input = $('#coupon-input');
      if (input) input.value = '';
      var result = $('#coupon-result');
      if (result) {
        result.textContent = '';
        result.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    }

    function resetForm() {
      selected = Object.create(null); cartItems = Object.create(null);
      appliedCoupon = null;
      appliedDiscount = 0;
      searchQuery = '';
      payMode = 'cash';
      payTender = 0;
      mixCash = 0;
      mixCard = 0;
      mixOnline = 0;
      if ($('#menu-search')) $('#menu-search').value = '';
      if ($('#mix-cash')) { $('#mix-cash').value = ''; $('#mix-card').value = ''; $('#mix-online').value = ''; }
      $('#coupon-input').value = '';
      $('#coupon-result').classList.add('hidden');
      manualInput.value = '';
      renderMenu();
      renderSelected();
      refreshTotals();
    }

    // Events
    $('#search-btn').addEventListener('click', searchCustomer);
    phoneInput.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      if (event.key === 'Enter') { event.preventDefault(); searchCustomer(); }
    });
    phoneInput.addEventListener('input', function () {
      var clean = phoneInput.value.replace(/[\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, function (d) { return String('\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9'.indexOf(d)); });
      if (clean !== phoneInput.value) phoneInput.value = clean;
    });
    $('#clear-customer').addEventListener('click', clearCustomer);
    $('#go-register').addEventListener('click', function () {
      var phone = normalizePhone(phoneInput.value);
      window.location.href = '/register' + (/^09\\d{9}$/.test(phone) ? '?phone=' + encodeURIComponent(phone) : '');
    });

    $('#category-tabs').addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var btn = event.target.closest('[data-cat]');
      if (!btn) return;
      activeCategory = btn.dataset.cat;
      menuVisibleLimit = 40; searchQuery = ''; $('#menu-search').value = '';
      $$('.cat-tab').forEach(function (tab) {
        tab.className = 'cat-tab btn btn-sm shrink-0 ' + (tab.dataset.cat === activeCategory ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-ghost');
      });
      renderMenu();
    });

    var menuSearch = $('#menu-search');
    if (menuSearch) {
      menuSearch.addEventListener('input', function () {
        searchQuery = menuSearch.value; menuVisibleLimit = 40;
        renderMenu();
      });
      menuSearch.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
        if (event.key === 'Enter') {
          event.preventDefault();
          var first = visibleMenu()[0];
          if (first) {
            addCartItem(first, 1);
            menuSearch.value = '';
            searchQuery = '';
            renderMenu();
          }
        } else if (event.key === 'Escape') {
          menuSearch.value = '';
          searchQuery = '';
          renderMenu();
        }
      });
    }

    var popularWrap = $('#popular-wrap');
    if (popularWrap) {
      popularWrap.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        var chip = event.target.closest('[data-quick]');
        if (chip) addItem(chip.dataset.quick);
      });
    }

    // --- \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A ---
    $('#payment-modes').addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var tab = event.target.closest('[data-pay]');
      if (!tab) return;
      payMode = tab.dataset.pay;
      if (payMode === 'mixed') {
        var total = currentTotal();
        var paid = mixCash + mixCard + mixOnline;
        if (paid === 0) { mixCash = total; setMoneyInput($('#mix-cash'), mixCash); }
      } else {
        payTender = currentTotal();
        setMoneyInput($('#pay-cash-input'), payTender);
      }
      refreshTotals();
    });

    var tenderInput = $('#pay-cash-input');
    tenderInput.addEventListener('input', function () {
      var digits = tenderInput.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
      tenderInput.value = digits ? money(digits) : '';
      payTender = Number(digits) || 0;
      renderPayment(currentTotal());
    });
    tenderInput.addEventListener('focus', function () { tenderInput.select(); });

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-tender]').forEach(function (chip) {
      chip.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var total = currentTotal();
        var kind = chip.dataset.tender;
        if (kind === 'exact') payTender = total;
        else if (kind === 'round') payTender = Math.ceil(total / 10000) * 10000;
        else payTender = total + Number(kind);
        setMoneyInput($('#pay-cash-input'), payTender);
        renderPayment(total);
      });
    });

    ['#mix-cash', '#mix-card', '#mix-online'].forEach(function (selector) {
      var el = $(selector);
      if (!el) return;
      el.addEventListener('input', function () {
        var digits = el.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
        el.value = digits ? money(digits) : '';
        var value = Number(digits) || 0;
        if (selector === '#mix-cash') mixCash = value;
        else if (selector === '#mix-card') mixCard = value;
        else mixOnline = value;
        renderPayment(currentTotal());
      });
    });

    var fillCash = $('#mix-fill-cash');
    if (fillCash) {
      fillCash.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var total = currentTotal();
        mixCash = Math.max(0, total - mixCard - mixOnline);
        setMoneyInput($('#mix-cash'), mixCash);
        renderPayment(total);
      });
    }

    menuGrid.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var btn = event.target.closest('[data-menu-index]');
      if (!btn) return;
      var item = MENU[Number(btn.dataset.menuIndex)];
      if (item) addCartItem(item, 1);
    });

    selectedList.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var inc = event.target.closest('[data-inc]');
      var dec = event.target.closest('[data-dec]');
      var rm = event.target.closest('[data-rm]');
      if (inc) { if (selected[inc.dataset.inc] >= 99) { toast('\u062D\u062F\u0627\u06A9\u062B\u0631 \u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u06F9\u06F9 \u0627\u0633\u062A.', 'error'); return; } selected[inc.dataset.inc] += 1; }
      else if (dec) {
        var name = dec.dataset.dec;
        selected[name] -= 1;
        if (selected[name] <= 0) delete selected[name];
      } else if (rm) {
        delete selected[rm.dataset.rm];
      } else return;
      resetCoupon();
      renderMenu();
      renderSelected();
      refreshTotals();
    });

    $('#clear-items').addEventListener('click', function () {
      selected = Object.create(null); cartItems = Object.create(null);
      resetCoupon();
      renderMenu();
      renderSelected();
      refreshTotals();
    });

    manualInput.addEventListener('input', function () {
      var digits = latinDigits(manualInput.value).replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
      manualInput.value = digits ? money(digits) : '';
      resetCoupon();
      refreshTotals();
    });

    $('#apply-coupon').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      if (!customer) { toast('\u0627\u0648\u0644 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0631\u0627 \u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); return; }
      var code = $('#coupon-input').value.trim();
      if (!code) { toast('\u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0631\u0627 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); return; }
      var base = subtotal() || parseAmount(manualInput.value);
      if (base <= 0) { toast('\u0627\u0648\u0644 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u06CC\u0627 \u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645\u200C\u0647\u0627 \u0631\u0627 \u0645\u0634\u062E\u0635 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); return; }

      var btn = $('#apply-coupon');
      setLoading(btn, true, '');
      try {
        var data = await api('/api/staff/coupons/check?code=' + encodeURIComponent(code) +
          '&userId=' + customer.id + '&subtotal=' + base);
        if (!data.valid) {
          appliedCoupon = null;
          appliedDiscount = 0;
          $('#coupon-result').className = 'mt-2 text-[11px] text-rose-300';
          $('#coupon-result').textContent = data.error || '\u06A9\u062F \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.';
          $('#coupon-result').classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
          appliedCoupon = { code: code.toUpperCase() };
          appliedDiscount = data.discount;
          $('#coupon-result').className = 'mt-2 text-[11px] text-emerald-300';
          $('#coupon-result').textContent = '\u06A9\u062F \u0627\u0639\u0645\u0627\u0644 \u0634\u062F \u2014 \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 ' + moneyFa(data.discount) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646';
          $('#coupon-result').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        refreshTotals();
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
      } finally {
        setLoading(btn, false);
      }
    });

    $('#c-coupons').addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var btn = event.target.closest('[data-coupon]');
      if (!btn) return;
      $('#coupon-input').value = btn.dataset.coupon;
      $('#apply-coupon').click();
    });

    async function submitInvoice() {
      if (submitting) return;
      if (!customer) { toast('\u0627\u0628\u062A\u062F\u0627 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0631\u0627 \u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648 \u0648 \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); phoneInput.focus(); return; }
      var totals = refreshTotals();
      if (totals.base <= 0) { toast('\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u06CC\u0634\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u0635\u0641\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.', 'error'); return; }

      var payment = computePayment(totals.total);
      if (!payment.valid) {
        toast(payment.error || '\u0627\u0637\u0644\u0627\u0639\u0627\u062A \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      // \u0628\u0627 \xAB\u0627\u0644\u0632\u0627\u0645 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\xBB\u060C \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC \u0628\u062F\u0648\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F
      if (REQUIRE_SHIFT && !HAS_OPEN_SHIFT && payment.cash > 0) {
        toast('\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u0648\u0644 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      var items = Object.keys(selected).map(function (name) {
        var item = cartItems[name];
        return { name: item.name, qty: selected[name], price: item.price };
      });
      var btn = $('#submit-invoice');
      submitting = true;
      setLoading(btn, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u062B\u0628\u062A\u2026');
      if (!requestId) {
        requestId = (window.crypto && window.crypto.randomUUID)
          ? window.crypto.randomUUID()
          : String(Date.now()) + '-' + Math.random().toString(16).slice(2);
      }
      try {
        var result = await api('/api/staff/invoices', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: {
            userId: customer.id,
            items: items,
            amount: items.length ? null : totals.base,
            couponCode: appliedCoupon ? appliedCoupon.code : null,
            clientRequestId: requestId,
            payment: { cash: payment.cash, card: payment.card, online: payment.online }
          }
        });
        requestId = null;
        $('#r-payment').textContent = paymentLabel(result.paymentMethod) +
          (result.change > 0 ? ' (' + moneyFa(result.change) + ' \u0628\u0627\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647)' : '');
        if (result.change > 0) {
          $('#r-change-row').classList.remove('hidden');
          $('#r-change').textContent = moneyFa(result.change) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646';
        } else {
          $('#r-change-row').classList.add('hidden');
        }
        $('#r-customer').textContent = result.customerName;
        $('#r-amount').textContent = moneyFa(result.amount) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646';
        $('#r-points').textContent = '+' + fa(result.pointsEarned) + ' \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632';
        var noteEl = $('#r-points-note');
        if (noteEl) noteEl.textContent = result.pointsNote || '';
        $('#r-total').textContent = fa(result.totalPoints) + ' \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632';
        $('#r-tier').textContent = TIER_LABELS[result.tier] || result.tier;
        if (result.discount > 0) {
          $('#r-discount-row').classList.remove('hidden');
          $('#r-discount').textContent = moneyFa(result.discount) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646';
        } else {
          $('#r-discount-row').classList.add('hidden');
        }
        $('#receipt-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
        toast('\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0627 \u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642\u06CC\u062A \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F.', 'success');

        // \u0628\u0647\u200C\u0631\u0648\u0632\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0646\u06CC \u0622\u0645\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u0631\u0648\u0632
        var countEl = $('#today-count');
        var totalEl = $('#today-total');
        countEl.textContent = fa(parseFaNumber(countEl.textContent) + 1);
        totalEl.textContent = moneyFa(parseFaNumber(totalEl.textContent) + result.amount);

        var list = $('#recent-list');
        if (list.querySelector('p')) list.innerHTML = '';
        var row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'px-4 py-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2';
        row.innerHTML = '<div class="min-w-0"><div class="text-[12px] font-medium truncate">' + escapeHtml(result.customerName) + '</div>' +
          '<div class="text-[10px] text-cream/35">\u0647\u0645\u06CC\u0646 \u062D\u0627\u0644\u0627</div></div>' +
          '<div class="text-[12px] font-bold text-amber-200 whitespace-nowrap">' + moneyFa(result.amount) + '</div>';
        list.prepend(row);
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
        // \u0627\u06AF\u0631 \u062F\u0631\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0633\u062A \u0646\u06CC\u0645\u0647\u200C\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u060C \u0634\u0646\u0627\u0633\u0647 \u06CC\u06A9\u062A\u0627 \u0622\u0632\u0627\u062F \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F \u062A\u0627 \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634 \u0628\u0639\u062F\u06CC \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u062A\u0627\u0632\u0647 \u0628\u0633\u0627\u0632\u062F
        requestId = null;
      } finally {
        submitting = false;
        setLoading(btn, false);
      }
    }

    $('#submit-invoice').addEventListener('click', submitInvoice);

    $('#new-invoice').addEventListener('click', function () {
      $('#receipt-modal').classList.add('hidden');
      resetForm();
      clearCustomer();
    });

    // --- \u0645\u06CC\u0627\u0646\u200C\u0628\u0631\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0635\u0641\u062D\u0647\u200C\u06A9\u0644\u06CC\u062F (\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0633\u0631\u0639\u062A \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642) ---
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      var tag = (event.target && event.target.tagName) || '';
      var typing = tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT';

      // Ctrl/Cmd + Enter : \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631
      if ((event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && event.key === 'Enter') {
        event.preventDefault();
        $('#submit-invoice').click();
        return;
      }
      // "/" : \u067E\u0631\u0634 \u0628\u0647 \u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648\u06CC \u0645\u062D\u0635\u0648\u0644
      if (!typing && event.key === '/') {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (menuSearch) menuSearch.focus();
        return;
      }
      // "?"/F1 : \u0631\u0627\u0647\u0646\u0645\u0627\u06CC \u0645\u06CC\u0627\u0646\u200C\u0628\u0631\u0647\u0627
      if (!typing && (event.key === '?' || event.key === 'F1')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        toast('\u0645\u06CC\u0627\u0646\u200C\u0628\u0631\u0647\u0627: / \u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648\u06CC \u0645\u062D\u0635\u0648\u0644 \xB7 Ctrl+Enter \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \xB7 Esc \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646 \u067E\u0646\u062C\u0631\u0647', 'info');
        return;
      }
      // Esc : \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646 \u067E\u0646\u062C\u0631\u0647\u0654 \u0631\u0633\u06CC\u062F
      if (event.key === 'Escape' && !$('#receipt-modal').classList.contains('hidden')) {
        $('#new-invoice').click();
      }
    });

    // init
    renderMenu();
    refreshTotals();
    phoneInput.focus();
  })();
  `;
  return appShell({
    title: "\u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631",
    role: "staff",
    userName: user.name,
    active: "invoice",
    body,
    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function staffHistoryPage(settings, user, invoices, summary, options = {}) {
  const q = (options.q ?? "").trim();
  const body = `
  <div class="p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
    ${pageHeader({
    title: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0645\u0646",
    subtitle: "\u0622\u062E\u0631\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627\u06CC\u06CC \u06A9\u0647 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0647\u200C\u0627\u06CC\u062F.",
    iconName: "clock",
    actions: `<a href="/staff" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("plus", "w-4 h-4")} \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F</a>`
  })}

    <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
      <div class="glass rounded-2xl p-5">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-cream/45 mb-2">${icon("receipt", "w-4 h-4 text-gold")} \u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627</div>
        <div class="text-2xl font-extrabold">${toPersianDigits(summary.count)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="glass rounded-2xl p-5">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-cream/45 mb-2">${icon("wallet", "w-4 h-4 text-gold")} \u0645\u062C\u0645\u0648\u0639 \u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634</div>
        <div class="text-2xl font-extrabold gold-text">${formatNumberFa(summary.total)} <span class="text-xs text-cream/40 font-normal">\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <form method="get" action="/staff/history" class="glass rounded-2xl p-3 mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
      <input type="search" name="q" value="${escapeHtml(q)}" class="field !py-2 text-[12px] flex-1 min-w-[180px]"
        placeholder="\u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648\u06CC \u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u060C \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644 \u06CC\u0627 \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u2026" />
      <label class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-cream/55 select-none cursor-pointer">
        <input type="checkbox" name="voided" value="1" class="accent-amber-500 w-3.5 h-3.5" ${options.onlyVoided ? "checked" : ""} />
        \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647\u200C\u0647\u0627
      </label>
      <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("search", "w-4 h-4")} \u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648</button>
      ${q || options.onlyVoided ? `<a href="/staff/history" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">\u067E\u0627\u06A9 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646</a>` : ""}
    </form>

    <div class="glass rounded-3xl overflow-hidden">
      <div class="px-5 py-4 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center gap-2">
        <span class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-gold/10 border border-gold/20 text-gold flex items-center justify-center">${icon("receipt", "w-4 h-4")}</span>
        <h2 class="font-bold text-sm">\u0644\u06CC\u0633\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627</h2>
        <span class="badge tone-muted me-auto">${toPersianDigits(invoices.length)} \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F</span>
      </div>
      ${invoices.length === 0 ? emptyState({
    title: "\u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0647\u200C\u0627\u06CC\u062F",
    message: "\u0628\u0627 \u0645\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639\u0647 \u0628\u0647 \u0635\u0641\u062D\u0647 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0646\u06CC\u062F \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0631\u0627 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.",
    iconName: "receipt",
    action: `<a href="/staff" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("plus", "w-4 h-4")} \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631</a>`
  }) : `<div class="table-wrap">
              <table class="data">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th>#</th>
                    <th>\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC</th>
                    <th>\u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645\u200C\u0647\u0627</th>
                    <th>\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A</th>
                    <th>\u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A</th>
                    <th>\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</th>
                    <th>\u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E</th>
                    <th>\u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  ${invoices.map(
    (invoice) => `<tr>
                        <td class="text-cream/35 text-xs">${toPersianDigits(invoice.id)}</td>
                        <td>
                          <div class="font-medium text-[12.5px]">${escapeHtml(invoice.customer_name)}</div>
                          <div class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/35 font-mono" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(invoice.customer_phone)}</div>
                        </td>
                        <td class="text-[11.5px] text-cream/50 max-w-[200px]">${escapeHtml(describeItems(invoice.items))}</td>
                        <td class="font-bold text-amber-200 whitespace-nowrap text-[12.5px]">${formatNumberFa(invoice.amount)}</td>
                        <td class="text-[11px] whitespace-nowrap">${escapeHtml(
      PAYMENT_LABELS[invoice.payment_method ?? ""] ?? "\u2014"
    )}</td>
                        <td class="text-emerald-300/80 text-[12px]">+${toPersianDigits(invoice.points_earned)}</td>
                        <td class="text-[11px] text-cream/45 whitespace-nowrap">${formatJalaliDateTime(invoice.created_at)}</td>
                        <td>${invoice.voided_at ? `<span class="badge tone-danger" title="${escapeHtml(invoice.void_reason ?? "")}">\u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644</span>` : '<span class="badge tone-success">\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631</span>'}</td>
                      </tr>`
  ).join("")}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>`}
    </div>
  </div>
  `;
  return appShell({
    title: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0645\u0646",
    role: "staff",
    userName: user.name,
    active: "history",
    body,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function customerSummaryForApi(user, status, coupons, extra = {}) {
  return {
    id: user.id,
    name: user.name,
    phone: user.phone,
    points: user.points,
    lifetime_points: user.lifetime_points,
    tier: user.tier,
    last_visit_date: user.last_visit_date,
    avg_visit_interval: user.avg_visit_interval,
    status_label: status.label,
    status_tone: status.tone,
    tier_label: tierLabel(user.tier),
    sms_opt_in: user.sms_opt_in,
    visit_earned_today: extra.visitEarnedToday === true,
    points_mode: extra.pointsMode ?? "amount",
    visit_points: extra.visitPoints ?? 0,
    visit_min_amount: extra.visitMinAmount ?? 0,
    coupons: coupons.map((c) => ({
      code: c.code,
      label: c.kind === "percent" ? `${c.value}\u066A` : `${formatMoney(c.value)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646`
    }))
  };
}

// src/ui/shift.ts
init_db();
init_utils();
function money(value) {
  return formatNumberFa(Math.max(0, Math.floor(value)) || 0);
}
function summaryRow(label, value, options = {}) {
  return `<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-2 border-b border-white/5 last:border-0">
    <span class="text-[11.5px] text-cream/50">${label}</span>
    <span class="text-[12.5px] ${options.strong ? "font-extrabold" : "font-bold"} ${options.tone ?? "text-cream"}">${value}</span>
  </div>`;
}
function staffShiftPage(input) {
  const { settings, userName, role, openShift: openShift2, summary, history, canViewAll } = input;
  const openCard = openShift2 ? `<div class="glass rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-5 border border-emerald-500/25">
        <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-3">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span class="badge tone-success">${icon("lock", "w-3 h-3")} \u0628\u0627\u0632</span>
              <span class="text-[11px] text-cream/40">\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A #${toPersianDigits(openShift2.id)}</span>
            </div>
            <h2 class="font-bold text-[15px] mt-2">\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0633\u062A</h2>
            <p class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/35 mt-1">\u0634\u0631\u0648\u0639: ${formatJalaliDateTime(openShift2.opened_at)}</p>
          </div>
          <button type="button" id="close-shift" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm whitespace-nowrap">${icon("lock", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642</button>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mb-3">
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/25 border border-white/10 p-3">
            <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/40 mb-1">\u0645\u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0647</div>
            <div class="text-[15px] font-extrabold">${money(openShift2.opening_cash)}<span class="text-[9.5px] font-normal text-cream/35 ms-1">\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646</span></div>

            <div class="text-[15px] font-extrabold text-emerald-300">${money(summary?.expectedCash ?? openShift2.opening_cash)}<span class="text-[9.5px] font-normal text-emerald-200/50 ms-1">\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/25 border border-white/10 px-3.5 py-2">
          ${summaryRow("\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC", money(summary?.cashSales ?? 0) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646", { tone: "text-emerald-300" })}
          ${summaryRow("\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A", money(summary?.cardSales ?? 0) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646")}
          ${summaryRow("\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646", money(summary?.onlineSales ?? 0) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646")}
          ${summaryRow("\u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631", formatNumberFa(summary?.invoiceCount ?? 0))}
          ${summaryRow("\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0646\u0642\u062F", "+" + money(summary?.movementsIn ?? 0))}
          ${summaryRow("\u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C \u0646\u0642\u062F", "\u2212" + money(summary?.movementsOut ?? 0))}
          ${summaryRow("\u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u062F\u0631 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A", formatNumberFa(summary?.voidCount ?? 0) + " \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631", {
    tone: "text-rose-300"
  })}
        </div>

        <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-3">
          <button type="button" data-move="in" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("plus", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0646\u0642\u062F</button>
          <button type="button" data-move="out" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("close", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C \u0646\u0642\u062F</button>
        </div>
      </div>` : `<div class="glass rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-5 border border-amber-500/25">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <span class="badge tone-warning">${icon("lock", "w-3 h-3")} \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647</span>
          <span class="text-[11px] text-cream/40">\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A</span>
        </div>
        <h2 class="font-bold text-[15px]">\u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642</h2>
        <p class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/35 mt-1 mb-3">\u0645\u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F\u06CC \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u0628\u062A\u062F\u0627\u06CC \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0631\u0627 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F. \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0647 \u0635\u0641\u0631 \u0647\u0645 \u0645\u062C\u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0633\u062A.</p>

        <label class="label" for="opening-cash">\u0645\u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0647 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 (\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646)</label>

        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mt-2">
