  ).join("")}
        </div>
        <button type="button" id="open-shift" class="btn btn-primary w-full mt-3">${icon("lock", "w-4 h-4")} \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642</button>
        ${settings.require_shift === 1 ? `<p class="text-[10px] text-amber-200/70 mt-2 leading-5">\u062A\u0627 \u0648\u0642\u062A\u06CC \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u060C \u062B\u0628\u062A \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC \u0645\u0645\u06A9\u0646 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.</p>` : `<p class="text-[10px] text-cream/30 mt-2 leading-5">\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u062A\u0641\u06A9\u06CC\u06A9 \u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634\u200C\u0647\u0627 \u0648 \u06A9\u0646\u062A\u0631\u0644 \u0646\u0642\u062F \u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F\u061B \u0627\u062E\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0631\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A.</p>`}
      </div>`;
  const historyRows = history.length ? history.map((shift) => {
    const diff = shift.counted_cash === null || shift.counted_cash === void 0 ? null : shift.counted_cash - shift.expected_live;
    return `<tr>
            <td class="text-[11px] text-cream/35">${toPersianDigits(shift.id)}</td>
            <td class="whitespace-nowrap">${shift.status === "open" ? '<span class="badge tone-success">\u0628\u0627\u0632</span>' : '<span class="badge tone-muted">\u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647</span>'}</td>
            <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 whitespace-nowrap">${formatJalaliDateTime(shift.opened_at)}</td>
            <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 whitespace-nowrap">${shift.closed_at ? formatJalaliDateTime(shift.closed_at) : "\u2014"}</td>
            <td class="text-[11.5px] whitespace-nowrap">${money(shift.opening_cash)}</td>
            <td class="text-[11.5px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap">${money(shift.expected_live)}</td>
            <td class="text-[11.5px] whitespace-nowrap">${shift.counted_cash === null ? "\u2014" : money(shift.counted_cash)}</td>
            <td class="whitespace-nowrap">${diff === null ? "\u2014" : `<span class="${diff === 0 ? "text-cream/50" : diff > 0 ? "text-sky-300" : "text-rose-300"} font-bold text-[11.5px]">${diff > 0 ? "+" : ""}${money(diff)}</span>`}</td>
          </tr>`;
  }).join("") : "";
  const body = `
  <div class="p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
    ${pageHeader({
    title: "\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0648 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A",
    subtitle: "\u06A9\u0646\u062A\u0631\u0644 \u0646\u0642\u062F\u060C \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0648 \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u0648 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642",
    iconName: "wallet",
    actions: `<a href="/staff" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("receipt", "w-4 h-4")} \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631</a>`
  })}

    <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-[1fr_1.15fr] gap-4 items-start">
      ${openCard}

      <div class="glass rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-5">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
          <h2 class="font-bold text-[14px]">${canViewAll ? "\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u062E\u06CC\u0631 (\u0647\u0645\u0647\u0654 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u06A9\u0646\u0627\u0646)" : "\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u062E\u06CC\u0631 \u0645\u0646"}</h2>
          ${canViewAll ? `<a href="/admin/shifts" class="text-[11px] text-gold/80 hover:underline">\u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644</a>` : ""}
        </div>
        ${history.length === 0 ? emptyState({ title: "\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647", message: "\u0628\u0627 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u060C \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0634\u0645\u0627 \u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.", iconName: "wallet" }) : `<div class="table-wrap">
                <table class="data">
                  <thead><tr><th>#</th><th>\u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A</th><th>\u0634\u0631\u0648\u0639</th><th>\u067E\u0627\u06CC\u0627\u0646</th><th>\u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0647</th><th>\u0646\u0642\u062F \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631</th><th>\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0634\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647</th><th>\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0644\u0627\u0641</th></tr></thead>
                  <tbody>${historyRows}</tbody>
                </table>
              </div>`}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- \u067E\u0646\u062C\u0631\u0647\u0654 \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A/\u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u0646\u0642\u062F -->
  <div id="move-modal" class="hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 overflow-y-auto">
    <div class="glass rounded-3xl max-w-sm mx-auto mt-20 p-5">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
        <h3 class="font-bold text-[15px]" id="move-title">\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0646\u0642\u062F</h3>
        <button type="button" id="move-cancel" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("close", "w-4 h-4")}</button>
      </div>
      <label class="label" for="move-amount">\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A (\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646)</label>
      <input id="move-amount" type="text" inputmode="numeric" dir="ltr" class="field text-left font-bold" placeholder="0" />
      <label class="label mt-2" for="move-note">\u062A\u0648\u0636\u06CC\u062D</label>
      <input id="move-note" type="text" class="field" maxlength="80" placeholder="\u0645\u062B\u0644\u0627\u064B: \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F \u06CC\u062E\u060C \u0648\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u0632 \u0628\u0647 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642" />
      <div class="flex gap-2 mt-4">
        <button type="button" id="move-confirm" class="btn btn-primary flex-1">\u062B\u0628\u062A</button>
        <button type="button" id="move-close" class="btn btn-ghost flex-1">\u0627\u0646\u0635\u0631\u0627\u0641</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- \u067E\u0646\u062C\u0631\u0647\u0654 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 -->
  <div id="close-modal" class="hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 overflow-y-auto">
    <div class="glass rounded-3xl max-w-md mx-auto mt-16 p-5">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
        <h3 class="font-bold text-[15px]">\u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642</h3>
        <button type="button" id="close-cancel" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("close", "w-4 h-4")}</button>
      </div>

      <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/25 border border-white/10 p-3 mb-3">
        ${summaryRow("\u0645\u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0647", money(openShift2?.opening_cash ?? 0))}
        ${summaryRow("\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC", money(summary?.cashSales ?? 0))}
        ${summaryRow("\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0646\u0642\u062F", "+" + money(summary?.movementsIn ?? 0))}
        ${summaryRow("\u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C \u0646\u0642\u062F", "\u2212" + money(summary?.movementsOut ?? 0))}
        ${summaryRow("\u0646\u0642\u062F \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631", money(summary?.expectedCash ?? 0) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646", { tone: "text-emerald-300", strong: true })}
      </div>

      <label class="label" for="counted-cash">\u0646\u0642\u062F \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0634\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u062F\u0631 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 (\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646)</label>
      <input id="counted-cash" type="text" inputmode="numeric" dir="ltr" class="field text-left font-bold" placeholder="0" />

      <div class="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-black/25 px-3 py-2">
        <span class="text-[11px] text-cream/50">\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0644\u0627\u0641</span>
        <span id="diff-preview" class="text-[12.5px] font-bold text-cream/40">\u2014</span>
      </div>

      <label class="label mt-2" for="close-note">\u062A\u0648\u0636\u06CC\u062D (\u0627\u062E\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0631\u06CC)</label>
      <textarea id="close-note" class="field min-h-[64px] text-[12px]" maxlength="200" placeholder="\u062F\u0644\u06CC\u0644 \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0644\u0627\u0641\u060C \u062A\u0648\u0636\u06CC\u062D \u062A\u062D\u0648\u06CC\u0644 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u2026"></textarea>

      <div class="flex gap-2 mt-4">
        <button type="button" id="close-confirm" class="btn btn-danger flex-1">${icon("lock", "w-4 h-4")} \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646 \u0648 \u062B\u0628\u062A</button>
        <button type="button" id="close-close" class="btn btn-ghost flex-1">\u0627\u0646\u0635\u0631\u0627\u0641</button>
      </div>
      <p class="text-[10px] text-cream/30 mt-2 leading-5">\u067E\u0633 \u0627\u0632 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646\u060C \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0642\u0627\u0628\u0644 \u0628\u0627\u0632\u06AF\u0634\u0627\u06CC\u06CC \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A \u0648 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F \u062F\u0631 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0628\u0639\u062F\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u0646\u062F.</p>
    </div>
  </div>
  `;
  const scripts = `
    var OPEN_SHIFT_ID = ${openShift2 ? String(openShift2.id) : "null"};
    var EXPECTED = ${summary ? String(summary.expectedCash) : "0"};
    var moveKind = 'in';

    var moveModal = $('#move-modal');
    var closeModal = $('#close-modal');

    function openMove(kind) {
      moveKind = kind;
      $('#move-title').textContent = kind === 'in' ? '\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0646\u0642\u062F \u0628\u0647 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642' : '\u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C \u0646\u0642\u062F \u0627\u0632 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642';
      $('#move-amount').value = '';
      $('#move-note').value = '';
      moveModal.classList.remove('hidden');
      $('#move-amount').focus();
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-move]').forEach(function (button) {
      button.addEventListener('click', function () { openMove(button.getAttribute('data-move')); });
    });

    $('#move-cancel').addEventListener('click', function () { moveModal.classList.add('hidden'); });
    $('#move-close').addEventListener('click', function () { moveModal.classList.add('hidden'); });
    moveModal.addEventListener('click', function (event) { if (event.target === moveModal) moveModal.classList.add('hidden'); });

    $('#move-amount').addEventListener('input', function () {
      var digits = this.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
      this.value = digits ? money(digits) : '';
    });

    $('#move-confirm').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      if (!OPEN_SHIFT_ID) return;
      var amount = Number($('#move-amount').value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')) || 0;
      if (amount <= 0) { toast('\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0631\u0627 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); return; }

      var button = this;
      setLoading(button, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u062B\u0628\u062A\u2026');
      try {
        var result = await api('/api/staff/shift/movements', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: { kind: moveKind, amount: amount, note: $('#move-note').value.trim() }
        });
        toast(moveKind === 'in' ? '\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0646\u0642\u062F \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F.' : '\u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C \u0646\u0642\u062F \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F.', 'success');
        setTimeout(function () { location.reload(); }, 600);
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
        setLoading(button, false);
      }
    });

    // --- \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 ---
    var opening = $('#opening-cash');
    if (opening) {
      opening.addEventListener('input', function () {
        var digits = this.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
        this.value = digits ? money(digits) : '';
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-opening]').forEach(function (chip) {
        chip.addEventListener('click', function () {
          var value = chip.getAttribute('data-opening');
          opening.value = Number(value) === 0 ? '' : money(Number(value));
        });
      });
      $('#open-shift').addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var amount = Number(opening.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')) || 0;
        var button = this;
        setLoading(button, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646\u2026');
        try {
          await api('/api/staff/shift/open', { method: 'POST', body: { openingCash: amount } });
          toast('\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u0634\u062F.', 'success');
          setTimeout(function () { location.reload(); }, 500);
        } catch (error) {
          toast(error.message, 'error');
          setLoading(button, false);
        }
      });
    }

    // --- \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 ---
    if (OPEN_SHIFT_ID) {
      $('#close-shift').addEventListener('click', function () {
        $('#counted-cash').value = EXPECTED ? money(EXPECTED) : '';
        updateDiff();
        closeModal.classList.remove('hidden');
        $('#counted-cash').focus();
        $('#counted-cash').select();
      });

      $('#close-cancel').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal.classList.add('hidden'); });
      $('#close-close').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal.classList.add('hidden'); });
      closeModal.addEventListener('click', function (event) { if (event.target === closeModal) closeModal.classList.add('hidden'); });

      function updateDiff() {
        var counted = Number($('#counted-cash').value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')) || 0;
        var diff = counted - EXPECTED;
        var el = $('#diff-preview');
        el.textContent = (diff > 0 ? '+' : '') + money(diff) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646';
        el.className = 'text-[12.5px] font-bold ' +
          (diff === 0 ? 'text-emerald-300' : diff > 0 ? 'text-sky-300' : 'text-rose-300');
      }

      $('#counted-cash').addEventListener('input', function () {
        var digits = this.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
        this.value = digits ? money(digits) : '';
        updateDiff();
      });

      $('#close-confirm').addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var counted = Number($('#counted-cash').value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')) || 0;
        var button = this;
        setLoading(button, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646\u2026');
        try {
          await api('/api/staff/shift/close', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: { countedCash: counted, note: $('#close-note').value.trim() }
          });
          toast('\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647 \u0634\u062F.', 'success');
          setTimeout(function () { location.reload(); }, 600);
        } catch (error) {
          toast(error.message, 'error');
          setLoading(button, false);
        }
      });
    }

    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      if (event.key !== 'Escape') return;
      moveModal.classList.add('hidden');
      closeModal.classList.add('hidden');
    });
  `;
  return appShell({
    title: "\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642",
    role: role === "admin" ? "admin" : "staff",
    userName,
    active: "shift",
    body,
    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function adminShiftsPage(input) {
  const { settings, userName, shifts, total, page, pageSize, filters, staffOptions, openShifts } = input;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
  const totals = shifts.reduce(
    (acc, shift) => {
      acc.expected += shift.expected_live;
      acc.counted += shift.counted_cash ?? 0;
      acc.diff += shift.counted_cash === null ? 0 : shift.counted_cash - shift.expected_live;
      return acc;
    },
    { expected: 0, counted: 0, diff: 0 }
  );
  const pageLink = (target) => {
    const search = new URLSearchParams();
    if (filters.from) search.set("from", filters.from);
    if (filters.to) search.set("to", filters.to);
    if (filters.staffId) search.set("staff", String(filters.staffId));
    if (filters.status && filters.status !== "all") search.set("status", filters.status);
    search.set("page", String(target));
    return `/admin/shifts?${search.toString()}`;
  };
  const rows = shifts.map((shift) => {
    const difference = shift.counted_cash === null || shift.counted_cash === void 0 ? null : shift.counted_cash - shift.expected_live;
    return `<tr>
        <td class="text-[11px] text-cream/35">${toPersianDigits(shift.id)}</td>
        <td class="text-[12px] whitespace-nowrap">${escapeHtml(shift.staff_name)}</td>
        <td class="whitespace-nowrap">${shift.status === "open" ? '<span class="badge tone-success">\u0628\u0627\u0632</span>' : '<span class="badge tone-muted">\u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647</span>'}</td>
        <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 whitespace-nowrap">${formatJalaliDateTime(shift.opened_at)}</td>
        <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 whitespace-nowrap">${shift.closed_at ? formatJalaliDateTime(shift.closed_at) : "\u2014"}</td>
        <td class="text-[11.5px] whitespace-nowrap">${money(shift.opening_cash)}</td>
        <td class="text-[11.5px] whitespace-nowrap">${formatNumberFa(shift.invoice_count)}</td>
        <td class="text-[11.5px] whitespace-nowrap">${money(shift.total_sales ?? 0)}</td>
        <td class="text-[11.5px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap">${money(shift.expected_live)}</td>
        <td class="text-[11.5px] whitespace-nowrap">${shift.counted_cash === null ? "\u2014" : money(shift.counted_cash)}</td>
        <td class="whitespace-nowrap">${difference === null ? "\u2014" : `<span class="${difference === 0 ? "text-cream/50" : difference > 0 ? "text-sky-300" : "text-rose-300"} font-bold text-[11.5px]">${difference > 0 ? "+" : ""}${money(difference)}</span>`}</td>
        <td class="text-left"><a href="/admin/shifts/${shift.id}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("eye", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u062C\u0632\u0626\u06CC\u0627\u062A</a></td>
      </tr>`;
  }).join("");
  const body = `
  <div class="p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
    ${pageHeader({
    title: "\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u200C\u0647\u0627 \u0648 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627",
    subtitle: "\u06A9\u0646\u062A\u0631\u0644 \u0646\u0642\u062F\u060C \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0644\u0627\u0641 \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0648 \u0639\u0645\u0644\u06A9\u0631\u062F \u0647\u0631 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A",
    iconName: "wallet",
    actions: `<a href="/staff/shift" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("plus", "w-4 h-4")} \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0645\u0646</a>`
  })}

    ${openShifts.length > 0 ? `<div class="glass rounded-2xl p-3.5 mb-4 border border-emerald-500/25">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <span class="badge tone-success">${icon("lock", "w-3 h-3")} ${formatNumberFa(openShifts.length)} \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0627\u0632</span>
              <span class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/40">\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u062D\u0627\u0636\u0631</span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              ${openShifts.map(
    (shift) => `<a href="/admin/shifts/${shift.id}" class="badge tone-muted hover:!text-gold">${escapeHtml(shift.staff_name)} \xB7 \u0627\u0632 ${formatJalaliDateTime(shift.opened_at)}</a>`
  ).join("")}
            </div>
          </div>` : ""}

    <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-4">
      <div class="glass rounded-2xl p-4">
        <div class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 mb-1">\u0646\u0642\u062F \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631 (\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0635\u0641\u062D\u0647)</div>
        <div class="text-[15px] font-extrabold text-emerald-300">${money(totals.expected)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="glass rounded-2xl p-4">
        <div class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 mb-1">\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0634\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647</div>
        <div class="text-[15px] font-extrabold">${money(totals.counted)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="glass rounded-2xl p-4">
        <div class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 mb-1">\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0644\u0627\u0641 \u06A9\u0644</div>
        <div class="text-[15px] font-extrabold ${totals.diff === 0 ? "text-cream/60" : totals.diff > 0 ? "text-sky-300" : "text-rose-300"}">
          ${totals.diff > 0 ? "+" : ""}${money(totals.diff)}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <form method="get" action="/admin/shifts" class="glass rounded-2xl p-4 mb-4">
      <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="text-[10px] text-cream/35 shrink-0">\u0627\u0632</span>
          <select id="from-day" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
          <select id="from-month" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
          <select id="from-year" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="text-[10px] text-cream/35 shrink-0">\u062A\u0627</span>
          <select id="to-day" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
          <select id="to-month" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
          <select id="to-year" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
        </div>
        <select name="staff" class="field !py-2 text-[12px]">
          <option value="">\u0647\u0645\u0647\u0654 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u06A9\u0646\u0627\u0646</option>
          ${staffOptions.map(
    (member) => `<option value="${member.id}" ${filters.staffId === member.id ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(member.name)}</option>`
  ).join("")}
        </select>
        <div class="flex gap-2">
          <select name="status" class="field !py-2 text-[12px] flex-1">
            <option value="all" ${filters.status === "all" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0647\u0645\u0647</option>
            <option value="open" ${filters.status === "open" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0628\u0627\u0632</option>
            <option value="closed" ${filters.status === "closed" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647</option>
          </select>
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("filter", "w-4 h-4")} \u0641\u06CC\u0644\u062A\u0631</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <input type="hidden" name="from" id="from-iso" value="${escapeHtml(filters.from)}" />
      <input type="hidden" name="to" id="to-iso" value="${escapeHtml(filters.to)}" />
    </form>

    <div class="glass rounded-3xl overflow-hidden">
      ${shifts.length === 0 ? emptyState({ title: "\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A\u06CC \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F", message: "\u0628\u0627 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u0641\u06CC\u0644\u062A\u0631\u0647\u0627 \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.", iconName: "wallet" }) : `<div class="table-wrap">
              <table class="data">
                <thead><tr><th>#</th><th>\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0645\u0646\u062F</th><th>\u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A</th><th>\u0634\u0631\u0648\u0639</th><th>\u067E\u0627\u06CC\u0627\u0646</th><th>\u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0647</th><th>\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631</th><th>\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634</th><th>\u0646\u0642\u062F \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631</th><th>\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0634</th><th>\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0644\u0627\u0641</th><th></th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3 border-t border-white/10">
              <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/40">\u0635\u0641\u062D\u0647 ${toPersianDigits(page)} \u0627\u0632 ${toPersianDigits(totalPages)} \xB7 ${formatNumberFa(total)} \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A</div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <a href="${pageLink(Math.max(1, page - 1))}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm ${page <= 1 ? "opacity-40 pointer-events-none" : ""}">${icon("right", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u0642\u0628\u0644\u06CC</a>
                <a href="${pageLink(Math.min(totalPages, page + 1))}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm ${page >= totalPages ? "opacity-40 pointer-events-none" : ""}">\u0628\u0639\u062F\u06CC ${icon("left", "w-3.5 h-3.5")}</a>
              </div>
            </div>`}
    </div>
  </div>
  `;
  const scripts = `
    initJalaliRange($('#from-day'), $('#from-month'), $('#from-year'), ${JSON.stringify(filters.from)}, 8);
    initJalaliRange($('#to-day'), $('#to-month'), $('#to-year'), ${JSON.stringify(filters.to)}, 8);
    document.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit', function () {
      var from = readJalaliRange($('#from-day'), $('#from-month'), $('#from-year'));
      var to = readJalaliRange($('#to-day'), $('#to-month'), $('#to-year'));
      $('#from-iso').value = from.iso || '';
      $('#to-iso').value = to.iso || '';
    });
  `;
  return appShell({
    title: "\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u200C\u0647\u0627",
    role: "admin",
    userName,
    active: "shifts",
    body,
    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function adminShiftDetailPage(input) {
  const { settings, userName, shift, summary, movements, invoices } = input;
  const difference = summary.difference;
  const isOpen = shift.status === "open";
  const movementRows = movements.length ? movements.map(
    (movement) => `<tr>
            <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 whitespace-nowrap">${formatJalaliDateTime(movement.created_at)}</td>
            <td>${movement.kind === "in" ? '<span class="badge tone-success">\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F</span>' : '<span class="badge tone-danger">\u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C</span>'}</td>
            <td class="text-[11.5px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap ${movement.kind === "in" ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-rose-300"}">${movement.kind === "in" ? "+" : "\u2212"}${money(movement.amount)}</td>
            <td class="text-[11px] text-cream/50">${escapeHtml(movement.reason ?? "\u2014")}</td>
            <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/35">${movement.created_by ? "#" + toPersianDigits(movement.created_by) : "\u2014"}</td>
          </tr>`
  ).join("") : "";
  const invoiceRows = invoices.length ? invoices.map(
    (invoice) => `<tr class="${invoice.voided_at ? "opacity-45" : ""}">
            <td class="text-[11px] text-cream/35">${toPersianDigits(invoice.id)}</td>
            <td class="text-[12px] whitespace-nowrap">${escapeHtml(invoice.customer_name)}</td>
            <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/35 font-mono" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(invoice.customer_phone)}</td>
            <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/45 whitespace-nowrap">${formatJalaliDateTime(invoice.created_at)}</td>
            <td class="text-[11.5px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap">${money(invoice.amount)}</td>
            <td class="text-[11px] whitespace-nowrap">${escapeHtml(PAYMENT_LABELS[invoice.payment_method ?? ""] ?? "\u2014")}</td>
            <td>${invoice.voided_at ? '<span class="badge tone-danger">\u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644</span>' : '<span class="badge tone-success">\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631</span>'}</td>
          </tr>`
  ).join("") : "";
  const body = `
  <div class="p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
    ${pageHeader({
    title: `\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A #${toPersianDigits(shift.id)}`,
    subtitle: `${shift.staff_name} \xB7 ${formatJalaliDateTime(shift.opened_at)}`,
    iconName: "wallet",
    actions: `<a href="/admin/shifts" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("right", "w-4 h-4")} \u0628\u0627\u0632\u06AF\u0634\u062A</a>`
  })}

    <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-[1.1fr_1fr] gap-4 items-start mb-4">
      <div class="glass rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-5">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          ${isOpen ? `<span class="badge tone-success">${icon("lock", "w-3 h-3")} \u0628\u0627\u0632</span>` : `<span class="badge tone-muted">\u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647</span>`}
          <span class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/40">\u0628\u0627\u0632 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u062A\u0648\u0633\u0637 ${escapeHtml(shift.staff_name)}</span>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mb-3">
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/25 p-3">
            <div class="text-[10px] text-emerald-200/70 mb-1">\u0646\u0642\u062F \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631</div>
            <div class="text-[15px] font-extrabold text-emerald-300">${money(summary.expectedCash)}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-2xl bg-black/25 border border-white/10 p-3">
            <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/40 mb-1">\u0646\u0642\u062F \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0634\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647</div>
            <div class="text-[15px] font-extrabold">${shift.counted_cash === null ? "\u2014" : money(shift.counted_cash)}</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        ${difference === null ? "" : `<div class="rounded-2xl border p-3 mb-3 ${difference === 0 ? "border-white/10 bg-black/25" : difference > 0 ? "border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/10" : "border-rose-400/30 bg-rose-500/10"}">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span class="text-[11px] text-cream/55">\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0644\u0627\u0641 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642</span>
                  <span class="font-extrabold text-[14px] ${difference === 0 ? "text-cream/60" : difference > 0 ? "text-sky-300" : "text-rose-300"}">${difference > 0 ? "+" : ""}${money(difference)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646</span>
                </div>
                <p class="text-[10px] text-cream/35 mt-1">${difference === 0 ? "\u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0645\u0646\u0637\u0628\u0642 \u0627\u0633\u062A." : difference > 0 ? "\u0646\u0642\u062F \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0634\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0628\u06CC\u0634\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A (\u06A9\u0645\u200C\u062B\u0628\u062A\u06CC \u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u06CC\u0627 \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0646\u0642\u062F \u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647)." : "\u06A9\u0645\u0628\u0648\u062F \u0646\u0642\u062F \u0646\u0633\u0628\u062A \u0628\u0647 \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631 (\u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C \u0646\u0642\u062F \u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u06CC\u0627 \u062E\u0637\u0627 \u062F\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0632\u06AF\u0634\u062A \u0628\u0627\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647)."}</p>
              </div>`}

        ${summaryRow("\u0645\u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F\u06CC \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0647", money(shift.opening_cash) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646")}
        ${summaryRow("\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC", money(summary.cashSales) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646")}
        ${summaryRow("\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A", money(summary.cardSales) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646")}
        ${summaryRow("\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646", money(summary.onlineSales) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646")}
        ${summaryRow("\u062C\u0645\u0639 \u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634", money(summary.totalSales) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646", { tone: "text-amber-200", strong: true })}
        ${summaryRow("\u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641", money(summary.totalDiscount) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646", { tone: "text-emerald-300" })}
        ${summaryRow("\u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631", formatNumberFa(summary.invoiceCount))}
        ${summaryRow("\u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u0646\u0642\u062F", "+" + money(summary.movementsIn))}
        ${summaryRow("\u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C \u0646\u0642\u062F", "\u2212" + money(summary.movementsOut))}
        ${summaryRow("\u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647", formatNumberFa(summary.voidCount) + " \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \xB7 " + money(summary.voidAmount) + " \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646", {
    tone: "text-rose-300"
  })}
        ${shift.notes ? `<div class="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-black/25 border border-white/10 p-3 text-[11px] text-cream/55">\u06CC\u0627\u062F\u062F\u0627\u0634\u062A: ${escapeHtml(shift.notes)}</div>` : ""}
        ${shift.closed_at ? `<p class="text-[10px] text-cream/30 mt-2">\u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u062F\u0631 ${formatJalaliDateTime(shift.closed_at)}${shift.closed_by_name ? " \u062A\u0648\u0633\u0637 " + escapeHtml(shift.closed_by_name) : ""}</p>` : ""}
        ${shift.status === "closed" && shift.expected_cash !== null && shift.expected_cash !== summary.expectedCash ? `<div class="mt-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-400/25 p-2.5 text-[10.5px] text-amber-200/85 leading-5">
                \u062F\u0631 \u0632\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0646\u060C \u0646\u0642\u062F \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631 ${money(shift.expected_cash)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0628\u0648\u062F\u061B \u067E\u0633 \u0627\u0632 \u0622\u0646 ${summary.voidCount} \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0648 \u0646\u0642\u062F \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u06A9\u0646\u0648\u0646 ${money(summary.expectedCash)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0627\u0633\u062A. \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0644\u0627\u0641 \u0646\u0645\u0627\u06CC\u0634\u200C\u062F\u0627\u062F\u0647\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0628\u0627 \u0645\u0642\u062F\u0627\u0631 \u0641\u0639\u0644\u06CC \u0645\u062D\u0627\u0633\u0628\u0647 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.
              </div>` : ""}
      </div>

      <div class="glass rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-5">
        <h2 class="font-bold text-[14px] mb-3">\u06AF\u0631\u062F\u0634 \u0646\u0642\u062F</h2>
        ${movements.length === 0 ? emptyState({ title: "\u06AF\u0631\u062F\u0634\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647", message: "\u062F\u0631 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0648\u0631\u0648\u062F \u06CC\u0627 \u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C \u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", iconName: "wallet" }) : `<div class="table-wrap"><table class="data">
                <thead><tr><th>\u0632\u0645\u0627\u0646</th><th>\u0646\u0648\u0639</th><th>\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A</th><th>\u062A\u0648\u0636\u06CC\u062D</th><th>\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u0646\u062F\u0647</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>${movementRows}</tbody>
              </table></div>`}
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="glass rounded-3xl overflow-hidden">
      <div class="px-4 py-3 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 class="font-bold text-[14px]">\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A</h2>
        <span class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/35">${formatNumberFa(invoices.length)} \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F</span>
      </div>
      ${invoices.length === 0 ? emptyState({ title: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647", message: "\u062F\u0631 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u06CC \u0635\u0627\u062F\u0631 \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", iconName: "receipt" }) : `<div class="table-wrap"><table class="data">
              <thead><tr><th>#</th><th>\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC</th><th>\u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644</th><th>\u0632\u0645\u0627\u0646</th><th>\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A</th><th>\u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A</th><th>\u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>${invoiceRows}</tbody>
            </table></div>`}
    </div>
  </div>
  `;
  return appShell({
    title: "\u062C\u0632\u0626\u06CC\u0627\u062A \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A",
    role: "admin",
    userName,
    active: "shifts",
    body,
    scripts: "",
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}

// src/ui/admin.ts
init_db();

init_utils();
function tierBadge(tier) {
  const meta = TIER_META[tier] ?? TIER_META.bronze;
  return `<span class="badge ${meta.badge}">${meta.icon} ${meta.label}</span>`;
}
function statCard(options) {
  const accents = {
    gold: "bg-gold/10 border-gold/25 text-gold",
    emerald: "bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/25 text-emerald-300",
    rose: "bg-rose-500/10 border-rose-500/25 text-rose-300",
    sky: "bg-sky-500/10 border-sky-500/25 text-sky-300"
  };
  return `<div class="glass rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 card-hover">
    <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
      <div class="min-w-0">
        <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/45 mb-1.5">${escapeHtml(options.label)}</div>
        <div class="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">
          ${options.value}
          ${options.unit ? `<span class="text-[10px] font-normal text-cream/40 ms-1">${escapeHtml(options.unit)}</span>` : ""}
        </div>
        ${options.hint ? `<div class="text-[10px] text-cream/35 mt-1.5">${options.hint}</div>` : ""}
      </div>
      <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${accents[options.accent ?? "gold"]}">
        ${icon(options.iconName, "w-5 h-5")}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}
function panel(options) {
  return `<section class="glass rounded-3xl overflow-hidden ${options.className ?? ""}">
    <div class="px-5 py-4 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center gap-2">
      <span class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-gold/10 border border-gold/20 text-gold flex items-center justify-center">${icon(options.iconName, "w-4 h-4")}</span>
      <h2 class="font-bold text-sm">${escapeHtml(options.title)}</h2>
      ${options.actions ? `<div class="me-auto flex items-center gap-2">${options.actions}</div>` : ""}
    </div>
    ${options.body}
  </section>`;
}
function adminDashboardPage(input) {
  const { settings, userName, stats, weather, jobRuns, monthLabel, todayLabel } = input;
  const defaultPassword = input.defaultPassword === true;
  const openShifts = input.openShifts ?? [];
  const shiftStrip = openShifts.length ? `<div class="glass rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border border-emerald-500/25">
        <span class="badge tone-success">${icon("wallet", "w-3 h-3")} ${formatNumberFa(openShifts.length)} \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0628\u0627\u0632</span>
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          ${openShifts.map(
    (shift) => `<span class="badge tone-muted">${escapeHtml(shift.staff_name)} \xB7 \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0647 ${formatNumberFa(shift.opening_cash)}</span>`
  ).join("")}
        </div>
        <a href="/admin/shifts" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm me-auto">${icon("chart", "w-4 h-4")} \u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u200C\u0647\u0627</a>
      </div>` : `<div class="glass rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <span class="badge tone-muted">${icon("lock", "w-3 h-3")} \u0647\u06CC\u0686 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u06CC \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A</span>
        <span class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/35">\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u06A9\u0646\u062A\u0631\u0644 \u0646\u0642\u062F \u0648 \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0644\u0627\u0641 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u060C \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u200C\u062F\u0627\u0631 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0627\u0632 \u06A9\u0646\u062F.</span>
        <a href="/admin/shifts" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm me-auto">${icon("chart", "w-4 h-4")} \u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u200C\u0647\u0627</a>
      </div>`;
  const maxSale = Math.max(...stats.salesSeries.map((d) => d.total), 1);
  const bars = stats.salesSeries.map((point) => {
    const height = Math.max(3, Math.round(point.total / maxSale * 100));
    return `<div class="bar-col">
        <div class="bar-fill" style="height:${height}%">
          <span class="bar-tip">${formatNumberFa(point.total)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646</span>
        </div>
        <span class="text-[9.5px] text-cream/35 whitespace-nowrap">${formatJalali(point.date, false)}</span>
      </div>`;
  }).join("");
  const jobs = [
    { key: "churn", title: "\u067E\u06CC\u0634\u200C\u0628\u06CC\u0646\u06CC \u0631\u06CC\u0632\u0634", desc: "\u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u062F\u0644\u062A\u0646\u06AF\u06CC", iconName: "trending" },
    { key: "birthday", title: "\u062A\u0628\u0631\u06CC\u06A9 \u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F", desc: "\u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0635\u06CC", iconName: "gift" },
    { key: "weather", title: "\u0622\u0628\u200C\u0648\u0647\u0648\u0627", desc: "\u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u0631\u0648\u0632\u0627\u0646\u0647", iconName: "sun" },
    { key: "maintenance", title: "\u0646\u06AF\u0647\u062F\u0627\u0631\u06CC", desc: "\u0628\u0627\u0632\u0645\u062D\u0627\u0633\u0628\u0647 \u0648 \u067E\u0627\u06A9\u200C\u0633\u0627\u0632\u06CC", iconName: "refresh" },
    { key: "monthly", title: "\u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0645\u0627\u0647\u0627\u0646\u0647", desc: "\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u062F\u0645\u06CC\u0646\u200C\u0647\u0627", iconName: "chart" }
  ];
  const jobsHtml = jobs.map((job) => {
    const run = jobRuns[job.key];
    return `<div class="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 border-b border-white/5 last:border-0">
        <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-gold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
          ${icon(job.iconName, "w-4 h-4")}
        </div>
        <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div class="text-[12px] font-medium">${job.title}</div>
          <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/35">
            ${run && run.at ? `\u0622\u062E\u0631\u06CC\u0646 \u0627\u062C\u0631\u0627: ${formatJalaliDateTime(run.at)}` : job.desc}
          </div>
        </div>
        <button type="button" data-job="${job.key}" class="run-job btn btn-ghost btn-sm !px-2.5" title="\u0627\u062C\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06CC">
          ${icon("refresh", "w-3.5 h-3.5")}
        </button>
      </div>`;
  }).join("");
  const body = `

    body: stats.recentInvoices.length === 0 ? emptyState({ title: "\u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647", message: "\u0627\u0632 \u067E\u0646\u0644 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0645\u0646\u062F \u0627\u0648\u0644\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0631\u0627 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.", iconName: "receipt" }) : `<div class="table-wrap">

          <div class="px-4 py-3 text-[9.5px] text-cream/30 leading-5 border-t border-white/5">
            \u062C\u0627\u0628\u200C\u0647\u0627 \u0637\u0628\u0642 \u0632\u0645\u0627\u0646\u200C\u0628\u0646\u062F\u06CC Cron \u0627\u062C\u0631\u0627 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u0646\u062F. \u0627\u062C\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06CC \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u062A\u0633\u062A \u0627\u0633\u062A \u0648 \u0645\u0645\u06A9\u0646 \u0627\u0633\u062A \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u062A\u06A9\u0631\u0627\u0631\u06CC \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u06A9\u0646\u062F.
          </div>`
  })}
    </div>

    iconName: "star",
    body: stats.topCustomers.length === 0 ? emptyState({ title: "\u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644\u06CC \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A", message: "\u0628\u0627 \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u060C \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646 \u0634\u0631\u0648\u0639 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.", iconName: "users" }) : `<div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 divide-y sm:divide-y-0 sm:divide-x sm:divide-x-reverse divide-white/5">

                      <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-300 to-amber-800 text-coffee-950 font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
                        ${escapeHtml(customer.name.slice(0, 1))}
                      </div>
                      <span class="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 rounded-full gold-bg text-coffee-950 text-[9px] font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center">${toPersianDigits(index + 1)}</span>

  (function () {
    $$('.run-job').forEach(function (button) {
      button.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var job = button.dataset.job;
        setLoading(button, true, '');
        try {
          var result = await api('/api/admin/jobs/' + encodeURIComponent(job), { method: 'POST' });
          toast(result.message, result.ok ? 'success' : 'error');
          setTimeout(function () { window.location.reload(); }, 1400);
        } catch (error) {
          toast(error.message, 'error');
          setLoading(button, false);
        }
      });

  return appShell({
    title: "\u062F\u0627\u0634\u0628\u0648\u0631\u062F",
    role: "admin",
    userName,

    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function adminCustomersPage(input) {
  const { settings, userName, customers, total, page, pageSize, params } = input;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
  const queryString = (newPage) => {
    const search = new URLSearchParams();
    if (params.q) search.set("q", params.q);
    if (params.tier) search.set("tier", params.tier);
    if (params.status) search.set("status", params.status);
    if (params.sort) search.set("sort", params.sort);
    search.set("page", String(newPage));
    return `/admin/customers?${search.toString()}`;
  };
  const rows = customers.map((customer) => {
    const status = customerActivityStatus(customer, settings.churn_grace_days, settings.churn_min_visits);
    return `<tr>
        <td>
          <a href="/admin/customers/${customer.id}" class="flex items-center gap-2.5 group">
            <span class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-300 to-amber-800 text-coffee-950 text-[12px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">${escapeHtml(customer.name.slice(0, 1))}</span>
            <span class="min-w-0">
              <span class="block text-[12.5px] font-medium group-hover:text-gold transition truncate">${escapeHtml(customer.name)}</span>
              <span class="block text-[10.5px] text-cream/35 font-mono" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(customer.phone)}</span>
            </span>
          </a>
        </td>
        <td>${tierBadge(customer.tier)}</td>
        <td class="font-bold text-amber-200">${formatNumberFa(customer.points)}</td>
        <td class="text-[12px]">${formatNumberFa(customer.visits)}</td>
        <td class="text-[12px] text-cream/60">${formatNumberFa(customer.total_spent)}</td>
        <td class="text-[11px] text-cream/45 whitespace-nowrap">${customer.last_visit_date ? formatJalali(customer.last_visit_date) : "\u2014"}</td>
        <td><span class="badge tone-${status.tone}">${status.label}</span></td>
        <td>
          <a href="/admin/customers/${customer.id}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm !px-2" title="\u062C\u0632\u0626\u06CC\u0627\u062A">
            ${icon("left", "w-3.5 h-3.5")}
          </a>
        </td>
      </tr>`;
  }).join("");
  const cards = customers.map((customer) => {
    const status = customerActivityStatus(customer, settings.churn_grace_days, settings.churn_min_visits);
    return `<a href="/admin/customers/${customer.id}" class="block glass rounded-2xl p-4 active:scale-[.99] transition">
        <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
          <span class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-300 to-amber-800 text-coffee-950 font-bold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">${escapeHtml(customer.name.slice(0, 1))}</span>
          <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div class="text-[13px] font-semibold truncate">${escapeHtml(customer.name)}</div>
              ${tierBadge(customer.tier)}
            </div>
            <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/40 font-mono mt-0.5" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(customer.phone)}</div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mt-2.5 text-[10.5px] text-cream/45">
              <span>${formatNumberFa(customer.points)} \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</span>
              <span>${formatNumberFa(customer.visits)} \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F</span>
              <span class="badge tone-${status.tone} !text-[9.5px] !py-0.5">${status.label}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </a>`;
  }).join("");
  const pagination = `<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3 border-t border-white/10">
    <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/40">\u0635\u0641\u062D\u0647 ${toPersianDigits(page)} \u0627\u0632 ${toPersianDigits(totalPages)} \u2014 ${formatNumberFa(total)} \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC</div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
      <a href="${queryString(Math.max(1, page - 1))}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm ${page <= 1 ? "opacity-40 pointer-events-none" : ""}">${icon("right", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u0642\u0628\u0644\u06CC</a>
      <a href="${queryString(Math.min(totalPages, page + 1))}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm ${page >= totalPages ? "opacity-40 pointer-events-none" : ""}">\u0628\u0639\u062F\u06CC ${icon("left", "w-3.5 h-3.5")}</a>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  const body = `
  <div class="p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
    ${pageHeader({
    title: "\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646",
    subtitle: "\u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648\u060C \u0641\u06CC\u0644\u062A\u0631 \u0648 \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062A \u0627\u0639\u0636\u0627\u06CC \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647",
    iconName: "users",
    actions: `<a href="/admin/export/customers.csv" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("download", "w-4 h-4")} \u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C\u06CC CSV</a>
        <button type="button" id="open-add" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("plus", "w-4 h-4")} \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F</button>`
  })}

    <form method="get" action="/admin/customers" class="glass rounded-2xl p-4 mb-4">
      <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_auto_auto_auto_auto] gap-2.5">
        <div class="relative">
          <span class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-cream/30">${icon("search", "w-4 h-4")}</span>
          <input type="search" name="q" value="${escapeHtml(params.q ?? "")}" class="field !pr-10" placeholder="\u0646\u0627\u0645\u060C \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644 \u06CC\u0627 \u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641\u2026" />
        </div>
        <select name="tier" class="field sm:w-auto" onchange="this.form.submit()">
          <option value="">\u0647\u0645\u0647 \u0633\u0637\u062D\u200C\u0647\u0627</option>
          ${["bronze", "silver", "gold", "vip"].map(
    (tier) => `<option value="${tier}" ${params.tier === tier ? "selected" : ""}>${TIER_META[tier].label}</option>`
  ).join("")}
        </select>
        <select name="status" class="field sm:w-auto" onchange="this.form.submit()">
          <option value="">\u0647\u0645\u0647 \u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627</option>
          <option value="active" ${params.status === "active" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644</option>
          <option value="sleeping" ${params.status === "sleeping" ? "selected" : ""}>\u062F\u0631 \u062E\u0637\u0631 \u0631\u06CC\u0632\u0634</option>
          <option value="no_visit" ${params.status === "no_visit" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0628\u062F\u0648\u0646 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F</option>
        </select>
        <select name="sort" class="field sm:w-auto" onchange="this.form.submit()">
          <option value="">\u0622\u062E\u0631\u06CC\u0646 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F</option>
          <option value="points" ${params.sort === "points" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0628\u06CC\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0646 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</option>
          <option value="created" ${params.sort === "created" ? "selected" : ""}>\u062A\u0627\u0632\u0647\u200C\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0646 \u0639\u0636\u0648\u06CC\u062A</option>
          <option value="name" ${params.sort === "name" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0646\u0627\u0645 (\u0627\u0644\u0641\u0628\u0627)</option>
        </select>
        <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("filter", "w-4 h-4")} \u0627\u0639\u0645\u0627\u0644</button>
      </div>
    </form>

    <div class="hidden md:block glass rounded-3xl overflow-hidden">
      ${customers.length === 0 ? emptyState({
    title: "\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u200C\u0627\u06CC \u067E\u06CC\u062F\u0627 \u0646\u0634\u062F",
    message: "\u0641\u06CC\u0644\u062A\u0631\u0647\u0627 \u0631\u0627 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u062F\u0647\u06CC\u062F \u06CC\u0627 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F \u0627\u0636\u0627\u0641\u0647 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.",
    iconName: "users",
    action: `<button type="button" onclick="document.getElementById('open-add').click()" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("plus", "w-4 h-4")} \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F</button>`
  }) : `<div class="table-wrap">
              <table class="data">
                <thead><tr><th>\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC</th><th>\u0633\u0637\u062D</th><th>\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</th><th>\u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u0647\u0627</th><th>\u0645\u062C\u0645\u0648\u0639 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F</th><th>\u0622\u062E\u0631\u06CC\u0646 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F</th><th>\u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A</th><th></th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
            ${pagination}`}
    </div>

    <div class="md:hidden space-y-2.5">
      ${customers.length === 0 ? `<div class="glass rounded-2xl py-10 text-center text-xs text-cream/40">\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u200C\u0627\u06CC \u067E\u06CC\u062F\u0627 \u0646\u0634\u062F.</div>` : cards}
      ${customers.length === 0 ? "" : pagination}
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Add customer modal -->
  <div id="add-modal" class="hidden modal-backdrop">
    <div class="modal-panel glass-strong rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
        <h2 class="text-lg font-bold">\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F</h2>
        <button type="button" id="close-add" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg text-cream/40 hover:text-cream hover:bg-white/5 flex items-center justify-center">${icon("close", "w-4 h-4")}</button>
      </div>
      <form id="add-form" class="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label class="label">\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0648 \u0646\u0627\u0645 \u062E\u0627\u0646\u0648\u0627\u062F\u06AF\u06CC</label>
          <input id="add-name" type="text" class="field" maxlength="60" placeholder="\u0645\u062B\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0639\u0644\u06CC \u0631\u0636\u0627\u06CC\u06CC" required />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label">\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644</label>
          <input id="add-phone" type="tel" inputmode="numeric" dir="ltr" class="field text-left tracking-wider" placeholder="09xxxxxxxxx" maxlength="13" required />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label">\u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E \u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F</label>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            <select id="add-day" class="field"></select>
            <select id="add-month" class="field">
              ${["\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0631\u062F\u06CC\u0646", "\u0627\u0631\u062F\u06CC\u0628\u0647\u0634\u062A", "\u062E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062F", "\u062A\u06CC\u0631", "\u0645\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062F", "\u0634\u0647\u0631\u06CC\u0648\u0631", "\u0645\u0647\u0631", "\u0622\u0628\u0627\u0646", "\u0622\u0630\u0631", "\u062F\u06CC", "\u0628\u0647\u0645\u0646", "\u0627\u0633\u0641\u0646\u062F"].map((m, i) => `<option value="${i + 1}">${m}</option>`).join("")}
            </select>
            <select id="add-year" class="field"></select>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label">\u06A9\u062F \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641 <span class="text-cream/30">(\u0627\u062E\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0631\u06CC)</span></label>
          <input id="add-referral" type="text" dir="ltr" class="field text-left tracking-wider" placeholder="REF-XXXXXX" maxlength="30" />
        </div>
        <div class="flex gap-2 pt-1">
          <button type="button" id="cancel-add" class="btn btn-ghost flex-1">\u0627\u0646\u0635\u0631\u0627\u0641</button>
          <button type="submit" id="add-submit" class="btn btn-primary flex-1">${icon("plus", "w-4 h-4")} \u0627\u0641\u0632\u0648\u062F\u0646</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>
  `;
  const scripts = `
  (function () {
    var modal = $('#add-modal');
    initJalaliPickers($('#add-day'), $('#add-month'), $('#add-year'), '', 90);

    $('#open-add').addEventListener('click', function () { modal.classList.remove('hidden'); $('#add-name').focus(); });
    $('#close-add').addEventListener('click', function () { modal.classList.add('hidden'); });
    $('#cancel-add').addEventListener('click', function () { modal.classList.add('hidden'); });
    modal.addEventListener('click', function (event) { if (event.target === modal) modal.classList.add('hidden'); });

    function normalizePhone(value) {
      var v = String(value || '').replace(/[\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, function (d) { return String('\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9'.indexOf(d)); });
      v = v.replace(/[\\s\\-()]/g, '');
      if (v.indexOf('+98') === 0) v = '0' + v.slice(3);
      else if (v.indexOf('0098') === 0) v = '0' + v.slice(4);
      else if (v.indexOf('98') === 0 && v.length === 12) v = '0' + v.slice(2);
      else if (v.indexOf('9') === 0 && v.length === 10) v = '0' + v;
      return v;
    }

    $('#add-form').addEventListener('submit', async function (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      var name = $('#add-name').value.trim();
      var phone = normalizePhone($('#add-phone').value);
      var birth = readJalaliPickers($('#add-day'), $('#add-month'), $('#add-year'));
      var referral = $('#add-referral').value.trim();
      if (name.length < 2) { toast('\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0631\u0627 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); return; }
      if (!/^09\\d{9}$/.test(phone)) { toast('\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644 \u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.', 'error'); return; }

      var btn = $('#add-submit');
      setLoading(btn, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u062B\u0628\u062A\u2026');
      try {
        await api('/api/admin/customers', { method: 'POST', body: { name: name, phone: phone, birth_date: birth, referral: referral } });
        toast('\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0628\u0627 \u0645\u0648\u0641\u0642\u06CC\u062A \u0627\u0636\u0627\u0641\u0647 \u0634\u062F.', 'success');
        modal.classList.add('hidden');
        setTimeout(function () { window.location.reload(); }, 900);
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
        setLoading(btn, false);
      }
    });
  })();
  `;
  return appShell({
    title: "\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646",
    role: "admin",
    userName,
    active: "customers",
    body,
    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function adminCustomerDetailPage(input) {
  const { settings, userName, user, invoices, coupons, referrer, referrals, totalSpent, visitCount } = input;
  const status = customerActivityStatus(
    { ...user, visits: visitCount },
    settings.churn_grace_days,
    settings.churn_min_visits
  );
  const body = `
  <div class="p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
    <a href="/admin/customers" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11.5px] text-cream/45 hover:text-gold mb-4 transition">
      ${icon("right", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u0628\u0627\u0632\u06AF\u0634\u062A \u0628\u0647 \u0641\u0647\u0631\u0633\u062A \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646
    </a>

    <div class="glass rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-6 mb-4 relative overflow-hidden">
      <div class="absolute -top-24 -left-16 w-64 h-64 rounded-full bg-gold/10 blur-3xl"></div>
      <div class="relative flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center gap-4">
        <div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-300 to-amber-800 text-coffee-950 text-2xl font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-amber-900/30">
          ${escapeHtml(user.name.slice(0, 1))}
        </div>
        <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            <h1 class="text-xl font-extrabold">${escapeHtml(user.name)}</h1>
            ${tierBadge(user.tier)}
            <span class="badge tone-${status.tone}">${status.label}</span>
            ${user.is_active === 0 ? '<span class="badge tone-danger">\u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644</span>' : ""}
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-4 mt-1.5 text-[11.5px] text-cream/45 flex-wrap">
            <span class="font-mono" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(user.phone)}</span>
            <span>\u0639\u0636\u0648 \u0627\u0632 ${formatJalali(user.created_at)}</span>
            <span class="font-mono" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(user.referral_code ?? "\u2014")}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button type="button" id="open-coupon" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("gift", "w-4 h-4")} \u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641</button>
          ${user.is_active === 1 ? `<button type="button" id="deactivate" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("close", "w-4 h-4")} \u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644</button>` : `<button type="button" id="activate" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("check", "w-4 h-4")} \u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644\u200C\u0633\u0627\u0632\u06CC</button>`}
          <button type="button" id="delete-customer" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm">${icon("trash", "w-4 h-4")} \u062D\u0630\u0641</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-4">
      ${statCard({ label: "\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0641\u0639\u0644\u06CC", value: formatNumberFa(user.points), iconName: "star", accent: "gold" })}
      ${statCard({ label: "\u0645\u062C\u0645\u0648\u0639 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F", value: formatNumberFa(totalSpent), unit: "\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646", iconName: "wallet", accent: "emerald" })}
      ${statCard({ label: "\u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F", value: formatNumberFa(visitCount), iconName: "receipt", accent: "sky" })}
      ${statCard({
    label: "\u0645\u06CC\u0627\u0646\u06AF\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u0635\u0644\u0647 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F",
    value: user.avg_visit_interval > 0 ? `${toPersianDigits(user.avg_visit_interval)}` : "\u2014",
    unit: user.avg_visit_interval > 0 ? "\u0631\u0648\u0632" : void 0,
    iconName: "clock",
    accent: "rose"
  })}
    </div>

    <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-[1.1fr_.9fr] gap-4 mb-4">
      ${panel({
    title: "\u0648\u06CC\u0631\u0627\u06CC\u0634 \u0627\u0637\u0644\u0627\u0639\u0627\u062A",

    body: `<form id="edit-form" class="p-5 space-y-4">
          <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label class="label">\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0648 \u0646\u0627\u0645 \u062E\u0627\u0646\u0648\u0627\u062F\u06AF\u06CC</label>
              <input id="e-name" type="text" class="field" maxlength="60" value="${escapeHtml(user.name)}" />
            </div>

              <label class="label">\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0648\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u0644</label>
              <input id="e-phone" type="tel" dir="ltr" class="field text-left tracking-wider" maxlength="13" value="${escapeHtml(user.phone)}" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="label">\u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E \u062A\u0648\u0644\u062F</label>

                ${["\u0641\u0631\u0648\u0631\u062F\u06CC\u0646", "\u0627\u0631\u062F\u06CC\u0628\u0647\u0634\u062A", "\u062E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062F", "\u062A\u06CC\u0631", "\u0645\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062F", "\u0634\u0647\u0631\u06CC\u0648\u0631", "\u0645\u0647\u0631", "\u0622\u0628\u0627\u0646", "\u0622\u0630\u0631", "\u062F\u06CC", "\u0628\u0647\u0645\u0646", "\u0627\u0633\u0641\u0646\u062F"].map((m, i) => `<option value="${i + 1}">${m}</option>`).join("")}
              </select>
              <select id="e-year" class="field"></select>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="flex justify-end">
            <button type="submit" id="save-btn" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("check", "w-4 h-4")} \u0630\u062E\u06CC\u0631\u0647 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631\u0627\u062A</button>

      ${panel({
