    iconName: "star",
    body: `<form id="points-form" class="p-5 space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label class="label">\u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</label>
            <div class="flex gap-2">
              <input id="p-delta" type="number" class="field text-left" dir="ltr" placeholder="\u0645\u062B\u0644\u0627\u064B 100 \u06CC\u0627 -50" />
              <button type="submit" id="points-btn" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm shrink-0">\u0627\u0639\u0645\u0627\u0644</button>
            </div>
            <p class="hint">\u0639\u062F\u062F \u0645\u062B\u0628\u062A \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u0641\u0632\u0648\u062F\u0646 \u0648 \u0639\u062F\u062F \u0645\u0646\u0641\u06CC \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u06A9\u0633\u0631 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632. \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0646\u0641\u06CC \u0627\u0639\u0645\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="rounded-xl bg-black/25 border border-white/10 p-3 text-[11px] text-cream/50 leading-6">
            \u0633\u0637\u062D \u0641\u0639\u0644\u06CC: <span class="text-cream font-semibold">${tierLabel(user.tier)}</span>
            \u2014 \u067E\u0633 \u0627\u0632 \u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645\u060C \u0633\u0637\u062D \u0628\u0647\u200C\u0635\u0648\u0631\u062A \u062E\u0648\u062F\u06A9\u0627\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0632\u0645\u062D\u0627\u0633\u0628\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.
          </div>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/40 leading-6">
            ${referrer ? `\u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641: <a href="/admin/customers/${referrer.id}" class="text-gold hover:underline">${escapeHtml(referrer.name)}</a>` : "\u0628\u062F\u0648\u0646 \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641"}
          </div>
        </form>`
  })}
    </div>

    ${panel({
    title: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627",
    iconName: "receipt",
    actions: `<span class="badge tone-muted">${toPersianDigits(visitCount)} \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631</span>`,
    body: invoices.length === 0 ? emptyState({ title: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647", message: "\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.", iconName: "receipt" }) : `<div class="table-wrap max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto">
              <table class="data">
                <thead><tr><th>#</th><th>\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A</th><th>\u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641</th><th>\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</th><th>\u06A9\u062F</th><th>\u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E</th><th>\u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A</th><th></th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>
                  ${invoices.map(
      (invoice) => `<tr>
                        <td class="text-cream/35 text-[11px]">${toPersianDigits(invoice.id)}</td>
                        <td class="font-bold text-amber-200 whitespace-nowrap">${formatNumberFa(invoice.amount)}</td>
                        <td class="text-[11px] text-emerald-300/70">${invoice.discount > 0 ? formatNumberFa(invoice.discount) : "\u2014"}</td>
                        <td class="text-[11px]">+${toPersianDigits(invoice.points_earned)}</td>
                        <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/40 font-mono" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(invoice.coupon_code ?? "\u2014")}</td>
                        <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/40 whitespace-nowrap">${formatJalaliDateTime(invoice.created_at)}</td>
                        <td>${invoice.voided_at ? `<span class="badge tone-danger" title="\u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0646: ${escapeHtml(invoice.voided_at)}">\u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647</span>` : `<span class="badge tone-success">\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631</span>`}</td>
                        <td class="text-left">${invoice.voided_at ? "" : `<button type="button" data-void-id="${invoice.id}" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm whitespace-nowrap">${icon("close", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644</button>`}</td>
                      </tr>`
    ).join("")}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>`
  })}

    <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-4">
      ${panel({
    title: "\u06A9\u062F\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641",
    iconName: "gift",
    body: coupons.length === 0 ? `<p class="text-[11px] text-cream/35 text-center py-6">\u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641\u06CC \u0646\u062F\u0627\u0631\u062F.</p>` : `<div class="divide-y divide-white/5 max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto">
                ${coupons.map((coupon) => {
      const expired = coupon.expires_at ? Date.parse(`${coupon.expires_at.replace(" ", "T")}Z`) < Date.now() : false;
      const inactive = coupon.is_active === 0 || expired || coupon.used_count >= coupon.max_uses;
      return `<div class="px-4 py-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <div>
                        <div class="font-mono text-[11.5px] tracking-wider" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(coupon.code)}</div>
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/35 mt-0.5">
                          ${coupon.kind === "percent" ? `${toPersianDigits(coupon.value)}\u066A \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641` : `${formatNumberFa(coupon.value)} \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646`}
                          \xB7 ${toPersianDigits(coupon.used_count)}/${toPersianDigits(coupon.max_uses)} \u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647
                          ${coupon.expires_at ? ` \xB7 \u062A\u0627 ${formatJalali(coupon.expires_at)}` : ""}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <span class="badge ${inactive ? "tone-muted" : "tone-success"}">${inactive ? "\u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647" : "\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644"}</span>
                    </div>`;
    }).join("")}
              </div>`
  })}

      ${panel({
    title: "\u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641\u06CC\u200C\u0647\u0627",
    iconName: "users",
    body: referrals.length === 0 ? `<p class="text-[11px] text-cream/35 text-center py-6">\u0647\u0646\u0648\u0632 \u06A9\u0633\u06CC \u0631\u0627 \u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641\u06CC \u0646\u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.</p>` : `<div class="divide-y divide-white/5">
                ${referrals.map(
      (referral) => `<a href="/admin/customers/${referral.id}" class="px-4 py-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 hover:bg-white/[0.03] transition">
                      <div class="text-[12px]">${escapeHtml(referral.name)}</div>
                      <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span class="badge ${referral.rewarded ? "tone-success" : "tone-muted"}">${referral.rewarded ? "\u067E\u0627\u062F\u0627\u0634 \u062F\u0627\u062F\u0647 \u0634\u062F" : "\u062F\u0631 \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F"}</span>
                        <span class="text-[10px] text-cream/30">${formatJalali(referral.created_at)}</span>
                      </div>
                    </a>`
    ).join("")}
              </div>`
  })}
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Coupon modal -->
  <div id="coupon-modal" class="hidden modal-backdrop">
    <div class="modal-panel glass-strong rounded-3xl max-w-sm w-full p-6">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <h2 class="text-base font-bold">\u06A9\u062F \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F</h2>
        <button type="button" id="close-coupon" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg text-cream/40 hover:text-cream hover:bg-white/5 flex items-center justify-center">${icon("close", "w-4 h-4")}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label class="label">\u0646\u0648\u0639</label>
          <select id="cp-kind" class="field">
            <option value="percent">\u062F\u0631\u0635\u062F\u06CC</option>
            <option value="amount">\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u062B\u0627\u0628\u062A (\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646)</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label">\u0645\u0642\u062F\u0627\u0631</label>
          <input id="cp-value" type="number" class="field text-left" dir="ltr" value="10" min="1" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label">\u0627\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 (\u0631\u0648\u0632)</label>
          <input id="cp-days" type="number" class="field text-left" dir="ltr" value="30" min="1" max="365" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label">\u062D\u062F\u0627\u06A9\u062B\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u0641\u0627\u062F\u0647</label>
          <input id="cp-max" type="number" class="field text-left" dir="ltr" value="1" min="1" max="100" />
        </div>
        <button type="button" id="create-coupon" class="btn btn-primary w-full">${icon("gift", "w-4 h-4")} \u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A \u06A9\u062F</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  `;
  const scripts = `
  (function () {
    var userId = ${user.id};
    initJalaliPickers($('#e-day'), $('#e-month'), $('#e-year'), ${jsonForScript(user.birth_date ?? "")}, 90);

    $('#edit-form').addEventListener('submit', async function (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      var name = $('#e-name').value.trim();
      var phone = $('#e-phone').value.trim();
      var birth = readJalaliPickers($('#e-day'), $('#e-month'), $('#e-year'));
      if (name.length < 2) { toast('\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0631\u0627 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); return; }
      var btn = $('#save-btn');
      setLoading(btn, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0630\u062E\u06CC\u0631\u0647\u2026');
      try {
        await api('/api/admin/customers/' + userId, { method: 'PUT', body: { name: name, phone: phone, birth_date: birth } });
        toast('\u0627\u0637\u0644\u0627\u0639\u0627\u062A \u0630\u062E\u06CC\u0631\u0647 \u0634\u062F.', 'success');
        setTimeout(function () { window.location.reload(); }, 800);
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
        setLoading(btn, false);
      }
    });

    $('#points-form').addEventListener('submit', async function (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      var delta = Number($('#p-delta').value);
      if (!delta) { toast('\u0645\u0642\u062F\u0627\u0631 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u0631\u0627 \u0648\u0627\u0631\u062F \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.', 'error'); return; }
      var btn = $('#points-btn');
      setLoading(btn, true, '');
      try {
        var result = await api('/api/admin/customers/' + userId + '/points', { method: 'POST', body: { delta: delta } });
        toast('\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F: ' + fa(result.points), 'success');
        setTimeout(function () { window.location.reload(); }, 800);
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
        setLoading(btn, false);
      }
    });

    function toggleActive(action) {
      return async function () {
        var ok = await confirmAction({
          title: action === 'deactivate' ? '\u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC' : '\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644\u200C\u0633\u0627\u0632\u06CC \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC',
          message: action === 'deactivate' ? '\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062F\u06CC\u06AF\u0631 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9 \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u062F \u0648 \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632\u0634 \u062D\u0641\u0638 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.' : '\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.',
          confirmText: action === 'deactivate' ? '\u063A\u06CC\u0631\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u06A9\u0646' : '\u0641\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u06A9\u0646',
          danger: action === 'deactivate'
        });
        if (!ok) return;
        try {
          await api('/api/admin/customers/' + userId + '/' + action, { method: 'POST' });
          toast('\u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0628\u0647\u200C\u0631\u0648\u0632\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0646\u06CC \u0634\u062F.', 'success');
          setTimeout(function () { window.location.reload(); }, 700);
        } catch (error) {
          toast(error.message, 'error');
        }
      };
    }
    if ($('#deactivate')) $('#deactivate').addEventListener('click', toggleActive('deactivate'));
    if ($('#activate')) $('#activate').addEventListener('click', toggleActive('activate'));

    // \u062D\u0630\u0641 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u2014 \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0628\u0627 \u062A\u0623\u06CC\u06CC\u062F \u0635\u0631\u06CC\u062D \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631
    var deleteBtn = $('#delete-customer');
    if (deleteBtn) {
      deleteBtn.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        if (!confirm('\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u0648 \u062A\u0645\u0627\u0645 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627\u060C \u06A9\u062F\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0648 \u0644\u0627\u06AF \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC\u0634 \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0647\u0645\u06CC\u0634\u0647 \u062D\u0630\u0641 \u0634\u0648\u062F\u061F \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06A9\u0627\u0631 \u0628\u0631\u06AF\u0634\u062A\u200C\u067E\u0630\u06CC\u0631 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A.')) return;
        if (!confirm('\u0645\u0637\u0645\u0626\u0646 \u0647\u0633\u062A\u06CC\u062F\u061F \u067E\u06CC\u0634\u0646\u0647\u0627\u062F \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u0645 \u067E\u06CC\u0634 \u0627\u0632 \u062D\u0630\u0641\u060C \u062E\u0631\u0648\u062C\u06CC \u067E\u0634\u062A\u06CC\u0628\u0627\u0646 \u0628\u06AF\u06CC\u0631\u06CC\u062F.')) return;

        setLoading(deleteBtn, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u062D\u0630\u0641\u2026');
        try {
          await api('/api/admin/customers/' + userId, { method: 'DELETE' });
          toast('\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u062D\u0630\u0641 \u0634\u062F.', 'success');
          setTimeout(function () { location.href = '/admin/customers'; }, 700);
        } catch (error) {
          toast(error.message, 'error');
          setLoading(deleteBtn, false);
        }
      });
    }

    var modal = $('#coupon-modal');
    $('#open-coupon').addEventListener('click', function () { modal.classList.remove('hidden'); });
    $('#close-coupon').addEventListener('click', function () { modal.classList.add('hidden'); });
    modal.addEventListener('click', function (event) { if (event.target === modal) modal.classList.add('hidden'); });

    $('#create-coupon').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var btn = $('#create-coupon');
      setLoading(btn, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A\u2026');
      try {
        var result = await api('/api/admin/coupons', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: {
            userId: userId,
            kind: $('#cp-kind').value,
            value: Number($('#cp-value').value),
            days: Number($('#cp-days').value),
            maxUses: Number($('#cp-max').value),
            reason: 'manual'
          }
        });
        toast('\u06A9\u062F ' + result.code + ' \u0633\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0647 \u0634\u062F.', 'success');
        setTimeout(function () { window.location.reload(); }, 1000);
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
        setLoading(btn, false);
      }
    });
  })();
  `;
  return appShell({
    title: user.name,
    role: "admin",
    userName,
    active: "customers",
    body,
    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function adminInvoicesPage(input) {
  const {
    settings,
    userName,
    invoices,
    total,
    sum,
    discount = 0,
    voidedCount = 0,
    paymentTotals = { cash: 0, card: 0, online: 0 },
    staffOptions = [],
    page,
    pageSize,
    filters
  } = input;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
  const pageLink = (newPage) => {
    const search = new URLSearchParams();
    if (filters.q) search.set("q", filters.q);
    if (filters.from) search.set("from", filters.from);
    if (filters.to) search.set("to", filters.to);
    if (filters.status && filters.status !== "all") search.set("status", filters.status);
    if (filters.payment) search.set("payment", filters.payment);
    if (filters.staff) search.set("staff", String(filters.staff));
    search.set("page", String(newPage));
    return `/admin/invoices?${search.toString()}`;
  };
  const body = `
  <div class="p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
    ${pageHeader({
    title: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627",
    subtitle: "\u062A\u0645\u0627\u0645 \u062E\u0631\u06CC\u062F\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u062F\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647",
    iconName: "receipt",
    actions: `<a href="/staff" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("plus", "w-4 h-4")} \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631</a>`
  })}

    <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-4">
      ${statCard({ label: "\u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627", value: formatNumberFa(total), iconName: "receipt", accent: "gold" })}
      ${statCard({ label: "\u0645\u062C\u0645\u0648\u0639 \u0641\u0631\u0648\u0634 (\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631)", value: formatNumberFa(sum), unit: "\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646", iconName: "wallet", accent: "emerald" })}
      ${statCard({ label: "\u0645\u062C\u0645\u0648\u0639 \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641", value: formatNumberFa(discount), unit: "\u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646", iconName: "gift", accent: "sky" })}
      ${statCard({ label: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647", value: formatNumberFa(voidedCount), iconName: "close", accent: "rose" })}
    </div>

    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-4 text-[11px]">
      <span class="badge tone-success">\u0646\u0642\u062F: ${formatNumberFa(paymentTotals.cash)}</span>
      <span class="badge tone-info">\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A: ${formatNumberFa(paymentTotals.card)}</span>
      <span class="badge tone-warning">\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646: ${formatNumberFa(paymentTotals.online)}</span>
      <span class="text-cream/30">\u062C\u0645\u0639 \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0647\u0645\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u06CC\u0644\u062A\u0631 (\u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631)</span>
    </div>

    <form id="filter-form" method="get" action="/admin/invoices" class="glass rounded-2xl p-4 mb-4">
      <div class="grid gap-2.5 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_auto_auto_auto]">
        <input type="search" name="q" value="${escapeHtml(filters.q ?? "")}" class="field" placeholder="\u0646\u0627\u0645\u060C \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC \u06CC\u0627 \u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u2026" />

        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="text-[10px] text-cream/35 shrink-0">\u0627\u0632 \u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E</span>
          <select id="from-day" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
          <select id="from-month" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
          <select id="from-year" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
        </div>

        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="text-[10px] text-cream/35 shrink-0">\u062A\u0627 \u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E</span>
          <select id="to-day" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
          <select id="to-month" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
          <select id="to-year" class="field !w-auto !px-2 !py-1.5 text-[11px]"></select>
        </div>

        <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("filter", "w-4 h-4")} \u0641\u06CC\u0644\u062A\u0631</button>
      </div>
      <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 mt-2.5">
        <select name="payment" class="field !py-2 text-[12px]">
          <option value="">\u0647\u0645\u0647\u0654 \u0631\u0648\u0634\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A</option>
          ${[
    { value: "cash", label: "\u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC" },
    { value: "card", label: "\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A\u200C\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646" },
    { value: "online", label: "\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646" },
    { value: "mixed", label: "\u062A\u0631\u06A9\u06CC\u0628\u06CC" },
    { value: "unknown", label: "\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 (\u0642\u062F\u06CC\u0645\u06CC)" }
  ].map(
    (option) => `<option value="${option.value}" ${filters.payment === option.value ? "selected" : ""}>${option.label}</option>`
  ).join("")}
        </select>
        <select name="status" class="field !py-2 text-[12px]">
          <option value="all" ${!filters.status || filters.status === "all" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0647\u0645\u0647\u0654 \u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627</option>
          <option value="valid" ${filters.status === "valid" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631</option>
          <option value="voided" ${filters.status === "voided" ? "selected" : ""}>\u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647</option>
        </select>
        <select name="staff" class="field !py-2 text-[12px]">
          <option value="">\u0647\u0645\u0647\u0654 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642\u200C\u062F\u0627\u0631\u0647\u0627</option>
          ${staffOptions.map(
    (member) => `<option value="${member.id}" ${filters.staff === member.id ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(member.name)}</option>`
  ).join("")}
        </select>
      </div>
      <input type="hidden" name="from" id="from-iso" value="${escapeHtml(filters.from ?? "")}" />
      <input type="hidden" name="to" id="to-iso" value="${escapeHtml(filters.to ?? "")}" />
      <p class="hint">\u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E\u200C\u0647\u0627 \u0634\u0645\u0633\u06CC\u200C\u0627\u0646\u062F\u061B \u062E\u0627\u0644\u06CC \u06AF\u0630\u0627\u0634\u062A\u0646 \u06CC\u0639\u0646\u06CC \u0628\u062F\u0648\u0646 \u0645\u062D\u062F\u0648\u062F\u06CC\u062A. \u0628\u0627\u0632\u0647 \xAB\u062A\u0627\xBB \u0634\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0647\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0647\u0645 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F.</p>
    </form>

    <div class="glass rounded-3xl overflow-hidden">
      ${invoices.length === 0 ? emptyState({ title: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u06CC \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F", message: "\u0628\u0627 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631 \u0641\u06CC\u0644\u062A\u0631\u0647\u0627 \u062F\u0648\u0628\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u062A\u0644\u0627\u0634 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.", iconName: "receipt" }) : `<div class="table-wrap">
              <table class="data">
                <thead><tr><th>#</th><th>\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC</th><th>\u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645\u200C\u0647\u0627</th><th>\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A</th><th>\u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A</th><th>\u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641</th><th>\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</th><th>\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u0646\u062F\u0647</th><th>\u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E</th><th>\u0648\u0636\u0639\u06CC\u062A</th><th></th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>
                  ${invoices.map(
    (invoice) => `<tr>
                        <td class="text-cream/30 text-[11px]">${toPersianDigits(invoice.id)}</td>
                        <td>
                          <a href="/admin/customers/${invoice.user_id}" class="text-[12.5px] hover:text-gold transition block">${escapeHtml(invoice.customer_name)}</a>
                          <span class="text-[10px] text-cream/30 font-mono" dir="ltr">${escapeHtml(invoice.customer_phone)}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="text-[11px] text-cream/45 max-w-[180px] truncate">${escapeHtml(invoiceItemNames(invoice.items))}</td>
                        <td class="font-bold text-amber-200 whitespace-nowrap text-[12.5px]">${formatNumberFa(invoice.amount)}</td>
                        <td class="whitespace-nowrap">${paymentBadge(invoice)}</td>
                        <td class="text-[11px] text-emerald-300/70">${invoice.discount > 0 ? formatNumberFa(invoice.discount) : "\u2014"}</td>
                        <td class="text-[11px] text-cream/60">+${toPersianDigits(invoice.points_earned)}</td>
                        <td class="text-[11px] text-cream/45">${escapeHtml(invoice.staff_name ?? "\u2014")}</td>
                        <td class="text-[10.5px] text-cream/40 whitespace-nowrap">${formatJalaliDateTime(invoice.created_at)}</td>
                        <td>${invoice.voided_at ? `<span class="badge tone-danger" title="${escapeHtml(`\u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644: ${invoice.void_reason ?? "\u0628\u062F\u0648\u0646 \u062F\u0644\u06CC\u0644"} \u2014 ${invoice.voided_at}`)}">\u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647</span>` : `<span class="badge tone-success">\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631</span>`}</td>
                        <td class="text-left">
                          <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                            <button type="button" data-detail-id="${invoice.id}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" title="\u062C\u0632\u0626\u06CC\u0627\u062A">${icon("eye", "w-3.5 h-3.5")}</button>
                            ${invoice.voided_at ? "" : `<button type="button" data-correct-id="${invoice.id}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" title="\u0627\u0635\u0644\u0627\u062D \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631">${icon("edit", "w-3.5 h-3.5")}</button>`}
                            ${invoice.voided_at ? "" : `<button type="button" data-void-id="${invoice.id}" data-void-amount="${invoice.amount}" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm whitespace-nowrap">${icon("close", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644</button>`}
                          </div>
                        </td>
                      </tr>`
  ).join("")}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3 border-t border-white/10">
              <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/40">\u0635\u0641\u062D\u0647 ${toPersianDigits(page)} \u0627\u0632 ${toPersianDigits(totalPages)}</div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <a href="${pageLink(Math.max(1, page - 1))}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm ${page <= 1 ? "opacity-40 pointer-events-none" : ""}">${icon("right", "w-3.5 h-3.5")} \u0642\u0628\u0644\u06CC</a>
                <a href="${pageLink(Math.min(totalPages, page + 1))}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm ${page >= totalPages ? "opacity-40 pointer-events-none" : ""}">\u0628\u0639\u062F\u06CC ${icon("left", "w-3.5 h-3.5")}</a>
              </div>
            </div>`}
    </div>
  </div>

      <textarea id="correct-reason" class="field min-h-[64px] text-[12px]" maxlength="160"

      <div class="flex gap-2 mt-4">

  <!-- \u067E\u0646\u062C\u0631\u0647\u0654 \u062C\u0632\u0626\u06CC\u0627\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 -->
  <div id="detail-modal" class="hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 overflow-y-auto">
    <div class="glass rounded-3xl max-w-lg mx-auto mt-10 p-5">
      <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-3">
        <h3 class="font-bold text-[15px]">\u062C\u0632\u0626\u06CC\u0627\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631</h3>
        <button type="button" id="detail-close" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">${icon("close", "w-4 h-4")}</button>
      </div>
      <div id="detail-body" class="text-[12.5px]"></div>
    </div>
  </div>
  `;
  const scripts = `
    initJalaliRange($('#from-day'), $('#from-month'), $('#from-year'), ${jsonForScript(filters.from ?? "")}, 8);
    initJalaliRange($('#to-day'), $('#to-month'), $('#to-year'), ${jsonForScript(filters.to ?? "")}, 8);

    $('#filter-form').addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
      var from = readJalaliRange($('#from-day'), $('#from-month'), $('#from-year'));
      var to = readJalaliRange($('#to-day'), $('#to-month'), $('#to-year'));

      if (from.partial || to.partial) {
        event.preventDefault();
        toast('\u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0628\u0627\u0632\u0647\u0654 \u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E\u060C \u0631\u0648\u0632 \u0648 \u0645\u0627\u0647 \u0648 \u0633\u0627\u0644 \u0631\u0627 \u06A9\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F \u06CC\u0627 \u0647\u0645\u0647 \u0631\u0627 \u062E\u0627\u0644\u06CC \u0628\u06AF\u0630\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062F.', 'error');
        return;
      }
      if (from.iso && to.iso && from.iso > to.iso) {
        event.preventDefault();
        toast('\u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E \u0634\u0631\u0648\u0639 \u0646\u0628\u0627\u06CC\u062F \u0628\u0639\u062F \u0627\u0632 \u062A\u0627\u0631\u06CC\u062E \u067E\u0627\u06CC\u0627\u0646 \u0628\u0627\u0634\u062F.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      $('#from-iso').value = from.iso || '';
      $('#to-iso').value = to.iso || '';
    });

    // --- \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644 \u0628\u0627 \u062F\u0644\u06CC\u0644 ---
    var voidModal = $('#void-modal');
    var voidId = null;
    var voidButton = null;

    function closeVoid() {
      voidModal.classList.add('hidden');
      voidId = null;
      voidButton = null;
      $('#void-reason').value = '';
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-void-id]').forEach(function (button) {
      button.addEventListener('click', function () {
        voidId = button.getAttribute('data-void-id');
        voidButton = button;
        $('#void-id-text').textContent = '#' + fa(voidId);
        $('#void-amount-text').textContent = moneyFa(Number(button.getAttribute('data-void-amount')) || 0);
        $('#void-reason').value = '';
        voidModal.classList.remove('hidden');
        $('#void-reason').focus();
      });
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-reason]').forEach(function (chip) {
      chip.addEventListener('click', function () {
        $('#void-reason').value = chip.getAttribute('data-reason');
      });
    });

    $('#void-cancel').addEventListener('click', closeVoid);
    $('#void-close').addEventListener('click', closeVoid);
    voidModal.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      if (event.target === voidModal) closeVoid();
    });

    $('#void-confirm').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      if (!voidId) return;
      var reason = $('#void-reason').value.trim();
      if (reason.length < 3) {
        toast('\u062F\u0644\u06CC\u0644 \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644 \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0646\u0648\u06CC\u0633\u06CC\u062F (\u062D\u062F\u0627\u0642\u0644 \u06F3 \u0646\u0648\u06CC\u0633\u0647).', 'error');
        $('#void-reason').focus();
        return;
      }

      var button = $('#void-confirm');
      setLoading(button, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644\u2026');

      async function send(force) {
        return api('/api/admin/invoices/' + voidId + '/void', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: force ? { reason: reason, allowClosedShift: true } : { reason: reason }
        });
      }

      try {
        var result;
        try {
          result = await send(false);
        } catch (firstError) {
          // \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0645\u062A\u0639\u0644\u0642 \u0628\u0647 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A: \u062A\u0623\u06CC\u06CC\u062F \u0635\u0631\u06CC\u062D \u0645\u062F\u06CC\u0631 \u0644\u0627\u0632\u0645 \u0627\u0633\u062A
          if (/\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647/.test(String(firstError.message || ''))) {
            setLoading(button, false);
            var ok = confirm(
              '\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0645\u0631\u0628\u0648\u0637 \u0628\u0647 \u06CC\u06A9 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0628\u0633\u062A\u0647\u200C\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A. \u0628\u0627 \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644\u060C \u06AF\u0632\u0627\u0631\u0634 \u0622\u0646 \u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0648 \u0646\u0642\u062F \u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0632\u0645\u062D\u0627\u0633\u0628\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u0634\u0648\u062F. \u0627\u062F\u0627\u0645\u0647 \u0645\u06CC\u200C\u062F\u0647\u06CC\u062F\u061F'
            );
            if (!ok) return;
            setLoading(button, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0628\u0637\u0627\u0644\u2026');
            result = await send(true);
          } else {
            throw firstError;
          }
        }
        toast('\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644 \u0634\u062F\u061B ' + fa(result.pointsReversed) + ' \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0628\u0631\u06AF\u0634\u062A \u062E\u0648\u0631\u062F.', 'success');
        setTimeout(function () { location.reload(); }, 700);
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
        setLoading(button, false);
      }
    });

    // --- \u0627\u0635\u0644\u0627\u062D \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 ---
    var correctModal = $('#correct-modal');
    var correctInvoice = null;

    function correctNumber(value) {
      var text = String(value == null ? '' : value)
        .replace(/[\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, function(d){ return String('\u06F0\u06F1\u06F2\u06F3\u06F4\u06F5\u06F6\u06F7\u06F8\u06F9'.indexOf(d)); })
        .replace(/[\u0660-\u0669]/g, function(d){ return String('\u0660\u0661\u0662\u0663\u0664\u0665\u0666\u0667\u0668\u0669'.indexOf(d)); })
        .replace(/[\\s,\u066C]/g, '');
      if (!text) return 0;
      if (!/^[0-9]+$/.test(text)) return NaN;
      var n = Number(text);
      return Number.isSafeInteger(n) ? n : NaN;
    }

    function moneyInput(el) {
      el.addEventListener('input', function () {
        var n = correctNumber(this.value);
        if (Number.isFinite(n)) this.value = this.value.trim() ? moneyFa(n) : '';
        recalcCorrect();
      });
    }

    function correctRows() {
      return Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#correct-rows [data-row]'));
    }

    function readCorrectItems() {
      return correctRows().map(function (row) {
        return {
          name: row.querySelector('[data-f="name"]').value.trim(),
          qty: correctNumber(row.querySelector('[data-f="qty"]').value),
          price: correctNumber(row.querySelector('[data-f="price"]').value)
        };
      });
    }

    function recalcCorrect() {
      var items = readCorrectItems();
      var subtotal = items.reduce(function (sum, item) { return sum + item.price * item.qty; }, 0);
      var discount = correctNumber($('#correct-discount').value);
      var total = Math.max(0, subtotal - discount);

      $('#correct-subtotal').textContent = Number.isFinite(subtotal) ? moneyFa(subtotal) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646' : '\u0639\u062F\u062F \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631';
      $('#correct-total').textContent = Number.isFinite(total) ? moneyFa(total) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646' : '\u0639\u062F\u062F \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631';

      var paid = ['#correct-cash', '#correct-card', '#correct-online'].reduce(function (sum, sel) {
        return sum + (correctNumber($(sel).value));
      }, 0);
      var gap = paid - total;
      var gapEl = $('#correct-gap');
      gapEl.textContent = Number.isFinite(gap) ? (gap > 0 ? '+' : '') + moneyFa(gap) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646' : '\u0639\u062F\u062F \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631';
      gapEl.className = 'font-bold ' + (gap === 0 ? 'text-emerald-300' : gap > 0 ? 'text-sky-300' : 'text-rose-300');
    }

    function addCorrectRow(item) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.setAttribute('data-row', '1');
      row.className = 'grid grid-cols-[1fr_64px_96px_28px] gap-1.5 items-center';
      row.innerHTML =
        '<input data-f="name" class="field !py-1.5 text-[11.5px]" placeholder="\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645" maxlength="60" value="' + escapeHtml(item.name || '') + '" />' +
        '<input data-f="qty" class="field !py-1.5 text-[11.5px] text-center" inputmode="numeric" dir="ltr" placeholder="\u06F1" value="' + escapeHtml(String(item.qty || 1)) + '" />' +
        '<input data-f="price" class="field !py-1.5 text-[11.5px] text-left" inputmode="numeric" dir="ltr" placeholder="\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u062A" value="' + (item.price ? moneyFa(item.price) : '') + '" />' +
        '<button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm !px-1.5" data-remove-row title="\u062D\u0630\u0641">' + '\u2715' + '</button>';

      row.querySelector('[data-remove-row]').addEventListener('click', function () {
        row.remove();
        recalcCorrect();
      });
      row.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(function (input) {
        input.addEventListener('input', recalcCorrect);
      });
      row.querySelector('[data-f="price"]').addEventListener('blur', function () {
        var n = correctNumber(this.value);
        if (Number.isFinite(n)) this.value = this.value.trim() ? moneyFa(n) : '';
        recalcCorrect();
      });
      $('#correct-rows').appendChild(row);
      recalcCorrect();
    }

    async function openCorrect(id) {
      setLoading($('#correct-confirm'), false);
      $('#correct-rows').innerHTML = '';
      $('#correct-discount').value = '';
      $('#correct-reason').value = '';
      $('#correct-error').classList.add('hidden');
      try {
        correctInvoice = await api('/api/admin/invoices/' + id);
      } catch (error) {
        toast(error.message, 'error');
        return;
      }

      $('#correct-title').textContent = '#' + fa(correctInvoice.id);
      var items = [];
      try { items = JSON.parse(correctInvoice.items || '[]'); } catch (e) { items = []; }
      if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) {
        items = [{ name: '\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u06CC', qty: 1, price: Number(correctInvoice.amount) + Number(correctInvoice.discount || 0) }];
      }
      items.forEach(function (item) {
        addCorrectRow({ name: item.name, qty: item.qty, price: item.price });
      });

      $('#correct-discount').value = correctInvoice.discount ? moneyFa(correctInvoice.discount) : '';
      $('#correct-cash').value = correctInvoice.paid_cash ? moneyFa(correctInvoice.paid_cash) : '';
      $('#correct-card').value = correctInvoice.paid_card ? moneyFa(correctInvoice.paid_card) : '';
      $('#correct-online').value = correctInvoice.paid_online ? moneyFa(correctInvoice.paid_online) : '';
      recalcCorrect();
      correctModal.classList.remove('hidden');
      $('#correct-reason').focus();
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-correct-id]').forEach(function (button) {
      button.addEventListener('click', function () { openCorrect(button.getAttribute('data-correct-id')); });
    });

    $('#correct-add-row').addEventListener('click', function () { addCorrectRow({ name: '', qty: 1, price: 0 }); });
    ['#correct-discount', '#correct-cash', '#correct-card', '#correct-online'].forEach(function(selector){ moneyInput($(selector)); });

    function closeCorrect() {
      correctModal.classList.add('hidden');
      correctInvoice = null;
    }
    $('#correct-cancel').addEventListener('click', closeCorrect);
    $('#correct-close').addEventListener('click', closeCorrect);
    correctModal.addEventListener('click', function (event) { if (event.target === correctModal) closeCorrect(); });

    $('#correct-confirm').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      if (!correctInvoice) return;
      var items = readCorrectItems();
      var reason = $('#correct-reason').value.trim();
      var errorEl = $('#correct-error');

      var fail = function (message) {
        errorEl.textContent = message;
        errorEl.classList.remove('hidden');
      };

      if (!items.length || items.length > 50 || items.some(function(item){ return !item.name || item.name.length > 60 || !Number.isSafeInteger(item.qty) || item.qty < 1 || item.qty > 99 || !Number.isSafeInteger(item.price) || item.price < 0 || item.price > 1000000000; })) return fail('\u0646\u0627\u0645\u060C \u062A\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F \u06CC\u0627 \u0642\u06CC\u0645\u062A \u0627\u0642\u0644\u0627\u0645 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u061B \u0631\u062F\u06CC\u0641 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0631\u0627 \u0627\u0635\u0644\u0627\u062D \u06CC\u0627 \u062D\u0630\u0641 \u06A9\u0646\u06CC\u062F.');
      if (reason.length < 3) return fail('\u062F\u0644\u06CC\u0644 \u0627\u0635\u0644\u0627\u062D \u0631\u0627 \u0628\u0646\u0648\u06CC\u0633\u06CC\u062F (\u062D\u062F\u0627\u0642\u0644 \u06F3 \u0646\u0648\u06CC\u0633\u0647).');

      var total = items.reduce(function (sum, item) { return sum + item.price * item.qty; }, 0) -
        (correctNumber($('#correct-discount').value));
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(total) || total <= 0) return fail('\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0646\u0647\u0627\u06CC\u06CC \u06CC\u0627 \u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641 \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.');

      var cash = correctNumber($('#correct-cash').value);
      var card = correctNumber($('#correct-card').value);
      var online = correctNumber($('#correct-online').value);
      if ([cash, card, online].some(function(n){ return !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 0; })) return fail('\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A\u06CC \u0646\u0627\u0645\u0639\u062A\u0628\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.');
      if (cash + card + online < total) return fail('\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A\u06CC \u06A9\u0645\u062A\u0631 \u0627\u0632 \u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0633\u062A.');

      errorEl.classList.add('hidden');
      var button = this;
      setLoading(button, true, '\u062F\u0631 \u062D\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0635\u0644\u0627\u062D\u2026');
      try {
        var result = await api('/api/admin/invoices/' + correctInvoice.id + '/correct', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: {
            items: items,
            discount: correctNumber($('#correct-discount').value),
            payment: { cash: cash, card: card, online: online },
            reason: reason
          }
        });
        toast('\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 #' + fa(result.replaces) + ' \u0627\u0635\u0644\u0627\u062D \u0634\u062F\u061B \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F: #' + fa(result.newInvoiceId), 'success');
        setTimeout(function () { location.reload(); }, 800);
      } catch (error) {
        setLoading(button, false);
        fail(error.message);
      }
    });

    // --- \u062C\u0632\u0626\u06CC\u0627\u062A \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 ---
    var detailModal = $('#detail-modal');

    function closeDetail() {
      detailModal.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-detail-id]').forEach(function (button) {
      button.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var id = button.getAttribute('data-detail-id');
        setLoading(button, true, '');
        try {
          var invoice = await api('/api/admin/invoices/' + id);
          $('#detail-body').innerHTML = renderInvoiceDetail(invoice);
          detailModal.classList.remove('hidden');
        } catch (error) {
          toast(error.message, 'error');
        } finally {
          setLoading(button, false);
        }
      });
    });

    $('#detail-close').addEventListener('click', closeDetail);
    detailModal.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      if (event.target === detailModal) closeDetail();
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        if (!voidModal.classList.contains('hidden')) closeVoid();
        if (!detailModal.classList.contains('hidden')) closeDetail();
        if (!correctModal.classList.contains('hidden')) closeCorrect();
      }
    });

    function renderInvoiceDetail(invoice) {
      var items = [];
      try { items = JSON.parse(invoice.items || '[]'); } catch (e) { items = []; }
      var rows = (items || []).map(function (item) {
        var line = (Number(item.price) || 0) * (Number(item.qty) || 0);
        return '<div class="flex items-center justify-between py-1.5 border-b border-white/5">' +
          '<span>' + escapeHtml(String(item.name || '\u2014')) + ' \xD7 ' + fa(item.qty || 0) + '</span>' +
          '<span class="font-bold text-amber-200">' + moneyFa(line) + '</span></div>';
      }).join('');

      var payRows = [];
      if (invoice.payment_method) {
        payRows.push(['\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A', { cash: '\u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC', card: '\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A\u200C\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646', online: '\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646', mixed: '\u062A\u0631\u06A9\u06CC\u0628\u06CC' }[invoice.payment_method] || invoice.payment_method]);
        if (invoice.paid_cash) payRows.push(['\u0646\u0642\u062F \u062F\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A\u06CC', moneyFa(invoice.paid_cash)]);
        if (invoice.paid_card) payRows.push(['\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A', moneyFa(invoice.paid_card)]);
        if (invoice.paid_online) payRows.push(['\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646', moneyFa(invoice.paid_online)]);
        if (invoice.change_amount) payRows.push(['\u0628\u0627\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647', moneyFa(invoice.change_amount)]);
      } else {
        payRows.push(['\u0631\u0648\u0634 \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A', '\u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 (\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0642\u062F\u06CC\u0645\u06CC)']);
      }
      payRows.push(['\u0634\u06CC\u0641\u062A \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642', invoice.shift_id ? '#' + fa(invoice.shift_id) : '\u2014']);

      return '' +
        '<div class="space-y-1.5 mb-3">' +
          '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0631\u0647 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631</span><span class="font-bold">#' + fa(invoice.id) + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">\u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC</span><span class="font-bold">' + escapeHtml(invoice.customer_name || '\u2014') + ' <span class="text-cream/35 font-mono text-[11px]" dir="ltr">' + escapeHtml(invoice.customer_phone || '') + '</span></span></div>' +
          '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">\u062B\u0628\u062A\u200C\u06A9\u0646\u0646\u062F\u0647</span><span>' + escapeHtml(invoice.staff_name || '\u2014') + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">\u0632\u0645\u0627\u0646 \u062B\u0628\u062A</span><span>' + formatJalaliDateTime(invoice.created_at) + '</span></div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="rounded-xl bg-black/25 border border-white/10 p-3 mb-3">' + (rows || '<p class="text-cream/40 text-[11px]">\u0622\u06CC\u062A\u0645\u06CC \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.</p>') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="space-y-1.5 mb-3">' +
          '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">\u062C\u0645\u0639 \u0627\u0642\u0644\u0627\u0645</span><span>' + moneyFa(invoice.subtotal || invoice.amount) + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">\u062A\u062E\u0641\u06CC\u0641</span><span class="text-emerald-300">' + moneyFa(invoice.discount || 0) + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/10 pt-1.5"><span class="text-cream/50">\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A \u0646\u0647\u0627\u06CC\u06CC</span><span class="font-bold text-amber-200">' + moneyFa(invoice.amount) + ' \u062A\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0646</span></div>' +
          '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632</span><span>+' + fa(invoice.points_earned || 0) + '</span></div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="rounded-xl bg-black/25 border border-white/10 p-3 space-y-1.5">' +
          payRows.map(function (row) {
            return '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><span class="text-cream/50">' + row[0] + '</span><span>' + row[1] + '</span></div>';
          }).join('') +
        '</div>' +
        (invoice.voided_at
          ? '<div class="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-400/25 p-3 text-[11.5px] text-rose-200">\u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u062F\u0631 ' + formatJalaliDateTime(invoice.voided_at) + ' \u0628\u0627\u0637\u0644 \u0634\u062F\u0647 \u0627\u0633\u062A.<br>\u062F\u0644\u06CC\u0644: ' + escapeHtml(invoice.void_reason || '\u2014') + '</div>'
          : '');
    }
  `;
  return appShell({
    title: "\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631\u0647\u0627",
    role: "admin",
    userName,
    active: "invoices",
    body,
    scripts,
    cafeName: settings.cafe_name
  });
}
function paymentBadge(invoice) {
  const method = invoice.payment_method;
  if (!method) return '<span class="badge tone-muted" title="\u0641\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0648\u0631 \u0642\u062F\u06CC\u0645\u06CC\u061B \u0631\u0648\u0634 \u067E\u0631\u062F\u0627\u062E\u062A \u062B\u0628\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647">\u2014</span>';
  const map = {
    cash: { label: "\u0646\u0642\u062F\u06CC", tone: "tone-success" },
    card: { label: "\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A", tone: "tone-info" },
    online: { label: "\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646", tone: "tone-warning" },
    mixed: { label: "\u062A\u0631\u06A9\u06CC\u0628\u06CC", tone: "tone-gold" }
  };
  const info = map[method] ?? { label: method, tone: "tone-muted" };
  const parts = [];
  if (invoice.paid_cash > 0) parts.push(`\u0646\u0642\u062F ${formatNumberFa(invoice.paid_cash)}`);
  if (invoice.paid_card > 0) parts.push(`\u06A9\u0627\u0631\u062A ${formatNumberFa(invoice.paid_card)}`);
  if (invoice.paid_online > 0) parts.push(`\u0622\u0646\u0644\u0627\u06CC\u0646 ${formatNumberFa(invoice.paid_online)}`);
  const tooltip = parts.length ? parts.join(" + ") + (invoice.change_amount > 0 ? ` | \u0628\u0627\u0642\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647 ${formatNumberFa(invoice.change_amount)}` : "") : info.label;
  return `<span class="badge ${info.tone}" title="${escapeHtml(tooltip)}">${info.label}${invoice.change_amount > 0 ? " \u21BA" : ""}</span>`;
}
function invoiceItemNames(raw2) {
  try {
    const items = JSON.parse(raw2 || "[]");
    if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) return "\u2014";
    return items.map((item) => `${item.name} \xD7${item.qty}`).join("\u060C ");
  } catch {
    return "\u2014";
  }
}
function customersCsv(rows) {
  const header = [
    "id",
    "name",
    "phone",
    "tier",
    "points",
    "visits",
    "total_spent",
    "last_visit",
    "birth_date",
    "referral_code",
    "created_at"
  ];
  const lines = [header.join(",")];
  for (const row of rows) {
    lines.push(
      [
        row.id,
        csvCell(String(row.name)),
        csvCell(row.phone),
        row.tier,
        row.points,
        row.visits,
        row.total_spent,
        row.last_visit_date ?? "",
        row.birth_date ?? "",
        csvCell(row.referral_code ?? ""),
        row.created_at
      ].join(",")
    );
  }
  return "\uFEFF" + lines.join("\r\n");
}
function csvCell(value) {
  let text = String(value ?? "");
  if (/^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(text)) text = `'${text}`;
  if (/[",\n\r]/.test(text)) text = `"${text.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
  return text;
}

function adminSettingsPage(input) {
  const { settings, userName, menu, smsToday } = input;
  const hasSmsKey = settings.sms_api_key.trim().length > 0;
  const hasWeatherKey = settings.weather_api_key.trim().length > 0;
  const smsProviderLabel = settings.sms_provider === "smsir" ? "sms.ir" : settings.sms_provider === "kavenegar" ? "\u06A9\u0627\u0648\u0647\u200C\u0646\u06AF\u0627\u0631" : "\u062D\u0627\u0644\u062A \u0622\u0632\u0645\u0627\u06CC\u0634\u06CC";
  const templateCards = TEMPLATE_META.map((meta) => {
    const value = settings.sms_templates[meta.key];
    return `<div class="glass rounded-2xl overflow-hidden" data-template-card="${meta.key}">
      <div class="px-4 py-3 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
        <span class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-gold/10 border border-gold/20 text-gold flex items-center justify-center">${icon(meta.iconName, "w-3.5 h-3.5")}</span>
        <div class="min-w-0">
          <div class="text-[12.5px] font-semibold">${meta.title}</div>
          <div class="text-[10px] text-cream/35">${meta.description}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="me-auto flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="badge tone-muted" data-count-for="${meta.key}">\u06F0 \u06A9\u0627\u0631\u0627\u06A9\u062A\u0631</span>
          <span class="badge tone-info" data-parts-for="${meta.key}">\u06F1 \u0628\u062E\u0634</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="p-4">
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mb-2.5">
          ${meta.vars.map(
      (variable) => `<button type="button" data-var="{${variable}}" data-target="tpl-${meta.key}" class="badge tone-muted hover:!text-gold hover:border-gold/40 transition font-mono !text-[10px]" dir="ltr">{${variable}}</button>`
    ).join("")}
        </div>
        <textarea id="tpl-${meta.key}" rows="4" maxlength="1000" class="field text-[12.5px] leading-8" spellcheck="false">${escapeHtml(value)}</textarea>
        <div class="mt-2 rounded-xl bg-black/25 border border-white/5 p-3">
          <div class="text-[9.5px] text-cream/30 mb-1">\u067E\u06CC\u0634\u200C\u0646\u0645\u0627\u06CC\u0634</div>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-cream/60 leading-6 whitespace-pre-line" data-preview-for="${meta.key}"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join("");
  const body = `
  <div class="p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
    ${pageHeader({
    title: "\u062A\u0646\u0638\u06CC\u0645\u0627\u062A \u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647",
    subtitle: "\u0645\u062A\u0646 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9\u200C\u0647\u0627\u060C \u0646\u0633\u0628\u062A \u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632\u062F\u0647\u06CC\u060C \u0645\u0646\u0648 \u0648 \u0627\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0644 \u0633\u0631\u0648\u06CC\u0633\u200C\u0647\u0627",
    iconName: "settings",
    actions: `<button type="button" id="save-settings" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">${icon("check", "w-4 h-4")} \u0630\u062E\u06CC\u0631\u0647 \u062A\u063A\u06CC\u06CC\u0631\u0627\u062A</button>`
  })}

    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-2 mb-5">
      ${[
    { href: "#general", label: "\u0627\u0637\u0644\u0627\u0639\u0627\u062A \u06A9\u0627\u0641\u0647", iconName: "coffee" },
    { href: "#points", label: "\u0627\u0645\u062A\u06CC\u0627\u0632 \u0648 \u0633\u0637\u062D", iconName: "star" },
    { href: "#rewards", label: "\u0628\u0627\u0634\u06AF\u0627\u0647 \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC", iconName: "gift" },
    { href: "#templates", label: "\u0642\u0627\u0644\u0628 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645\u06A9", iconName: "chat" },
    { href: "#automation", label: "\u062C\u0627\u0628\u200C\u0647\u0627\u06CC \u062E\u0648\u062F\u06A9\u0627\u0631", iconName: "refresh" },

              <input id="weather_api_key" type="password" dir="ltr" class="field text-left font-mono" value="" autocomplete="off"
